It still begins `  Files and Folders:  `, still comes only under `  Its drop folder, on this Mac:  `, after the `Warning:` line when both are said, and is now also said for iCloud Drive and every folder in `~/Library/CloudStorage`, Dropbox's included.
- Changed, a function the app calls: settings.js `needsFilesAccess(path, home)` keeps its signature, but is now also true for a folder in iCloud Drive and for every folder in `~/Library/CloudStorage`, Dropbox's included (the app's design picks its privacy lines by it and says Dropbox brings none). `cloudFolder` does not change.
- Unchanged, word for word: both `Make available offline` lines (when they come is unchanged); every `Not written: the drop folder …` refusal of today, and `Not written: the drop folder would change from …`; `Not written: the folder that is to hold the drop folder is not there.`; `The folder <folder> is watched by <Mac> already. A drop folder is watched by one Mac only.`; `A folder is for transcripts or for unedited video, and not for both: …`; `Not written: the drop folder cannot be the exports folder, or inside it, or hold it.`; and D8's read-back lines (`  Its drop folder, on this Mac:  <path>`, `  Exports from HyperFrames, looked for in:  <path>`, `show`'s `  Its drop folder:  <path>`). Exit codes are as today.
- Which line is said, changed in these cases (each ends 2):
  - `BRIMM VIDEO Transfer` beside BRIMM Video, or Zoom Video Transfer in it, picked as the drop folder while the transcript tool's file lists it in that very spelling, was refused by `A folder is for transcripts or for unedited video, …` (no "Nothing was changed." after it); it is now refused first by `Not written: the drop folder cannot be BRIMM VIDEO Transfer, or inside it, or hold it.`, then a blank line and `Nothing was changed.`. The same for `BRIMM VIDEO MacBook`, which was taken unless the file listed it.
  - A folder the transcript tool's agent lists, picked as the drop folder, and named through a link, with a slash at the end or in other letter case, or listed only by the agent's file turned off (`.plist.off`), was taken (0); it now gets the transcript line.
  - A pick with a `.` or `..` part (`--drop-folder`, or `--exports-folder`), which at the base was taken (0), or got the transcript line or the marker line only when its spelling matched, now gets `Not written: the drop folder must be named by a whole path with no . or .. in it.` (or `… the exports folder must be …`), before either of install.sh's lines. A pick through a folder not there and `..` into another Mac's marked folder, which at the base ended 0, is one of these.
  - A picked folder holding a file still arriving (a browser's `.download`, `.crdownload`, `.part` or `.tmp`) of a video, and no other video, was taken (0); it now gets the holds-a-video line.
  - A refusal of an exports folder the run did not name (any new `Not written: the exports folder …` line, not the nested one) is now two lines before the blank line: the refusal, then `The exports folder is <path>, kept from before: …` or `… as none is set: …`.
  - A picked folder another Mac has marked, or that the transcript tool's agent lists (on or turned off), still gets install.sh's own line, the marker line or the transcript line, whatever it holds: settings.js neither counts nor reads such a folder. It gets another line only when settings.js refuses first: the folder by its place (BRIMM Video's folders, `BRIMM VIDEO Transfer` or `BRIMM VIDEO MacBook`, Work, her chat's, Zoom's, Downloads), or the exports folder (next case). One another Mac has marked that cannot be read, which at the base ended 0 at `--print` (install.sh cannot see the marker either), now gets `Not written: the drop folder could not be read (…`.
  - Precedence: settings.js says its lines before install.sh says either of its two. So an exports folder refused (nested with the drop folder, as at the base, or any new `Not written: the exports folder …` line) is said in place of the marker line or the transcript line when both apply; and settings.js's drop folder refusals (by place, holds a video, could not be read) and its exports refusals come before `Not written: the drop folder would change from …`: a picked folder that holds a video while the old folder holds work now gets the holds-a-video line where the app saw "would change from", and so asks the drop question again where it used to stop the run.
- New, for the app to read (each followed by a blank line and `Nothing was changed.`, exit 2): the drop folder picker's `Not written: the drop folder holds a video already, …`, `… holds <n> videos already, …`, `… could not be read (<code>), so what it holds is not known. Make it readable (…), then run this again.`, `… cannot be BRIMM VIDEO Transfer, …`, `… cannot be BRIMM VIDEO MacBook, …` and `… must be named by a whole path with no . or .. in it.`, which begin as the drop folder's refusals do (`… could not be read (EPERM) …` is what a picked folder gives when macOS does not let Terminal, or the app the installer runs in, read it, and every later run gives it again until that is allowed); the exports picker's `Not written: the exports folder …` lines, of which the two for a marker carry a path, the folder that holds the marker by its real path (`… and <Mac> has marked <folder> as its own (…)` and `… and <Mac> has marked <folder>, which holds it, as its own (…)`: new in L1c, so these words cost nothing to the app yet); and, on a run that named no exports folder, the line after an exports refusal, `The exports folder is <path>, kept from before: name another with --exports-folder.` or `The exports folder is <path>, as none is set: name another with --exports-folder.` (it does not begin `Not written:`; the app may read the path from it, after `The exports folder is ` and before the last `, kept from before:` or `, as none is set:`).
- What the check looks in, for the app's privacy note (no line changes): settings.js, which `--print` and the real run both ask, now reads a picked drop folder (the count; never one this Mac watches now, another Mac's marked one or one the transcript tool lists), and looks for a marker in the exports folder and each folder above it; but never inside the Downloads folder in the home folder, as the app's design promises ("Downloads is never looked in"), whether Downloads is the exports folder by default or a folder in it is picked (such a pick is still made real by its path, as at the base, by `sameOrNested`). So, as at the base, a run with Downloads as the exports folder brings no question from macOS about Downloads; a pick under Desktop or Documents, in a cloud folder or on another disk, as the drop folder or the exports folder, may bring one to the app the installer runs in (Terminal) at `--print`, and a Don't Allow there gives `… could not be read (EPERM) …` for a drop folder, and for an exports folder no line (a marker it cannot see is no sign).

**Contract touched:** the guide's words, in step with brimm-operations: the overview (`overviewLines`) gains one line, right under `Your drop folder: <words>`, when this Mac does not let her chat look in that folder (macOS's refusal, EPERM; a folder shut by its own permissions gets no line, as at the base), on any Mac (one with a drop folder of its own, or one whose drop folder is in Dropbox): `This Mac does not let BRIMM Video Studio look in your drop folder, <words>. Allow it in System Settings, under Privacy & Security, Files and Folders, then ask again.`; and with that line said and no video under way, `Nothing is under way. To start, put the video in your drop folder.` is left out; and a video in a Mac's own drop folder (`here`) that this Mac's import has not seen five minutes after it came (the later of its ctime and its birth time), while none of this Mac's imports is at work, has a new stage, `unseen`, and step, `This Mac's import has not seen it yet: macOS may not let the import look in your drop folder. Ask Jason.`, in place of `It is coming into your drop folder. Wait for the "Ready" notice.` (`guide wait` and `guide tell` do not wait at it). No line of the BRIMM Ops page's guide becomes wrong; its "Into Studio" part may add what to do when Studio says either. `episodes`' line after the videos waiting is not the guide's. The hand-off files and their fixtures, `madeBy`, `tool/shared/`, lib.js, run.js and guide-note.js do not change.

**Size:** L (the second draft was M-L; the third added the "unseen" step, the `.`/`..` refusal, the count of files still arriving and the exports line that names its folder; this round moves it onto dfa9966 and adds little: the walk that passes over Downloads and its guard, the marker's folder named, the "If it does" warning, EPERM alone for the guide's line, and the later of two times for "unseen").

**The coordinator's rulings on the design (7 Nov 2026), binding over the section above:**
- The `unseen` stage and its words are not L1c's: the node-access notice session builds that notice after Phase 3 merges. Leave out `unseen`, UNSEEN_MS and every line, test and skill sentence of it.
- L1c is built on the branch with L1d, L2c, L3 and L8 in: L1d took `holding` out of `episodes`, and L3 changed how statuses are read (`ctx.statuses`, `readStatusesIn`). Place `episodes`' new line after the waiting block and before "Imports:", as the section says, by what the code is now.
- `retry` (edit.js, its "It is empty." for this Mac's drop folder) says the drop folder cannot be read when macOS refuses it (EPERM or EACCES), in `dropUnreadable`'s words, never "It is empty.".
- `exportsFolderFault` also refuses `~/BRIMM Video Work` and every folder in it (an episode's `video/` among them), and `~/Library/Application Support/BRIMM Video`, with the section's `Not written: the exports folder cannot be <what>, or inside it.` (`<what>`: `BRIMM Video Work`, `Studio's settings folder`). BRIMM Video itself and its Assets stay allowed.
- install.sh's drop-folder transcript refusal reads only the `.plist`, as the section has it. No app is named in any "cannot be read" sentence.
- A drop folder that is not there at all (ENOENT) is passed over as today.

### L2. Layout 6, `where.js`, and the other Mac

**Goal:** A record can say its files are on one Mac; `where.js` says where each file and folder is; a plan marked local is still a plan; another Mac lists the episode and changes nothing.

**Evidence:**
- `episode.js`: `RECORD_VERSION = 4` (38), `PLANNED_VERSION = 5` (41), `NEWEST_VERSION` (42); `layoutOf` (316-323); `faultFor` (429-470), which accepts a field it does not know; `fileProblems` (687-701) and `filesProblems` (705-712), which call any listed file not in the folder "missing".
- `isPlannedRecord` (episode.js 252) requires `version === 5`, so a plan written at layout 6 would be no plan: `gated` (gate.js 87) would take it for a video waiting for its answer, the join would stop (join.js 380), and the plans lists and footage offers would leave it out (footage.js 52, 64, 120, 466, 630; plan.js 347, 487; edit.js 736 and 757 in `findEpisode`, 963 in `episodeLines`, 1345 in `app`, 1401 in `appReel`, 2679 in `keptOverWhy`, 5923 in `guideFacts`).
- `findEpisode` (edit.js 726-775) and its gate (`gateOf`, 456-473) are where every command that names an episode finds it; COMMAND_NAMES (6493) is what gate.test.js walks.
- The guide's steps for a video or a session open elsewhere: `videoNext` (guide.js 424, the step at 428) and `sessionNext` (317, the step at 327).

**Files:** `tool/episode.js`; create `tool/where.js` and `tool/tests/where.test.js`; `tool/edit.js` (`findEpisode`'s gate, `episodes`' lines, `guideFacts`); `tool/guide.js`; tests `episode.test.js`, a new `tool/tests/local-episode.test.js` (a world with a layout-6 record, used by later tasks too), `gate.test.js`, `guide.test.js`, `footage*.test.js`, `join.test.js`.

**Interfaces.** Produces:
- `episode.js` `LOCAL_VERSION = 6`, `NEWEST_VERSION = 6`; `layoutOf` gives 6 for a record with `local`; `faultFor` takes `local` as undefined, null, or `{ on: <non-empty string>, original?: { file, bytes, mtimeMs } }` at layout 6 or above.
- `isPlannedRecord` accepts layout 5, or layout 6 when the record has `local`.
- `fileProblems(folder, record, { stat, machine, localFolder })`: a local file is looked at in `localFolder` (the episode's `video/`) on `local.on`, and not at all on another Mac; `filesProblems` takes the same options.
- `where.js`:
  - `filePlace(record, rel) -> "dropbox" | "local"`: `final/`, `history/`, `Original files/`, `record/` and `PLAN_FILE_NAMES` (plan.js 247) are "dropbox"; every other name is "local" when `record.local`, else "dropbox".
  - `folderAt(ctx, { folder, record }, place) -> path`: `folder` for "dropbox"; for "local", `episodeFilesFolder(ctx.home, basename(folder))` on a local record and `folder` on any other.
  - `fileAt(ctx, { folder, record }, rel) -> path`: `join(folderAt(ctx, { folder, record }, filePlace(record, rel)), rel)` (L6 adds the recording's branch). A part read through `partsOf` (footage.js 128) has its `folder` and `record`, and needs nothing more.
  - `onOtherMac(ctx, record) -> string`: `record.local.on` when it is not `ctx.machine`, else "".
  - `ON_OTHER_MAC(name, mac) -> string`: the line below.
- The other Mac's refusal is in `findEpisode`'s gate, so every command that names an episode says `ON_OTHER_MAC` and writes nothing on another Mac; `guide`, `episodes` and `status` list it instead.

**Failing tests first:**
- `episode.test.js`: a record with `local` is written at layout 6 and read back; `readEpisode(folder, { known: 5 })` reads it as newer and `writeEpisode` refuses it at `known: 5`; `editing` and `released: null` are in it; `local: {}` and `local` at layout 5 are faults; a record without `local` is still layout 4 or 5.
- `episode.test.js`, `gate.test.js`: a plan at layout 6 with `local` is a plan: `isPlannedRecord` is true, the gate lets `plan`, `name`, `join`, `app` and `resolve` through, `plan "<title>"` shows it, the footage offers list it, and `plan "<title>" join` takes it.
- `where.test.js`: `filePlace` for `IMG.mov`, `IMG.words.json`, `IMG.rough-cut.srt`, `history/2026-11-06T10-00-00 IMG.cut-plan.json`, `final/IMG.mp4`, `Original files/IMG.mov`, `teleprompter.txt` and `record/0002.json`, with and without `local`; `folderAt` and `fileAt` for each.
- On another Mac (`ctx.machine` "Jason's MacBook", whose settings say `drop: "Drop"`, and `local.on` "Mac Studio"): `episodes` lists it as on the Mac Studio, with no file called missing; walking COMMAND_NAMES as gate.test.js does, every command that names the episode says `ON_OTHER_MAC` and writes nothing; no card is offered for its video while it waits for her answer; the guide's step is `It is on the Mac Studio. Carry on there.`, for a video and for a session.

**Steps:** tests; fail; `episode.js`; `where.js`; the gate's refusal; `guideFacts` and the guide; whole suite; commit.

**Words Sallie sees:**
- `"<name>" is on the Mac Studio: carry on there. Nothing was changed.` (a session or a video not yet answered by its label, as every line names it).
- The guide: `It is on the Mac Studio. Carry on there.`

**Contract touched:** the guide's words, in step with brimm-operations: for a local video or session on another Mac, `It is on <Mac>. Carry on there.` in place of `It is open on <Mac>. Carry on there.` (`videoNext` and `sessionNext`; Phase 2 took the take-over out of `videoNext`'s words). **Size:** M.

### L2c. Every reader through `fileAt` and `folderAt`

**Goal:** Every reader of an episode's own files finds them where they are, one file at a time or a whole folder; `history/` follows the file it holds.

**Evidence:**
- Find the readers with `grep -nE 'join\([A-Za-z.]*folder' tool/*.js` (it finds `found.folder`, `folder` and `part.folder`). Among them: app.js `changedSince` (376), `adoptProject` (411), `makeProject` (447-478), `putVideoBack` (540); app-reel.js 167, 219, 234, 256, 288; edit.js `notHere` (1112), `open` (1230-1262, the notes at 1250), `notMade` (1892-1898), `suggestionOpening` (1951-1960), `finishEpisode` (2001-2150: `filesProblems` at 2043, `suggestionOpening` at 2097 and 2128), `show` (2263-2274), `resolveIn` (2709-2880), `reels` (3160-3206), `planFootage` (3411), the work copy's run (`workcopy`, 3452, which reads the video at 3503), `saveSpellings` (3878-3954), `answerKind` (4717-4879), `earlierFiles` and `setAsideWritten` (5034-5184), `whichCommand` (5409), `broughtOver` (5473-5480); free.js 321; join.js `wordsOfPart` (179: a missing words file gives `[]`, and the part then has no place, with no error), 448, 501, 533 (the concat list); late-wait.js `localSource` (39-53); plan-project.js 88; finish.js 776; suggestions-in-main.js 81; unedited.js `versionIn` (427); reading.js `renderRough` (714-733, which reads the recording at 715: Phase 2 kept the rough cut's render, into `renders/`); video-clock.js `videoClock` (19-54: the words file, the cut plan and `reels.json`), which `graphic … ideas` and `reels` read through `clockRead` (edit.js 3574-3582). Phase 2 took away three readers this list named: `stillArriving` (with `release`, F5), `markReels` (with the Final Cut commands, F7) and timeline.js's `clockFor` (with the timelines, F2 and F7; `videoClock` reads in its place).
- `history/` is read as a whole folder: by the hand-off's readers, finish.js `handoffsBeside` (178-181), `handedOff` (187-196), `lastRevision` (205) and free.js `handedOffForSure` (245-254, at 247); by the readers of local kinds, finish.js `teachingsIn` (213), `reelsListsIn` (218), which with the hand-offs feed `highestNumberOf` (491, where a number is never given twice), reading.js `rulesPlanKept` (217-237) and edit.js `readBefore` (4965-4973). Writers: finish.js 1600-1611 (`finishSession`: a session's hand-off set aside from `final/`), reading.js 504 and 669 (a reading's own set-asides, put in with its files, which `intoEpisode` lists: unedited.js 1185, 1205), edit.js `resolveIn` (2796-2799, 2880) and `setAsideWritten` (5107-5184).

**Ruling for L2c: `history/` follows the file.** A Dropbox file set aside (from `final/`, a plan file, a record) goes into `history/` in Dropbox and is listed as today. A local record's set-asides stay on the Mac, in `video/history/`, and are never listed, whoever sets them aside (`putHere`'s own renames, or a reading's `history/…` files, L4): nothing checks them, and `folderAt` finds them. So every `history/…` a local record lists is in Dropbox; the hand-off's readers read Dropbox's `history/` as today, and the readers of local kinds (`teachingsIn`, `reelsListsIn`, `rulesPlanKept`, `readBefore`) read `folderAt(…, "local")` and its `history/`.

**Files:** every reader and writer above; tests `local-episode.test.js`, `finish*.test.js`, `reading.test.js`, `join.test.js`.

**Interfaces.** Consumes L2's `filePlace`, `folderAt`, `fileAt`. Produces no new name: every reader above goes through them, and every caller of `fileProblems` gives `localFolder: episodeFilesFolder(ctx.home, <folder name>)` for a local record.

**Failing tests first:**
- `local-episode.test.js`, on the Mac that holds it: `episodes` lists it with no file line; `app`, `show … transcript`, `which`, `reels`, `free`, `graphic … ideas` and `kind --again` find their files in `video/`; `open` and `app` say no "Make available offline" line.
- `join.test.js`: `plan "<title>" join` of a local plan finds each part's words in its own `video/` (no part "with no place").
- `finish*.test.js`: a local session's next hand-off numbers its items after those in its teachings in `video/` and `video/history/`; a hand-off set aside from `final/` lands in Dropbox's `history/`, listed, and counts for the next revision.
- `reading.test.js`: `rulesPlanKept` of a local raw episode finds the rules' plan in `video/history/`.

**Steps:** tests; fail; the readers and writers; whole suite; commit.

**Words Sallie sees:** none new. **Contract touched:** none. **Size:** M-L.

Phase 2 merged first and took all of it away, so no builder is given L2b; its ID is kept, and L8 now follows L2c. What it named, and where each went:

### L8. Episodes from before the switch keep the old way

**Goal:** On a Mac switched to its picked drop folder, an episode filed before the switch works as today until it is handed off (D9), and no old code is deleted. Built right after L2c (L2b, which came between them, is dropped): its tests are the net every later task keeps green.

**Evidence:** Jason's answer to Question 2, "Yes, some do". What such an episode still reads of this Mac after the switch: no pending (P3-1, and `heldInOld` refuses the switch while one is owed), no status of this Mac's import in `Episodes/.importing` (likewise, and P3-1 counts them), and its files in its folder in Dropbox. `guide.js` `opsText` (146-150) appends `ONLINE_ONLY` (101) after the page's "taken" step of a raw video; the finish's online-only line is on its old path (finish.js 1135, in `finishNow`).

**Files:** create `tool/tests/old-episodes-after-switch.test.js` (a world whose Mac has `dropPath` and `exportsPath`, holding a layout-4 raw episode, a layout-4 finished video, and a layout-5 plan with one part in Dropbox); `tool/guide.js` (`opsText`: `ONLINE_ONLY` only for a record without `local`).

**Interfaces.** Consumes L1's settings and L2's `record.local`. Produces nothing new: every later task branches on `record.local` and leaves this world's results as they are.

**Failing tests first** (most pass at once; each later task keeps them passing):
- `episodes` lists the old raw episode with its files checked in its Dropbox folder, and no "on <Mac>" line.
- `kind "<id>" raw --private no --again` reads it the old way: the reading's files go into its Dropbox folder, and its read run waits for its own late check (`LATE_CHECK_MS`).
- `app "<id>"` makes its project from the recording in its Dropbox folder.
- `finish "<id>" --yes` writes its hand-off, and its run waits for its check as today; nothing is written under `pending/` but what today's finish writes.
- `spell "<id>" add …` while that check runs is refused in today's words.
- After its hand-off: nothing in `pending/original/`, and no `Original files/` in its folder.
- The guide's step after the page's "taken" step of the old raw episode ends with the "Make online-only" sentence; a local raw episode's does not.
- The old plan: `plan "<title>" join` joins the old way (the joined video filed into the plan's Dropbox folder, with its pending); a part dropped after the switch joins from its own `video/`.

**Steps:** tests; the `opsText` fork; whole suite; commit.

**Words Sallie sees:** none new. **Contract touched:** the guide's words, in step with brimm-operations: the "taken" step of a local raw video no longer ends with the "Make online-only" sentence; an old one's still does. **Size:** M.

### L3. The import files a video on this Mac

**Goal:** On a local Mac, the import makes its files from the dropped video in place, as today, then files the episode on this Mac: the video copied into `video/`, its files beside it, and only the first record in Dropbox; the original deleted from the drop folder once the record is written. No pending and no wait before Ready (L5 adds the first record's check, behind). It never takes or empties another episode's folder.

**Evidence:**
- The import works on the drop file in place (`unedited.js` `workOn`, 484; the arrival wait 520-); Dropbox comes in only at filing (`filing.js` `fileEpisode` 121-184: `put` 140-147 clones the video and each file, the record at 174) and at the late check (`keepLateCheck`, unedited.js 568-577; `pendingFor` 193-197; `writePending` 367-372; `finishLate` 489-542, which deletes the drop file at 506). The first record is one of the late check's entries today (filing.js 149, 174).
- `findFolder` (filing.js 64-79) names a folder from Episodes alone (`episodeFolderName`, names.js 23-29: `<day> <stem> (<Mac>)`, then `-2`), swallows any error listing Episodes (67-71), and reuses a folder with no whole record and no video (74-76), which every local episode's Dropbox folder is once its first record is lost. Stems repeat ("Movie Recording.mov").
- Status files: `importing.js` `writeStatus` (102), `readStatuses` (123), `statusesOf` (150), `removeStatus` (165), each deriving `Episodes/.importing` from `root` (61-67) and naming the file after the video (39), which may be a client's name. Their calls: filing.js 212 and 244 (`statusReporter`), 266 (`heldBy`), 348 and 352 (`clearCrashes`), 476 (`waitingStatus`), 511 and 537 (`finishLate`), 588 (`hold`), 602 and 607 (`releaseHeld`), 635, 705 and 767 (`importEpisodes`); edit.js 796 (`importLines`), 1013 (`episodes`), 1627 and 1635 (`lateCheckOwed`), 1683 and 1723 (`retry`), 6038 (`guideDrops`); late-wait.js 65, 80; unedited.js 460 (`labelsGiven`), 1251 (`importHold`); switch.js 79-81 (and the words at 80, 132 and 145 that name the folder).
- Until L3, `importing.js` and `switch.js` read and write `Episodes/.importing` alone (`importingFolder(root)`), which install.sh no longer makes on a local Mac (L1): a local Mac's status writes would fail there. L1 made `importingFolderOf` and `statusFoldersOf` (settings.js 86 and 88-92), and nothing calls them yet; it left `heldInOld` (switch.js 66-186, called from settings.js `planned` at 583) reading `Episodes/.importing` alone (L1's deferred minor: "heldInOld must read statusFoldersOf"). No Mac is switched to a drop folder of its own before Phase 3 merges, so nothing between L1 and L3 guards it.
- Where another Mac's import matters: `importHold` (unedited.js 1247-1268), `lateCheckAt` and `ownCheckOnly` (late-wait.js 61-67, 77-81), `importLines` (edit.js 795-805), `heldBy` (filing.js 261-277). A MacBook on a Dropbox drop folder writes its statuses in `Episodes/.importing` (move.zsh 197 passes `--drop Drop`).
- `episodeHolding` (filing.js 334-339) says a held video stays "until Dropbox's check of its copies passes".

**Files:** `tool/filing.js` (`findFolder`, `fileEpisode`, `heldBy`, `clearCrashes`, `importEpisodes`), `tool/unedited.js` (`workOn`'s filing, `keepLateCheck`, `importHold`), `tool/importing.js`, `tool/late-wait.js`, `tool/switch.js` (`heldInOld`), `tool/settings.js` (`planned`'s call of `heldInOld`), every status call above; tests `filing.test.js`, `unedited.test.js` (the import's describes: every import is light since Phase 2), `importing.test.js`, `edit.test.js` (its describe "node edit.js bring, status and retry"), `settings.test.js` (where `heldInOld` is tested, through `write` and `plan`), `late-wait.test.js`, the lock's place tests.

**Interfaces.** Consumes L1's `episodeFilesFolder`, `importingFolderOf`, `statusFoldersOf`, `isLocal`, and L2's `LOCAL_VERSION`. Produces:
- `importing.js`, renamed so a call left on an old name fails: `writeStatusIn(folder, status, writeText)`, `readStatusesIn(folders, now)` (each entry with its `folder`), `statusesIn(folders, video, machines, now)`, `removeStatusIn(folder, video, machine, id)`; each throws for a folder whose name is not `importing` or `.importing`. Writes go to `importingFolderOf(settings, home)`; reads go to `statusFoldersOf(settings, home)`. edit.js keeps `ctx.statuses` (where it writes) and `ctx.statusFolders` (where it reads).
- `filing.js` `heldBy({ root, statusFolders, video, bytes, now, gatedAs })`; `clearCrashes({ statusFolders, machine, now, alive, pid })` removes this Mac's stopped statuses in whichever folder each is; `switch.js` `heldInOld({ …, statusFolders })`, which settings.js `planned` gives `statusFoldersOf(held, home)`: the folders of the settings the switch is from.
- `filing.js` `findFolder(episodes, { video, bytes, machine, day, work, importId, dropId })`: listing Episodes throws on any error but ENOENT; a name is free only when neither `Episodes/<name>` nor `<work>/<name>/video` is there, unless that `video/.import.json` names this import (`importId` and `dropId` both); a re-run of a stopped import finds its folder by that marker, whatever day it was named on.
- `fileEpisode(…, { onRecordCopy })` on a local Mac: the record's `writeText` writes the record whole into the import's run folder (kept), calls `onRecordCopy({ from, to, bytes })`, and then clones it in by the rule; it returns `{ name, path, record, entries }`, `entries` holding that entry alone. L3 passes no hook; L5 passes the one that writes the `record` pending.

**The order of filing on a local Mac:** before its work, the import lists Episodes: an error other than ENOENT leaves the video for the next look, as a video still arriving is (no status, one log line, the drop file left); at the filing step, list Episodes again (an error then fails the import, as today, and she says "try it again"); find the folder (above); make `video/` and write `video/.import.json` (`{ import, drop }`) before anything else; write each made file there whole (a name of its own, then renamed), keeping a list of what this run wrote; check the drop file is unchanged (`sameFile`); copy it into `video/` under a name of its own (a clone on one disk, a whole copy otherwise), check its size, and rename it into place; write `0001.json` by the rule, its work copy kept, with `local: { on: machine }`; then delete the drop file, if it is unchanged. If anything fails before the record, only the files this run wrote are removed, then the marker, then `video/` if it is empty; the drop file stays; the import ends as one that failed, as today. A drop file that cannot be deleted stays, held by its episode, and the log says so.

**Failing tests first:**
- `unedited.test.js` / `filing.test.js`, an import on a local Mac:
  - the recording and its `.srt`, `.transcript.txt`, `.words.json`, `.cut-plan.txt`, `.cut-plan.json`, `.rough-cut.srt` and `.rough-cut.transcript.txt` are in `video/`, at the sizes the record lists;
  - the Ready banner is posted, and the drop file deleted, before any `wait`; no import pending is written (`pending/*.json`; L5 adds the first record's, in `pending/record/`);
  - `Episodes/<folder>/` in Dropbox holds `record/0001.json` and nothing else; the record is layout 6, with `local.on` this Mac, `editing` and `released: null`;
  - its status files are in the local importing folder (`importingFolderOf(settings, home)`) while it runs, with no `Episodes/.importing` there, as install.sh leaves a local Mac, and gone after; nothing is written in `Episodes/.importing`.
- "a record that cannot be written leaves the video in the drop folder": the record write fails; the video is in the drop folder at its size; nothing this run wrote is left in `video/`; the status says it failed; the next run imports it.
- "two drops of one stem on one day, Episodes unreadable for the second": the second import does no work and writes no status, its drop file stays and is looked at again, and the first episode's `video/` is untouched; with Episodes unreadable only at the filing step, it fails with its status, and the first `video/` is untouched.
- "a first record lost, then a drop of the same stem": the new import takes a new name, and the first episode's `video/` is untouched.
- "a run stopped after the copy and before the record": the video is still in the drop folder, and the next run finds its folder by the marker, on the same day and on a later one.
- "a run stopped after the record and before the delete": the video stays in the drop folder, held by its episode, in the words `already imported as <label> (<short id>). It stays in the drop folder for you to delete.`
- "a drop folder in Dropbox": the video is copied out, never renamed, and deleted only after the record is written; a placeholder there is read through first, as today.
- "a Zoom download is held as today", with its status in the local folder.
- "a video dropped again under the same name and size is held by its episode", as today.
- "another Mac's import still holds a reading here": a status of "Jason's MacBook" in `Episodes/.importing`, at work on a video, holds `importHold` for that video on the local Mac Studio, as today.
- "a status call on any other folder throws".
- `retry` on a local Mac finds a failed import by its local status (`edit.test.js`); `heldInOld` on a switch from a picked folder reads the local status folder (`statusFoldersOf`): in `settings.test.js`, `write --drop Drop --switch-back` on a Mac whose local status folder holds a failed import of a video in its picked folder is refused in `heldInOld`'s words and writes nothing.

**Steps:** tests; fail; `importing.js` and its calls (`heldInOld` and its call in `planned` among them); `findFolder`; `fileEpisode`, `unedited.js`; whole suite; commit.

**Words Sallie sees:** the held line above. The banner stays `Ready: <label>. In Claude, say "what's waiting?"`, and comes when the import's own work ends, not 15 minutes later.

**Contract touched:** none. **Size:** L.

### L4. Readings, copies, joins and footage on a local episode

**Goal:** On a local episode, Claude's reading starts at once and puts its files into `video/`; the copy for HyperFrames starts at once from `video/`; a join reads each part from its own place and files the joined video into the plan's `video/`; nothing writes a pending or waits for a check.

**Evidence:**
- reading.js: the hold loop (306-331, `READ_LOOK_MS` 52, `READ_HOLD_MS` 53); `written` (459-630) puts its files through `keepCheckInStep` (609) and `intoEpisode` (unedited.js 883-1217: it takes the Dropbox folder from the video's path at 886, `ownLateCheck` 1002, `replaceIn` 1182, the record 1205, the wait 1214); `writtenSession` (644-708) puts a session's teachings files in the same way (698). A raw reading's branch (495-538) renders the rough cut into `renders/` (`renderRough`, 714-733) and writes no Final Cut file (Phase 2's F3).
- edit.js: `waitForImport` (3364-3398); `answerKind` (4693-4888; `readingWaitsUntil` at 4872); `kind --again`'s set-aside (`setAsideWritten`, 5107-5184).
- join.js: parts read at 448 and 501 (`hereWhy` reads through); a single part filed with `fileEpisode(… into …)` (496-513), then `pendingFor`/`writePending` (518-520) and "copied into Dropbox … in 15 minutes" (521-523); a HeyGen voice taken in through `importInto` (425); `afterJoin` (585-606) kickstarts the agent; `opensAt` (571-577).
- footage.js: "A plan's record is never written for a part" (5, 123); `makePart` (403).
- workcopy.js `makeFootageCopies` (929-956) reads parts through `readAll`; plan-project.js `copyOfFootage` (86).
- late-wait.js `projectWaits` (183-186): "reading" while a read run is at work and the record holds no reading yet, "verify" while the import's check is owed (`owedFor`); `waitFacts` (171-176). Used by app.js (89 in `readyAt`, 432-450 in `adoptProject` and `makeProject`), app-reel.js (120, 194, 211, 297), edit.js (1185 in `claimAndBring`, 5828 in `guideCopy`).
- Phase 1's parked items here: M2 (a past-due pending: reading at once, copy refused) has no local case; `reading.js` `AGAIN` (66) puts a flag in her words.

**Files:** `tool/reading.js`, `tool/unedited.js` (a local put beside `intoEpisode`), `tool/edit.js` (`waitForImport`, `answerKind`, `setAsideWritten`), `tool/join.js`, `tool/workcopy.js`, `tool/footage.js`, `tool/plan-project.js`, `tool/explainer.js`, `tool/late-wait.js`, `tool/app.js`, `tool/app-reel.js`; tests `reading.test.js`, `edit-kind.test.js`, `edit-workcopy*.test.js`, `join.test.js`, `footage*.test.js`, `app.test.js` (where `plan-project.js` is tested, with `join.test.js`), `edit-app*.test.js`, `late-wait.test.js`.

**Interfaces.** Produces in `unedited.js`:
- `putHere(found, files, { change, expectRev, home, writer }) -> { put: true, record } | { put: false, why }`: it first writes `video/.put.json`, the renames it will make; then each file written whole into `video/` under a name of its own; each file it replaces renamed into `video/history/<stamp> <name>`; then renamed into place; then the record written to Dropbox with `expectRev`, by the rule; then the journal removed. A file it is given under `history/` (a reading's own set-aside: reading.js 504, 669) is written into `video/history/` and never listed; `putHere` lists only files outside `history/`. A refused record write renames each replaced file back and removes the new ones, and gives `{ put: false, why }` in `intoEpisode`'s words for that case. A journal left by a stopped run is undone first by the next `putHere` on that episode. No wait and no check.
- Callers: `reading.js` `written` and `writtenSession` for a local record (in place of `keepCheckInStep` + `intoEpisode`); join.js's filing, and `plan … voice`'s run (explainer.js), which files a voice as the plan's video; `setAsideWritten` uses `video/history/` for a local file.
- `late-wait.js`: for a local record `projectWaits` drops only its "verify" branch, and "reading" stays; `waitFacts` gives nothing.

**Ruling for L4: a plan is filmed on one Mac.** A planned record with no footage is on no Mac. The first `footage` answer for it on a local Mac writes two records: the plan's first, with its `expectRev`, adding `local: { on: <this Mac> }` (layout 6, still a plan by L2); then the part's. A run stopped between them leaves a plan marked local with no part yet, which is harmless: the answer is said again. `plan … voice` on a local Mac marks the plan local the same way before it files the voice, and files it with `putHere`. A video answered as footage on another Mac for a plan already on this one is refused, and nothing is written; and that Mac's planned card never offers it: `plannedOffers` (footage.js 82) and the plans waiting for footage (52, 466) leave out a plan whose `local.on` is another Mac. A plan whose footage began before the switch has no `local`, and joins the old way (L8); a part dropped since is read from its own `video/`. Cost if wrong: footage filmed on two Macs is dropped on one.

**Failing tests first:**
- `reading.test.js`, a raw reading of a local episode: no `READ_LOOK_MS` wait, no lock file, no mark, no pending; the reading's files are in `video/` (no Final Cut file is made since Phase 2's F3; the rough cut's render is in the episode's work folder, in `renders/`, as for any episode, and listed nowhere), the replaced ones in `video/history/`; the record is written last; no `wait` of `LATE_CHECK_MS`; the notice goes at once.
- "a local session's reading writes nothing under `Episodes/<folder>/` but its record", and does not wait; "a local session read twice leaves nothing in `Episodes/<folder>/history/`, and nothing of its earlier teachings in its record": they are in `video/history/`.
- "a refused record write puts the earlier files back": the record write fails with `CHANGED`; `video/` holds the earlier files, byte for byte; the result says why, with the `AGAIN` line.
- "a reading stopped between its renames and its record is undone by the next put": `video/` holds the files the record lists, at its sizes.
- `edit-kind.test.js`: a local raw answer starts the reading and the copy, and says `waitForReading`'s words, with no time of Dropbox's.
- `app.test.js`: "no project is made while a local read run is at work" (`projectWaits` "reading"); on a local episode `readyAt` names no Dropbox time and `projectWaits` is never "verify".
- `edit-workcopy*.test.js`: the copy of a local episode reads `video/<file>`, with no wait step in the panel.
- `join.test.js`: two parts joined; the joined video is in the plan's `video/`; no pending; `kickstart` never called; the notice is `Ready: "<title>" is made from its footage. In Claude, say "what's next?"`.
- A single part filed into the plan's `video/` by a clone on this Mac (the part stays its own episode).
- `footage.test.js`: the first part answered for a plan on a local Mac writes the plan's record with `local.on`, then the part's; "a footage answer stopped after the plan's record and before the part's: the plan is local and still a plan, and the answer can be said again"; on another Mac, `footage "<label>" "<plan>" talking --client no` says `"<plan>" is filmed on the Mac Studio: drop its footage there. Nothing was changed.` and writes nothing, and that Mac's planned card does not offer the plan.
- `plan … voice` on a local Mac: the plan's record gets `local`, the voice is in `video/`, and no pending is written.
- `AGAIN`: `Nothing was changed. Tell me what it is again, and Claude reads it again.`

**Steps:** tests; fail; `putHere` and its journal; the callers; join and footage; the copies; `AGAIN`; whole suite; commit.

**Words Sallie sees:** the join's notice above; `AGAIN` above; `"<plan>" is filmed on the Mac Studio: drop its footage there. Nothing was changed.` No line names Dropbox for a local episode.

**Contract touched:** none. **Size:** L.

### L5. The checks run behind, and nothing waits for them

The coordinator adds this to the plan before the builder starts (plan, "How each task is built"). Rulings 0 to 6 are the plan's, word for word; 7 to 36 settle what they leave open. The coordinator accepts or rejects six of them before any code: ruling 7 departs from ruling 2 (the `final` pending's file name); ruling 14 reads ruling 3's "the record in force holds this revision" for an export; ruling 16 departs from today's finish, which made a gone episode folder's `final/` again and flagged a problem (a check now puts a gone folder's pending away with one short notice, and a `record` check makes the folder again only while this Mac holds the episode's `video/`); ruling 30 keeps ruling 4's moved `keepCheckInStep` under its old name as a one-line wrapper; ruling 32 departs from ruling 0's "only when it is gone" (a first record Dropbox left unreadable is put back too); and ruling 33 departs from D5's "15 minutes after its copy" (a kind's 15 minutes count from the latest of the copy, the Mac's last wake and its last start). Before either builder starts, the coordinator also updates the pre-flight row "L4 / L7, L5 / L7" to what L5 really changes in edit.js (`findEpisode`, `finishEpisode`'s Reels branch, `status`, `guideFacts`, `stoppedWords`, `saveSpellings`, `lateCheckWaits`' callers, `setAsideWritten`, `resolveIn` and `HELP`'s `guide` lines) and in `tool/tests/episodes.js` (`setup()`'s kick stand-in), and either merges L7 after L5, rebased on it, or tells L7's builder not to touch `guideFacts`' `checking` lines or `setup()`'s kick; and moves into L9's list, by name, the two skill sentences of Words ("Her chat", "The skill twins, for L9"), since L7 and L9 hold the skill files one at a time. By-hand steps for the plan's P3-7 are at the end ("By hand, for the coordinator to add"). Jason's answers of 7 Nov settle the four questions: the launch agent deletes the export from Downloads after the check, on every Mac ("Yes, let it delete": Known limits 7, By hand); Power Nap stays on ("fine, leave power nap on": ruling 33 stands); a check that waits looks every 5 minutes for up to an hour ("yes": ruling 36 stands); and a vanished episode folder is "not always" his doing ("not always, keep a short notice": ruling 16 changed). Line numbers are at f690ed9 (L1, L1b, L2, L2c, L8, L3, L4, L1d and L1c built); a builder finds code by its function's name. What L5 takes from the built tasks is under "Consumes", checked against that code; `L5-task.md.before-reconcile` keeps the design as it was made at c125ae7.

**Reconciled with the built code (7 Nov):** (1) every interface L5 consumes is now the built one, and every line number is re-taken at f690ed9; (2) L3 added the hook ruling 10 asked for, textWriter's `onCopy` with `deps.onRecordCopy`: the finish uses it, and L5 adds none to dropbox.js (D1 and D2 become nets); (3) a record write that throws after its pending was written always takes the pending away, and a pending whose record never reached Dropbox and of which nothing is at its `to` is dropped before the folder is looked at (algorithm B, step 0), so nothing is put back for an import whose `video/` was emptied (I2); (4) ruling 16 posts a short notice; (5) the built code changed these too: `keepCheckInStep`'s problem words give `importHold` the status folders (L3), so its wrapper does; the local import's run now waits for its record's check after the banner, so L3's test at unedited.test.js 2848 and I1 and I4-I7 look during the run; L3 already writes no import pending on a local Mac, L4 already makes `importHold`, `lateCheckAt`, `readingWaitsUntil`, `localSource`, `waitFacts` and `projectWaits`' "verify" give nothing for a local record, and L8 already keeps "Make online-only" out of a local video's guide; a reused part's words are read through `readJson`, which gives null for a file gone, not a throw.

**Goal:** On a local episode (its record has `local`), the finish ends as soon as its hand-off is written. Dropbox's check of its copies in `final/` is made 15 minutes later by the launch agent's loop, from a pending on this Mac's disk; so is the check of a local import's first record, and later (L6) of `Original files/`. A spelling, a second hand-off, the Reels' hand-off, a correction and a session's hand-off go ahead at once, and the check never puts an older file back over theirs. An episode from before the switch is finished, checked and stopped as today, line for line.

**Evidence** (f690ed9):
- The finish checks in its own run: `finishNow` (finish.js 670-1177) copies into `final/` (`put`, 943-951; `taken`, 952-963, only an export in Downloads by `sameFolder` since L1b, 963), keeps `lastCopy` (988), writes its record by the rule with the work copy kept (`recordWriter`, 1051-1064), shows the hand-off (1068), leaves `finish.checking.json` (`markChecking`, 1074-1085), ends its bar (1091), waits (`my.wait`, 1097), checks by size (1100-1113; `stillMine` and `movedOn`, 1107-1108), puts its record back when it is gone and no later record is there (1118-1127; `laterRecord`, 632), deletes the exports it took when `holds` (1130-1141; `unchanged`, 139), says the online-only line (1143), and in `finally` clears the marker, flags a problem and posts `problemNotice` (58; 1153-1167), clears the note when it ended well (1168), lets a claim go (1170-1173) and removes its `work` (1175). The run holds `episode.lock` throughout (`runDetached`, detached.js 172-222), so every command meanwhile is stopped.
- The marker is believed only while the run's lock is held with its nonce: `checkingNow` (finish-check.js 60-74); `markChecking` (41), `clearChecking` (47), `flagProblem` (78), `clearProblem` (83), `problemFlagged` (92). Its readers: `status` (edit.js 1621-1703; the checking line at 1672-1673, only while `command === "finish"`), `stoppedWords` (1893-1899; callers 2137, 3326, 4198, 4648), `guideFacts` (6053-6142; `checked` at 6095, only while `running === "finish"`), whose `checking` feeds `guideItems`' `also` line (6269; `checkSentence` 6224, `ON_THE_CHECK` 6231) and guide.js `reelsStep` (402-405) and `videoNext` (456, 463).
- What else waits on a check: `lateCheckWaits` (edit.js 3706-3709; asked at 3957 and 4057 in `saveSpellings`, 3938).
- The writers into `final/`: `finishNow` (the finish and `finish … reels`); `finishFinished` (finish.js 1342-1513: its puts 1472-1483, its record 1490, `putBack` 1449-1470), which `importHandoff` (edit.js 3821-3840) calls for a finished video's or an iPhone clip's hand-off from the import's captions; `saveSpellings`' raw branch (its puts 4059-4063, its record through `save` at 4071; `save` 1861-1880); `finishSession` (finish.js 1547-1686: its puts and record 1612-1632, the earlier hand-off removed after the record 1669-1675); `setAsideWritten` (edit.js 5225-5306: record 5266, removals after it 5291-5297), which `kind … --again` (`answerKind`, 4809) and `emptyRevisionAgain` (5360) use; and `resolveIn` (2780-), which for a keep of B writes its record (2999-3001, `write` 2912) and then puts B in A's place (`replaceIn`, 3018). Several read files in `final/` well before they would take a lock, and build their own from them: `saveSpellings` each part's captions and words (4013) and the hand-off (4036), having checked only that `finals` (3985) are there and whole on this Mac; `finishFinished` the hand-off on file (1393) and, through `respellHeldReels` (1265), each held Reel's captions and words (1294); `finishNow` a reused part's captions and words (810-811) and, for the Reels, the hand-off on file (`reelsBase`, 733); `setAsideWritten` clones the files it sets aside from `final/` into `history/` (5259).
- R1c's machinery: `takeLateCheckLock` (filing.js 655-660), `releaseLateCheckLock` (663), `settleReading` (672-687, sizes alone), `dueAt` (640), `LOCK_AGAIN_MS` (643), `lateCheckLockOf` (649); `importEpisodes` (857-1056) with `checked` keyed by id (869), `mine()` (875), `finishDue` (887-920), the per-minute timer (970-982, its try/catch 976-980), `clearRuns` first (1004), `finishDue(true)` unguarded at the run's start (1007), the loop (1015-1054) and its last look (1016-1027), its wait a minute at most against the wall clock (`clock`, 858; `Math.min(LOOK_AGAIN_MS, left)`, 1029-1032), `waitingStatus` (701-707) written for every open pending (972, 1031, 1045). The launch agent's deps carry no `clock` and no `alive` (`depsFor`, unedited.js 1624-1629; `uneditedFolder` adds `episode`, 1513). `keepCheckInStep` (late-wait.js 108-161; its problem words call `importHold` at 130, with `statusFolders: statusReadFolders(ctx)` since L3), `LOCK_LOOK_MS` and `LOCK_WAIT_MS` (75-76); its one caller is reading.js (import 36, call 623), only for a record without `local` since L4 (615).
- Sleep: the loop's wait comes back within a minute of a wake, and `clock()` then shows every hour slept. `process.hrtime` is no awake clock here: on the Mac Studio's node, the one its launch agent runs (`/opt/homebrew/bin/node`, v26.11.1, libuv 1.53.0), it read 3,244,679 s on 7 Nov, equal to `CLOCK_MONOTONIC_RAW`, which runs on through sleep, against `CLOCK_UPTIME_RAW`'s 2,677,453 s. `sysctl -n kern.waketime` gives the last wake (`{ sec = 1793966294, usec = 876198 } Fri Nov  6 06:58:14 2026`), and `sec = 0` on a Mac that has not slept since it started; `sysctl -n kern.boottime` gives the last start in the same form. A restart is no wake: the launch agent starts at login (`RunAtLoad`, install.sh 359), at the same moment as Dropbox.
- Pendings are `pending/<id>.json` (`pendingFile` 587, `writePending` 590-595, `readPendings` 598-615, `*.json` alone, so a folder in `pending/` is never read as an import's). Readers: `mine()`, `clearRuns` (1061-1079, which reads the pendings before it asks which runs are alive, and calls `resolve(p.work)` for every pending, 1069, which throws for `null`), `owedFor` (late-wait.js 24), `importHold` (unedited.js 1281-1306, at 1297), `heldInOld` (switch.js 111), `lateCheckOwed` (edit.js 1713).
- The import's first record is one of its late check's entries on a Mac whose drop folder is in Dropbox (filing.js `put`, 193-200, and the record, 227; `keepLateCheck`, unedited.js 582-591; `keepWork`, 569). On a local Mac (L3) `fileEpisode` files through `fileLocal` (filing.js 308-394), whose `entries` are the first record's alone; `afterFiling` (unedited.js 595-608) writes no import pending on a local filing, and keeps the run folder only when `deps.onRecordCopy` is set (598).
- `ownLateCheck` judges a file by its bytes, never its size alone (`whose`, unedited.js 1000-1009; `sameBytes` 891 and `startsAlike` 868, not exported); `lateCheck` (dropbox.js 89-110) judges by size alone and makes a missing folder again (`mkdirSync(…, { recursive: true })`, 103); `isWhole` (123-136); `textWriter` (221-235) writes the work copy, calls `onCopy` when it is given (228, L3's), and clones it in.
- `writeEpisode` (episode.js 677-692) makes `record/` (688) and writes `record/<expectRev + 1>.json`, then notes it in `written.json` (690), only once the clone has returned; `acceptRev` (654) lowers that note after `undone` (659-664) fires, so `written.json` is no witness that a record did *not* reach Dropbox, but a rev it notes was written. `lastWritten` (623). `recordNumbers` (572-580), `readEpisode` (605-619), which passes over a newest record that does not read and gives the one before.
- Every writer asks `undone` before it writes, and says `Dropbox undid this Mac's last change to <name>. Say it again.` and calls `acceptRev` when it fires: `claimAndBring` (edit.js 1255, 1298), `save` (1863), `finishEpisode` (2120), `noteAgreement` (3227), `saveSpellings` (4051), `setAsideWritten` (5231), `claimAndWait` (5423), `finishFinished` (finish.js 1425), `finishSession` (1593); and L4's `putHereNow` (put-here.js 51), which writes a local record's next record and nothing in `final/`. Each found its episode through `findEpisode` (edit.js 753-799), where every command that names an episode finds it, before it reads anything of it. `respelledHandoffV2` (spelling.js 88-106) keeps the hand-off on file's `main` and swaps only its `srt`. `toolLog` (edit.js 4790-4798) appends a line to `~/Library/Logs/brimm-video.log`, where detached runs write.
- The import has the drop file and what it was when it arrived in hand where it files: `file` and `arrived` (`fileId(file)`, unedited.js 536); today's import pending keeps them as `drop` and `dropFile` (`keepLateCheck`, 584), and `finishLate` asks `sameFile(pending.drop, pending.dropFile)` (filing.js 727) before it deletes it. `sameFile` (filing.js 625-629) counts a file as the same when no id is given.
- The gate: `gated` (gate.js 88), `sessionNotCleared` (69), `gatedAsOf` (111), `heldName` (95), and `linksOf(root).sessionsBeside(record, folder)` (finish.js 323-361).
- The kick: `contextFor` takes `kickstart` and `agentRunning` from its deps or the real ones (edit.js 410-411; real ones 1517-1528, which run `launchctl`). The test world's `setup()` (tests/episodes.js 42-157) stands in `agentRunning: () => false` (108) and gives no `kickstart`: a test that reached it would run the real `launchctl kickstart`.
- The launch agent posts notices through `banner` (unedited.js 1613), given to `importEpisodes` as `notify` (`depsFor`, 1628; `uneditedFolder`, 1515).
- A finish's quick part writes `finish.asked.json` (edit.js 2225, its `at` a local stamp) before it starts the run, which takes `episode.lock` only once node has loaded and `findEpisode` has run (`runDetached`, detached.js 172; `runStarted` waits `START_WAIT_MS`, 5 s, edit.js 2244). finish-ended.js gives `askedAt(home, folder)` (15) and `endedSinceYes(home, folder)` (28), which counts a result only when its run began at or after the yes.
- `fileId(path)` (filing.js 618) gives `{ ino, size, mtimeMs }` or null. `replaceIn` deletes a file and clones its copy in, so a copy put back is a new inode. `noteWritten` (episode.js 634-649) passes over a note it cannot write.
- Where a writer stops before its lock over a file in `final/` that is gone or not at its size: `saveSpellings`' `gone` (3986-3987: `… is not in the episode's folder, so the final transcripts cannot be spelled again. Nothing was changed. Say finish again.`) and `coming` (3988-3992); `finishNow`'s reused part, its captions read with `readFileSync` (810), whose throw reaches the outer catch (1146-1147) with its message (a path), and its words through `readJson` (811), which gives null for a file gone and throws nothing; `reelsBase`'s `missingWords` (finish.js 1207, called at 733, and in the Reels' quick part through `reelsWhy`, edit.js 2022-2034, asked at 2102, before any run starts); `finishFinished`'s `standing` (1360-1368), which calls a file of another size than the record lists "wrong", so `coming` (1376-1380) and `respellHeldReels`' `coming` (1285, 1407) send it to a `bring` run (`importHandoff`, edit.js 3824-3826: `Dropbox is still bringing … over: say it again in a minute.`), and a gone hand-off holding Reels gives `lostReelsWords` (1401).
- Her chat's words of the finish's check: `HELP`'s `guide` lines (edit.js 139-142: `… with a line of when Studio checks Dropbox's copies (a spelling and handing it off again wait until then), though the finish goes on checking them for a quarter of an hour.`), pinned by edit-guide-app.test.js 631 and written into `tests/fixtures/today-says.json` (`UPDATE_TODAY=1`, edit-labels.test.js 156-157); the skill twins' `the finish goes on checking Dropbox's copies for a quarter of an hour, which she need not wait for` (tool/studio/SKILL.md 75, SKILL-open.md 69) and `… a quarter of an hour longer, which she need not wait for.` (SKILL.md 222, SKILL-open.md 218), pinned by studio.test.js 756 and 768.

**Files:**
- `tool/filing.js`: the pending kinds and their readers and writers; `dueAt`, `lastWake`, `pastLookUntil`, `isOpen`, `WAIT_LOOK_MS`, `WAIT_LOOK_FOR_MS`; `clearRuns`; `importEpisodes` (`mine`, `checked`, the wake, `finishDue`'s dispatch and decision table, the waiting statuses, ruling 6's read); `settleReading`; `takeOutOfCheck`, `restoreReads` and `busyWords`, with `LOCK_LOOK_MS` and `LOCK_WAIT_MS` (moved in); `CHECKS`, `checkRecord`, `recordHolds`, `checkFinal`, `putOwnRecordBack`, `ownRecordBack`; `finishAtWork`, `FINISH_START_MS`; `askAgent`; `logTo`.
- `tool/dropbox.js`: `sameBytes` and `startsAlike` (moved from unedited.js, unchanged). `textWriter`'s `onCopy` (L3's) is used as it is (ruling 10).
- `tool/finish-check.js`: `problemNotice` (moved from finish.js, unchanged words), `clearChecking`'s revision, `checkingNow`'s local branch.
- `tool/finish.js`: `finishNow`'s local branch; the lock in `finishFinished` and `finishSession` on a local record; `problemNotice` imported.
- `tool/unedited.js`: the local import's `record` pending (`workOn`'s hook, `afterFiling`'s `copied`, the filing's two catches); `sameBytes` and `startsAlike` imported from dropbox.js.
- `tool/late-wait.js`: `keepCheckInStep` becomes a one-line wrapper of `takeOutOfCheck`; `LOCK_LOOK_MS` and `LOCK_WAIT_MS` re-exported from filing.js.
- `tool/edit.js`: `findEpisode` (ruling 35), `finishEpisode` (its Reels branch: `restoreReads` before `reelsWhy`, algorithm F), `saveSpellings`, `setAsideWritten`, `resolveIn`, `stoppedWords`, `status`, `guideFacts`; `HELP`'s `guide` lines (Words, "Her chat").
- `tool/tests/fixtures/today-says.json`: written again with `UPDATE_TODAY=1`, its help lines alone changed.
- `tool/guide.js`: doc comments only (the `checking` fact, 177-182; `reelsStep`, 399-401).
- `README.md`: "Finishing an episode" steps 5 to 7, "What she is told, and when" and "Afterwards" (333-349); "What is where", the rows of filing.js, late-wait.js and finish-check.js, and `pending/` with its kinds.
- Tests: `tool/tests/episodes.js` (setup's kick stand-in); create `tool/tests/checks-world.js`, `tool/tests/checks-behind.test.js`, `tool/tests/local-world.js` and `tool/tests/local-finish.test.js`; change `tool/tests/dropbox.test.js`, `late-wait.test.js`, `finish-check.test.js`, `settings.test.js`, `unedited.test.js` (L3's describe of a local import, 2814-: I1-I7, and its test at 2848, whose run now waits for its record's check after the banner), `edit-session-mark.test.js`, `edit-guide-app.test.js` (the `guide` help's new clause).
- Not changed: `tool/finish-reels.js` (its `SAY_AGAIN` is used), `tool/finish-ended.js` (`askedAt` and `endedSinceYes` are used), `tool/reading.js` (it goes on calling `keepCheckInStep`), `tool/switch.js` (`heldInOld` already reads `pending/*.json` alone; test H1 holds it), `tool/importing.js`, the hand-off modules and fixtures, `tool/shared/`, `lib.js`, `run.js`; the skill twins and `studio.test.js` (L9's, by the coordinator's move).

---

#### Interfaces

**Consumes**, by exact name:
- L1 (built): `settings.js` `isLocal`, `episodeFilesFolder`, `workFolder`, `episodeWork`, `pendingFolder`, `runsFolder`, `downloadsFolder`, `sameFolder`.
- L1b (built): the finish's `taken` holds only an export from Downloads by `sameFolder` (finish.js 963); `exportsFolderOf`.
- L1d (built): `namesQuietly` is gone: every Mac names a waiting video by its label, and nothing L5 adds names a video by its file.
- L1c (built): `guideItems` gives `{ items, old, unreadable }` (edit.js 6233-) from `guideDrops`' `{ videos, unreadable }` (6177), and guide.js `overviewLines` may lead with `dropUnreadable` (30, 579-584); L5 leaves them as they are, and its `also` line stays where it is (6269).
- L2 (built): a local record holds `local: { on: "<Mac>" }` at layout 6 (`LOCAL_VERSION = 6`, `NEWEST_VERSION = LOCAL_VERSION`, episode.js 50-51; `newEpisode` takes `local`), read and written back whole by `readEpisode` and `writeEpisode`, so every record a writer writes on it stays local; where.js `keptOnAMac(record)` (it holds `local`: every new path of L5 that this design says "with `local`" asks it), `filePlace(record, rel)` ("dropbox" for `final/…`, `record/…`, `history/…`, `Original files/…` and a plan's three files), `folderAt` and `fileAt` (finish.js and edit.js call it as `fileOf`, 44 and 127); the gate refuses a local episode on any other Mac (`ON_OTHER_MAC`, `gateOf`, edit.js 472-473) before a command reads it, so only this Mac writes a local episode's `final/` and records, and its `finished.on` is always this Mac.
- L2c (built): `finishNow` reads the cut plan (finish.js 782), a reused part's words (811) and, for the Reels, the import's words (874) through `fileOf`; `saveSpellings` reads its `finals` through `fileOf` (edit.js 3985-3988, 4013, 4036, 4061), which for a file in `final/` is the episode's folder in Dropbox; `finishEpisode`'s `problemsOf` (edit.js 1191-1195, asked at 2131) looks for a local file in `video/`; `final/` and Dropbox's `history/` are read as today.
- L3 (built): `fileEpisode(args, deps)` on a local Mac (`deps.episode.local`, and no `into`) is `fileLocal` (filing.js 308-394): `video/` with its marker `.import.json` (`IMPORT_MARK`, 63; `{ import: importIdOf(dropFile), drop: dropIdOf(dropFile) }`, 631-637), each made file and the recording written whole in it, then the first record through `textWriter({ work: join(work, "record"), keep: true, onCopy: deps.onRecordCopy, … })` (334). `textWriter` (dropbox.js 221-235) calls `onCopy({ from, to, bytes })` once the work copy `from` (`<work>/record/<pid>-<n>-0001.json`) is written whole and before it clones it to `to` (`<Episodes>/<folder>/record/0001.json`); a throw from `onCopy` puts nothing in, keeps the work copy (`keep`) and is thrown on. The hook is given no record: L5 reads the id from `from`'s text. `writeEpisode` notes rev 1 in `written.json` (with `home`) once the clone has returned (episode.js 690). A throw before the record is in place (the hook's, or `cloneIn`'s, which takes away what it cloned) reaches `fileLocal`'s catch (370-381), which removes what the run wrote in `video/` and the marker, then `video/` and the work folder when empty, and `record/` and the episode's folder when this run made them, and throws on; nothing after its `writeEpisode` throws (the drop file's delete, 384-392, is in a `try`). `workOn`'s catch of the filing (unedited.js 649-660 for the picture-only filing, 777-790 for the main one) then fails the import (`it could not be filed into Episodes: <reason>`, its failed status, the notice `<label> could not be put into Episodes. It stays in the drop folder; see the log.`), and its `finally` removes the run folder (`keepWork` false, 811). Once `fileEpisode` returns, `afterFiling` (595-608, called inside the filing's `try`, 648 and 776) writes no import pending on a local filing, and keeps the run folder only when `deps.onRecordCopy` is set (598). `workOn` (486-) passes its own `deps` (made again at 580, with `log`, `files` and `progress`) to both `fileEpisode` calls (647, 772), with `file` (the drop file), `arrived` (its `fileId` when it arrived, 536), `label` (558) and `work` (`<runsFolder>/<pid>-<time>`, 566) in scope. `fileLocal` deletes the drop file once the record is written and leaves one it cannot delete, held by its episode (`already imported as <label> (<short id>)`); L5 takes no witness from the drop file's absence (she may take it away by hand: ruling 9), only from its being there as it arrived (algorithm B, step 6). `findFolder` (filing.js 95-) gives a stopped import's folder again by its marker only while `Episodes/<name>` has no whole record, and otherwise a name no folder in Episodes or `BRIMM Video Work` has. Statuses are written by `writeStatusIn` into `statusWriteFolder(episode)` and read by `readStatusesIn` from `statusReadFolders(episode)` (filing.js 409, 412): `episode.statuses` and `episode.statusFolders`, which `uneditedFolder` gives (unedited.js 1513), else `Episodes/.importing`. `importEpisodes(dir, deps, importOne)` keeps its signature. L3's tests of a local import are the describe "an episode filed on a Mac with a drop folder of its own (Phase 3, L3)" (unedited.test.js 2814-), its world `local()` (2826, on `episodic({ local: true })`, whose `wait` moves its clock, 1286-1292), and its hook's test gives `t.deps.onRecordCopy` (2882).
- L4 (built): a local reading puts its files through reading.js `putLocal` (649-660) and unedited.js `putHere` (1249), which is put-here.js `putHereNow` (22-): its journal `video/.put.json`, its files renamed into `video/`, its record last by `expectRev` (never `record/0001.json`), with no `late-check.lock`, no pending and nothing in `final/`; it asks `undone` (51), keeps its record's work copy (`join(work, "record")` for a reading, `.runs/record-<pid>` with no `work`), and passes an `onRecordCopy` it is given on as `onCopy` (reading.js 655 gives its deps' own; L5 gives none). reading.js calls `keepCheckInStep` only for a record without `local` (615-623). filing.js `fileIntoPlanHere` (243-268) files the join's or a voice's video into a local plan by `putHereNow`, with `entries: []` and no pending; `afterFiling` keeps no run folder for it (602-605), and L5 gives it no hook. For a local record (`keptOnAMac`) late-wait.js `localSource`, `lateCheckAt`, `readingWaitsUntil` and `waitFacts` give null and `projectWaits` only "reading" or "", and unedited.js `importHold` gives `{ why: "", waits: false, state: "" }` (1285); `importHold` now takes `statusFolders`. `setAsideWritten` (edit.js 5225-5306) sets a local file aside into `video/history/`, unlisted, and a `final/` file into Dropbox's `history/`, listed (5228-5229, 5257-5262). join.js `afterJoin` asks no agent run for a local plan (622-623).
- Today's code: `takeLateCheckLock`, `releaseLateCheckLock`, `settleReading`, `finishLate`, `cloneIn`, `replaceIn`, `isWhole`, `readEpisode`, `recordNumbers`, `recordFile`, `recordText`, `lastWritten`, `episodesFolder`, `conflictedCopies`, `conflictedOf`, `sameFile`, `fileId`, `statusReadFolders`, `clockTime`, `markChecking`, `flagProblem`, `clearProblem`, `runningCommand`, `askedAt`, `endedSinceYes`, `shownName`, `withShortId`, `gated`, `gatedAsOf`, `linksOf`, `listedAs`, `checkSentence`, `keptOnAMac`.

**Produces.**

*The pending kinds.* Every pending is written whole on this Mac (a `.writing` name, then renamed). `id` is always the episode's record id; `name` its folder's name (its work folder in `BRIMM Video Work`, where its locks are); `folder` its folder in Episodes. A pending's key is its file's path from `pendingFolder(home)`.

| kind | key | shape |
|---|---|---|
| import (today) | `<id>.json` | as today, with no `kind` |
| `record` | `record/<id>.json` | `{ version: 1, kind: "record", id, name, folder, root, machine, label, drop, dropFile, entries: [{ from, to, bytes }], work, lastCopy, copied?: true, problem?: "<text>", nextLook?: <ms>, lookUntil?: <ms> }`: `entries` the first record's entry alone; `work` the import's run folder; `label` the import's label, by which every line names it; `drop` the drop file's path and `dropFile` what it was when it arrived (`{ ino, size, mtimeMs }`, or null), as today's import pending keeps them; `copied` once its record is in Dropbox (ruling 9); `nextLook` and `lookUntil` once its check has waited (ruling 36) |
| `final` | `final/<id>-<revision>.json` (ruling 7) | `{ version: 1, kind: "final", id, name, folder, root, machine, revision, rev, entries: [{ from, to, bytes }], took: [{ path, snap: { size, mtimeMs }, name, place: "Downloads", key }], work, lastCopy, record: { from, to, bytes }, copied?: true, reading?: { pid, at, to: [path], nonce, rev }, problem?: "<text>", told?: <ms>, wasProblem?: "<text>", nextLook?: <ms>, lookUntil?: <ms> }`: `entries` the copies put into `final/` (never the record); `rev` the record number the finish writes; `record` its record's entry, there from the first write (ruling 10); `took[].key` the part's key; `reading` a writer's mark (ruling 19); `told` the time a writer last took an entry out of it while it had a `problem`, and `wasProblem` that problem's text (ruling 17); `nextLook` and `lookUntil` once its check has waited or been put off (rulings 14, 15, 36). It keeps no name: its lines name it as ruling 15 says |
| `original` (L6) | `original/<id>.json` | L6's; L5 reads it only through `readEveryPending`, `clearRuns` and the dispatch |

*`tool/filing.js`:*
- `PENDING_KINDS = ["record", "final", "original"]`.
- `pendingKeyOf(pending) -> string`: `<id>.json` for one with no `kind`; `final/<id>-<revision>.json` for a `final`; `<kind>/<id>.json` for any other.
- `writePending(home, pending)`: writes `join(pendingFolder(home), pendingKeyOf(pending))` whole, making its folder; throws when it cannot. For a pending with no `kind` this is today's file, so every caller of today is unchanged.
- `readPendingAt(home, key) -> pending | null`: the pending in that file when it reads whole, is `version: 1`, has an `entries` list and a finite `lastCopy`, and its own key is `key`.
- `readPendingsOf(home, kind) -> [pending]`: every `*.json` in `pending/<kind>/` that reads whole, is `version: 1`, has that `kind`, a string `id`, an `entries` list and a finite `lastCopy` (a `final` one also integer `revision` and `rev`), and whose key is its file's; oldest `lastCopy` first. A folder that cannot be read gives [].
- `readEveryPending(home) -> [pending]`: `readPendings(home)` and each of `PENDING_KINDS`' `readPendingsOf`, oldest `lastCopy` first.
- `removePending(home, pending)`: removes its file (`force`).
- `readPendings(home)`: unchanged (the import's alone).
- `dueAt(pending, woke = null) -> ms`: `Math.max(base, Number.isFinite(pending.nextLook) ? pending.nextLook : -Infinity)`, where `base` is `pending.lastCopy` for a pending with `owed: true` (L6's move, due at once), `pending.lastCopy + LATE_CHECK_MS` for one with no `kind` (an import's, as today), and `Math.max(pending.lastCopy, Number.isFinite(woke) ? woke : -Infinity) + LATE_CHECK_MS` for any other (ruling 33). A `nextLook` only ever makes a look later (ruling 24).
- `lastWake({ run = spawnSync } = {}) -> ms | null`: the later of the Mac's last wake and its last start (ruling 33). For each of `kern.waketime` and `kern.boottime`, `run("/usr/sbin/sysctl", ["-n", <that name>], { encoding: "utf8" })`, and of its output the first `sec = <digits>`, times 1000, when it ends 0; a name whose run does not end 0, prints no such number, or throws gives nothing. The larger of the two numbers given (a wake of `sec = 0`, on a Mac that has not slept since it started, gives 0, and the start is later); null when neither gives one.
- `pastLookUntil(pending, now) -> boolean`: `Number.isFinite(pending.lookUntil) && now > pending.lookUntil`.
- `isOpen(pending, now) -> boolean`: `!pending.problem && !pastLookUntil(pending, now)`.
- `WAIT_LOOK_MS = 5 * 60 * 1000`, `WAIT_LOOK_FOR_MS = 60 * 60 * 1000`: how soon a check that waits looks again in the run, and for how long a run goes on looking (ruling 36).
- `LOCK_LOOK_MS = 1000`, `LOCK_WAIT_MS = 2 * 60 * 1000` (moved from late-wait.js, same values).
- `takeOutOfCheck(ctx, { name, record, paths, reads = [], rev }, { wait, log = () => {}, problemWhy = () => "" }) -> Promise<{ none: true } | { why } | { end } | { busy: "held" | "unsettled" | "restored" | "lost" }>`: `keepCheckInStep` moved. For a record without `local`: exactly `keepCheckInStep`'s body and results today (`{ none }`, `{ why }` in its words, `{ end }`; `rev` and `reads` unused), where line 130's `importHold(…).why` becomes `problemWhy(record)` and its fallback after `||` stays word for word. For a record with `local`: `{ end }` with `late-check.lock` held, or `{ busy }` with no lock held and no mark written; "restored" has put back a copy among `reads`, or found one changed since the writer read it (ruling 34; algorithm D). `reads` is `[{ path, id }]`: `path` absolute, `id` the `fileId(path)` the writer took just before it read the file, or null for a file it reads only under the lock. `ctx` is a command's: `home`, `root`, `machine`, `alive`, `now`, `isWhole`, and `deps.clone` and `deps.sameDisk` when a test gives them. `end({ put }) -> { reopened: boolean }` is to be called once: `reopened` is true when it wrote a pending without its `problem` (ruling 17); a second call does nothing and gives `{ reopened: false }`.
- `restoreReads(ctx, { name, record, folder, rels, rev }, { wait, log }) -> Promise<"" | "held" | "unsettled" | "restored" | "lost">`: for a record without `local`, "" at once. Else the doubtful ones of `rels` (paths from `folder`): those `record.files` lists whose file is not there (`statSync(…, { throwIfNoEntry: false })` gives nothing) or is there at another size than it lists. None → "", and no lock is asked. Else `takeOutOfCheck(ctx, { name, record, paths: [], reads: <the doubtful ones, absolute, each with id: null>, rev }, { wait, log })`: a `busy` → that word; `{ end }` → `end({ put: false })` and "" (no held pending held them: the writer goes on to today's words). Ruling 34; algorithms E and F call it before their refusals.
- `busyWords(busy, shown, { done, again }) -> string`: the whole sentence a writer says when it is `busy`, for each of "held", "unsettled", "restored" and "lost" (Words).
- `FINISH_START_MS = 60 * 1000`; `finishAtWork(home, name, alive, now) -> boolean` (ruling 14): `runningCommand(home, name, alive) === "finish"`, or `askedAt(home, name)` is a time `at` with `0 <= now - at < FINISH_START_MS` and `endedSinceYes(home, name)` is null (a yes given within the minute whose run has not ended: its run may not hold `episode.lock` yet).
- `askAgent(ctx, shown)`: `if (!ctx.agentRunning()) ctx.kickstart()` in one `try`; a throw is written with `logTo(ctx.home)` as the (kick) line of Words, named `shown`, and nothing else happens. The writers of algorithm F call it when their `end` gives `reopened`, and `resolveIn` as ruling 36 says.
- `RECORD_LOST(label) -> string`: the notice of a first record that cannot be put back (Words).
- `FOLDER_GONE(who) -> string`: the one line, posted and logged, of a check that puts its pending away because its episode's folder is gone from Episodes (ruling 16; Words).
- `CHECKS = { record: checkRecord, final: checkFinal }`. L6 adds `original` to it.
- `checkRecord(pending, deps)`, `checkFinal(pending, deps)`: synchronous; called by `finishDue` under the episode's `late-check.lock` with the pending read again; each writes or removes its own pending and its `work`, and gives back nothing (algorithms B and C). `deps` is the loop's deps (`episode: { root, machine, home }`, `log`, `notify`, and `clone`, `sameDisk` and `isWhole` when a test gives them, else the rule's and dropbox.js `isWhole`) with `clock` and `alive` laid over them as `importEpisodes` resolves them (`deps.clock || Date.now`, `deps.alive || isRunning`), and `once(line)`, which logs a line once in the run for this pending (algorithm A): the launch agent's own deps carry neither `clock` nor `alive` (`depsFor`), so a check never calls `deps.clock` or `deps.alive` as given. A check called with no `once` (a test's own call) logs those lines with `deps.log`.
- `recordHolds(pending, { isWhole }) -> true | false | null`, by the record file the finish wrote (ruling 11).
- `putOwnRecordBack(pending, { isWhole, opts }) -> { put: boolean, problems: [text] }`: algorithm C, step 3, alone; synchronous; the caller holds the episode's `late-check.lock`. `opts` the rule's options (`clone`, `sameDisk`, `log`).
- `ownRecordBack(ctx, { name, folder, record }) -> boolean`: ruling 35; synchronous; true when it put a record back.
- `logTo(home) -> (line) => void`: appends `line` and a newline to `join(home, "Library", "Logs", "brimm-video.log")` (its folder made), as edit.js `toolLog` does, and passes over a throw. Every writer's `log` (algorithms E and F) and `ownRecordBack`'s are this.
- `settleReading(pending, { home, log })`: as today, but a mark with a `rev` is settled by ruling 20 (the record's number, and this Mac's `written.json` through `lastWritten(home, pending.id)`), reading the record in force past conflicted copies when none is of a file in `record/`; it writes the pending back by its key (`writePending`); its line for a pending with a `kind` is (settled) (Words), and for the import's, today's.
- `clearRuns(home, alive = isRunning)`: ruling 25.
- `importEpisodes(dir, deps, importOne)`: as today, with algorithm A; `deps.checks`, when a test gives it, is laid over `CHECKS`; `deps.wokeAt`, when a test gives it, is what `lastWake` would give: the later of when the Mac last woke and when it last started (ruling 33).

*`tool/dropbox.js`:*
- `sameBytes(a, b) -> boolean` and `startsAlike(a, b, length) -> boolean`: moved from unedited.js (868-895) unchanged and exported; unedited.js imports them.
- `textWriter`: unchanged. Its `onCopy` is L3's (dropbox.js 221-235): called with `{ from, to, bytes }` once the work copy is written whole and before it is put in; a throw from it puts nothing in, keeps the work copy only with `keep`, and is thrown on, so the record's write fails (ruling 10; D1 and D2 hold it).

*`tool/finish-check.js`:*
- `problemNotice(name) -> string`: finish.js's, moved and exported, the same words; finish.js imports it.
- `clearChecking(home, folder, revision)`: with a `revision`, the marker is taken away only when it names that revision; without one, as today.
- `checkingNow(home, folder, record, alive = isRunning)`: for a record without `local`, as today. For a record with `local`: the marker, read and shaped as today (its nonce is not asked), is believed when the file `join(pendingFolder(home), "final", \`${record.id}-${marker.revision}.json\`)` reads as a pending with neither `problem` nor `wasProblem`; no lock is asked, and the record's own revision is not asked (ruling 22: a spelling since leaves that check owed, and it still takes the export out of Downloads). Gives `{ revision, due, took }` as today, or null. (It builds the path from `settings.js` itself; test K3 holds it to `pendingKeyOf`.)

*`tool/finish.js`:* `finishNow(ctx, found, deps)` keeps its signature and result; its local branch is algorithm E. `finishFinished` and `finishSession` keep theirs; on a local record they take the lock (algorithm F), and a busy one gives `{ why }`.

*`tool/unedited.js`:* on a local filing (`episode.local`, and no `into`) `workOn` lays `onRecordCopy` over its `deps` where it makes them again (580), so both `fileEpisode` calls give it to `fileLocal`: it writes the `record` pending (ruling 9), with `drop: file` and `dropFile: arrived`, then calls any `deps.onRecordCopy` it was given (L3's hook test, unedited.test.js 2882), and throws when it cannot write the pending. `afterFiling`'s local branch, which then keeps the run folder (598), writes the pending again with `copied: true` in a `try` of its own (it runs inside the filing's `try`, 648 and 776, whose catch would fail the filing). Each of the filing's two catches (649, 777) first takes away a pending the hook wrote (ruling 9).
