So a copy Dropbox lost or left at another size within the 15 minutes is put back before `coming` would start a `bring` for it or `lostReelsWords` would refuse. (2) After `coming` (1380) and before the reads at 1384-1386: `ids` of `prior.handoff` and of each held Reel's captions and words (every file in `final/` it may read: 1393, 1294). (3) When it puts a file (`ordered`, computed before its `try`: it is pure, and is not empty): `paths` = `ordered.map(([rel]) => join(folder, rel))`, `reads` from `ids` (`[]` with no `prior`), `rev` = `found.record.rev + 1`; busy → `{ why: busyWords(busy, who, { done: "nothing was written", again: "Say it again in a minute." }) }`; `end({ put: true })` after `writeEpisode` (1490), and ask; in its `catch` after `putBack`, and in its `finally`, `end({ put: false })`.
- `finishSession`: `paths` = `[to, ...(prior ? [join(folder, prior)] : [])]`, no `reads` (what it writes is made from the teachings, and a session's earlier hand-off is in no check: `kind … --again` took it out), `rev` = `record.rev + 1`, before its `try`; busy → `{ why: busyWords(busy, who, { done: "nothing was written", again: "Say it again in a minute." }) }`; `end({ put: true })` after `writeEpisode` (1632), and ask; in its `catch` after its put-back, and in its `finally`, `end({ put: false })`.
- `setAsideWritten`, when some of `aside.rels` start with `final/` or `handoff` is given: `paths` = those `final/` paths and `handoff.rel`, absolute, `reads` = those `final/` paths of `aside.rels`, absolute, each with `id: null` (what it clones into `history/`, 5259, under its lock), `rev` = `base.rev + 1`, after its `undone` check and before its `try`; busy → `ctx.say(busyWords(busy, shownName(base), { done: "nothing was changed", again: "Say it again in a minute." }))` and `{ code: 1 }`; `end({ put: true })` after `writeEpisode` (5266), and ask; in its `catch` after its put-back, and in its `finally`, `end({ put: false })`.
- `resolveIn`, for a keep of B of a file that is not a record (`!isRecord`) whose `of` starts with `final/`: after the refusals that end it before any write (`blocked`, the unreadable copy, `keptOverWhy`, A not there) and before step 1's set-aside: `paths` = `[at(of)]`, no `reads` (B is a conflicted copy, in no check, and A is replaced by her choice), `rev` = `record.rev + 1`; busy → `refuse(ctx, busyWords(busy, name, { done: "nothing was changed", again: 'Say "sort out the conflict" again in a minute.' }))`; the rest of the function in a `try` whose `finally` calls `end({ put: replaced })`, `replaced` set once `replaceIn(at(copy), at(of))` (3018) returns. And every `resolveIn` of a local record that ends 0 (any keep, of any file, a reopened pending's included) calls `askAgent(ctx, name)` once, when a `final` pending of `readPendingsOf(ctx.home, "final")` has its record's `id`, `ctx.machine` and this root (ruling 36).

---

#### The walk: each order of a check and a writer, for the three kinds

| kind | order | what happens | test |
|---|---|---|---|
| `record` | its check, then a reading's put (L4) | the check finds `0001.json` ours (or puts it back); `putHere` writes `0002.json` | R1, R4 |
| `record` | a reading's put, then Dropbox loses `0001.json`, then the check | `0001.json` gone and `0002.json` there: nothing put back | R2 |
| `record` | the import stopped before its clone; the check, then the re-import | dropped; the next look imports it into the same folder by its marker | I4, R3 |
| `record` | the import stopped before its clone; the re-import, then the check | `0001.json` is the re-import's (another id): the stopped one's pending dropped | R5b |
| `record` | the import stopped after its clone and before `copied` (or `copied` could not be written); Dropbox then lost `0001.json` | this Mac's `written.json` notes its rev 1, so its record reached Dropbox: put back (once its drop file is no longer there as it arrived) | R11, I6 |
| `record` | the import stopped before its clone, and she then took its video out of the drop folder by hand | no `copied`, no note: dropped, as ruling 0 says | R13 |
| `record` | the import's record write throws after its pending was written (its clone fails), and `fileLocal` empties what the run wrote in `video/` | the pending taken away, and the run folder with the run; one whose removal fails is dropped at its check (step 0): nothing put back, no notice, no folder made | I2 |
| `record` | the import stopped after its clone and before `copied`; Dropbox then left `0001.json` garbled, so the re-import cannot write over it | taken as reached by the note, or by the file beginning as the work copy does: replaced from it; the drop file is then held by its episode (L3); one that is neither waits, its work copy kept | R14 |
| `record` | Dropbox lost `0001.json` while the drop file is still there as it arrived (not deleted yet, or Dropbox undid its delete with the clone) | nothing put back; the pending dropped; the next look imports it again into the same folder by its marker, and no put-back meets that import | R12 |
| `record` | Dropbox undid the making of the episode's folder, its record with it | made again, with its first record, while this Mac holds the episode's `video/`; put away, with the short notice, when it does not; Episodes that cannot be listed waits | R9 |
| `record` | any writer into `final/` | no writer's paths hold `record/0001.json`, so a `record` pending is never marked | T2 |
| `final` | the check holds the lock; a writer comes | the writer looks every `LOCK_LOOK_MS`; the check, judged by the record before the write, ends first; then the writer marks and writes | L8 |
| `final` | a writer holds the lock; the check comes due | put off `LOCK_AGAIN_MS`, said once; then made of what the writer left in it, judged by the writer's record | W1 |
| `final` | a writer died after its marks, before its record | the check takes the dead lock over; the mark is settled: no record numbered `rev` is there, so every marked entry listed at its bytes stays, and the finish's files are put back over the stopped write's, by bytes | W3, W3b |
| `final` | a writer died after its record, before `end` | settled: a record numbered `rev` is there, so the marked entries are the writer's and are left, whatever their size | W2, W2b |
| `final` | a writer died after its marks; a record-only write then took its number | settled as the row above: the stopped writer's files are left unchecked (ruling 20's cost) | W8 |
| `final` | the check died holding the lock | a writer takes the dead lock over and marks what it replaces; the next check makes the rest | W5, T6 |
| `final` | the writer is a finish that died before its record's clone | its own pending: not `copied`, `recordHolds` false, dropped; an earlier pending's mark settled as above | C9, W3 |
| `final` | the writer is a finish that died between its record's clone and `copied` | `recordHolds` true: its check is made | C10, L4 |
| `final` | a second hand-off within 15 minutes | its own pending; the first's no longer holds what the second replaced; both made | L6, L7 |
| `final` | `kind … --again` as a session, right after the hand-off | every `final/` entry it sets aside is taken out; the check puts nothing back and deletes no export; and so if Dropbox then loses the session's record | L14 |
| `final` | another version answered as a session after the hand-off | held beside it: nothing put back, no export deleted, the pending kept and made at each run's start; once that session is cleared, made as any other | C15b, C26 |
| `final` | `resolve` keeps B of a file in `final/` | B's path is taken out; the check leaves B, whatever its size | L13c |
| `final` | Dropbox leaves a copy short, or puts an older one back at its size, and a writer then reads it | a copy at another size is put back by the writer's `restoreReads` before its refusals; one at its size by the writer's judge under the lock; either way from the pending's copy, the writer refused, nothing marked; said again, it reads the copy put back | T10, T16, L21, L22, L23, L13(d), L13(e) |
| `final` | Dropbox loses a copy within the 15 minutes, and a writer then comes | `restoreReads` puts it back before the writer's `gone`, `coming` or `lostReelsWords` words, or its read that throws; the writer is refused with the restored words; said again, it goes | T16, L21(e), L13(a), L23(b), L27(a), L27(d) |
| `final` | a writer reads a short copy; the check then puts it back and goes, before the writer has the lock | the read's id is not the file's now: the writer is refused (restored), though no pending holds the copy any more; said again, it reads the whole copy | T15, L21(d) |
| `final` | a writer read a copy that is not whole and whose copy on this Mac is gone | the writer is refused ("lost"); the check flags that problem | T11 |
| `final` | a second finish is at work on the episode when the first's check comes due, or her yes to it was given within the minute | put off, said once, until that run ends; then each export is judged by the record it left | W9, W9b |
| `final` | her yes to a second finish comes while the check reads the copies | the check asks again under its lock before the first export: nothing deleted, the pending kept with a `nextLook`, made once the finish ends | W10 |
| `final` | a writer replaces the copy a check's problem was about | that pending loses `problem` and gets `wasProblem` and `told`; the writer asks the agent to run; the pending is made again in the run, with no second notice; a later hand-off's own check, passing, takes the note away | C22, T12, L25 |
| `final` | the episode's record cannot be read (a conflicted copy), or Episodes is away a moment, at the check | it waits: looked at again every five minutes for an hour, then at each run's start; `resolve` asks the agent to run | C11, C16, R9, L26 |
| `final` | a finish died before its record, its marks were settled (W3), and the next writer was given its revision | its pending is dropped: its record's number holds the new writer's record, of other bytes | C24 |
| `final` | a finish died before its record (its pending not `copied`); W3 then let the next finish have its revision, whose pending is written over that key while the loop makes another episode's check | read again under its lock, the pending is not the one the loop chose: let go, and made at its own due, not at once | F18 |
| `final` | a finish died before its record, after it put a copy of the earlier one's size; a writer then reads that copy | the dead finish's pending is not judged; the earlier, held one's copy is put back and the writer refused once; said again, it goes | T13 |
| `final` | Dropbox lost the finish's record; then a command names the episode | the command puts the record back first (ruling 35), and builds on it; nothing says the change was undone | L24, T14 |
| `final` | Dropbox lost the finish's record, and a command took its number (the lock was held when it looked) | the check finds its number taken: a problem, the notice, the exports kept | C13 |
| `final` | a writer died after its record, before `end`; Dropbox then lost its record | settled by `written.json`: the writer's files are left, not put back over | S4 |
| `record`, `final` | the Mac sleeps, or is shut down or restarted, past a check's due | the check is made 15 minutes after the wake or the start, not at it (an import's own pending, and L6's owed move, as before) | F13 |
| `original` (L6) | a run starts within 15 minutes of a wake with a pending past its `lookUntil` | looked at once in that run, once it is due, and the run waits for it | F17 |
| `original` (L6) | any writer into `final/` | none of its entries is in `final/`, so no writer marks it; L6's move takes the same lock, so a `final` check and the move never interleave | F4, F7 (L6's own tests) |

#### A crash at each step

| run | stopped | what it leaves | test |
|---|---|---|---|
| local import | after its pending, before its record's clone | pending without `copied`; no `0001.json`; the drop file; `video/` with its marker | I4, R3 |
| local import | after the clone, before `copied` | pending without `copied`; its `0001.json`, and rev 1 noted in `written.json`; the drop file, deleted or not | R4, R11, R12, R14 |
| local import | after `copied`; Dropbox then lost the record | pending with `copied`; no `0001.json` | R1, I5 |
| finish | after `takeOutOfCheck`'s marks, before or during its puts | marks (with `rev`) on earlier pendings; the record before it | W3, W3b |
| finish | after `before` wrote its pending, before the record's clone | its pending without `copied`, with its record's entry; no record holds its revision, or, once W3 has let the next writer have it, that writer's record has its number | C9, C24 |
| finish | after the record's clone, before `copied` | its pending without `copied`; its record holds its revision | C10, L4 |
| finish | after `copied`, before `end` | marks on earlier pendings; its record written | W2, W2b |
| finish | after `end`, before or after its kick | a whole pending, perhaps no agent run asked (made at the agent's next run) | L4, F7's second run |
| spelling or another writer | after its marks, before its record | marks; the record before it | W3 |
| spelling or another writer | after its record, before `end` | marks; its record written, and noted in `written.json` | W2, S4 |
| the check | holding the lock, part-way | a dead lock; the pending as it was; a copy half put back (`replaceIn` deletes first) | W5 |
| the check | after deleting an export, before the pending goes | the export gone; the pending | C17 |
| the check | after taking the note away, before the pending goes | the pending (made again, and noted again if it finds a problem); no note | C27 |
| the check | after the pending goes, before its `work` | a folder no pending names | F3 |
| the loop | while it waits | every pending on disk; the next run makes the due ones at its start | F7 |

---

#### Failing tests first

Every test names its world and what it asserts; every test file keeps its other tests green. The plan's headline cases are: "a local import writes its `record` pending before its record reaches Dropbox" (I1), "its check puts back a lost first record" (R1, I5) "and with a later record there puts nothing back" (R2), "a run stopped between the pending and the record" (I4, R3); "the finish of a local episode ends once its hand-off is written" (L1); "a spelling right after a hand-off writes the next revision at once" (L5); "the due check puts back a missing copy" (C1), "leaves a file the record in force lists at another size" (C2), "deletes the Downloads export only when every copy is whole and it is unchanged" (C4), "flags a problem as today" (C7); "a finish killed after its pending is written still has its check made" (L4, C10); "a `final` pending for a revision the record never held is dropped at its check" (C9); "a writer waits for the check's lock, never for the check" (L8); "a pending in `pending/<kind>/` is never read as an import's" (K1, H1, F6); "a pending with `work: null` stops no import" (F3); "a pending written during the loop's last look is made in the same run" (F9); "an episode from before the switch is finished as today" (L19, and L8's own tests).

**World changes** (written first; no assertion of their own):
- How the new tests reach L5's new names: a test file's static named import of a name its module does not export yet fails the whole file at load, its nets and its old tests with it. So every new test, and every helper a test imports (`checks-world.js`, `local-world.js`), reaches a name L5 adds through a namespace import of its module (`import * as filing from "../filing.js"`, then `filing.readPendingAt(…)`; the same for `dropbox.js`, `finish-check.js`, `late-wait.js` and `edit.js`), never a named one; a name that is there today (`writePending`, `takeLateCheckLock`, `importEpisodes`, `readPendings`, `markChecking`, …) may be imported by name. Each new test then fails alone, by a missing export (`filing.readPendingAt is not a function`), and every file still loads.
- `tool/tests/episodes.js` `setup()`: ruling 27.
- `tool/tests/checks-world.js` (create), the loop's world for the unit tests, nothing of edit.js in it:
  - Constants: `MAC = "Mac Studio"`, `ID = "e-20261106-ab0001"`, `NAME = "2026-11-06 Talk (Mac Studio)"`, `LABEL = "Video of 6 Nov, 10:00 (Mac Studio)"`, `L = Date.parse("2026-11-06T10:00:00-06:00")`, `mine = (pid) => pid === process.pid`, and the texts `MP4 = "0123456789"` (10 bytes), `SRT = "1\nHi.\n\n"` (7), `HANDOFF = '{"revision": 1}\n'` (16).
  - `world()`: a temporary `dir` with `home` (`<dir>/home`), `root` (`<dir>/BRIMM Video`, with `Episodes/`), and `drop` (`<dir>/Drop`, empty); `statuses()` (`readStatusesIn([importingFolder(root)]).map(({ status }) => status)`, as `filing.test.js`' `world` reads them, 249: with no `episode.statuses` or `statusFolders` in its deps, the loop's statuses are in `Episodes/.importing`, filing.js 409-412); `done()`.
  - `runs(w, at, over = {})`: `{ deps, logs, notices, waits, now }`, `deps` = `{ episode: { root: w.root, machine: MAC, home: w.home }, clock: () => clock, wait: async (ms) => { waits.push(ms); clock += ms; }, clone: <copies bytes and times, and throws when a file is at its target, as episodes.js' clone does>, sameDisk: () => true, keepAwake: () => {}, log: (line) => logs.push(line), notify: (text) => notices.push(text), alive: mine, ...over }`, the clock starting at `at`. (L3's `importEpisodes` needs nothing more.)
  - `run(w, at, over)`: `await importEpisodes(w.drop, deps, async () => assert.fail("nothing is waiting"))` (or `over.importOne`), giving `{ logs, notices, waits, results, now }`.
  - `liveLock(w, name, command)`: `takeLateCheckLock(w.home, name, command, mine)`. `deadLock(w, name, command = "write")`: `late-check.lock` of `name` written as `{ pid: 999999, command, nonce: "dead" }`.
  - `ctxOf(w, over = {})`: a command's ctx for `takeOutOfCheck`: `{ home: w.home, root: w.root, machine: MAC, alive: mine, now: () => L, isWhole: () => true, deps: { clone: <runs()' clone>, sameDisk: () => true }, ...over }`.
  - `recordOf({ id = ID, rev, over = {} })`: `{ ...newEpisode({ id, machine: MAC, at: "2026-11-06T09:00:00-06:00", video: { file: "Talk.mov", bytes: 10, seconds: 120, created: null }, kind: "episode", writer: { commit: "abc1234", on: MAC }, local: { on: MAC } }), name: { text: "Talk", placeholder: false }, files: { "Talk.mov": 10 }, rev, version: LOCAL_VERSION, ...over }` (L2's `faultFor` reads a whole `local` at layout 6).
  - `holdImport(w, video, lastCopy)`: `filing.test.js`' `holdCheck`, copied (an import pending of a whole file).
  - `holdRecord(w, at, { id = ID, name = NAME, copied = true, written = false, there = "ours", later = false, workCopy = true, drop = "gone", video = true, problem })`: `Episodes/<name>/record/` with `0001.json` as `there` says ("ours": `recordText(recordOf({ id, rev: 1 }))`; "another": the same of id `e-20261106-cd0002`; "unreadable": `{ half`; "short": the first 20 bytes of the "ours" text, which does not parse and begins as the work copy does; "none": no file), `0002.json` of the same id when `later`, the work copy `<dir>/work/import-<id>/record/1-0001.json` (the "ours" text) unless `workCopy` is false, when `written` this Mac's note of rev 1 of `id` (`acceptRev(w.home, id, 1)`, as `writeEpisode` notes it once its clone has returned), when `video` the episode's `video/` (`episodeFilesFolder(w.home, name)`, holding `.import.json`), the drop file as `drop` says, at `dropPath` = `<dir>/dropped/Talk.mov` (a folder of its own, not the loop's `drop`, which a file there would make the run import: the check asks only what the file is) ("there": holding `MP4`, its time set to `L - 60000`, and `dropFile` its `fileId`; "gone": no such file, and `dropFile` `{ ino: 1, size: 10, mtimeMs: L - 60000 }`; "another": a file there holding `"other text!"` (11 bytes, not `MP4`'s 10), its time left as written, with that same `dropFile`; "unknown": neither `drop` nor `dropFile` in the pending), and `pending/record/<id>.json` = `{ version: 1, kind: "record", id, name, folder, root: w.root, machine: MAC, label: LABEL, drop: dropPath, dropFile, entries: [{ from, to, bytes }], work: <dir>/work/import-<id>, lastCopy: at, …(copied ? { copied: true } : {}), …(problem ? { problem } : {}) }`. Gives `{ pending, key, folder, to, from, work, dropPath }`.
  - `holdFinal(w, at, { id = ID, name = NAME, revision = 1, copied = true, recordWritten = true, workCopies = true, exported = true, mark = null, problem, episode = true })`: when `episode`, `Episodes/<name>/` with `record/0001.json` (`recordOf({ id, rev: 1 })`) and, when `recordWritten`, `record/0002.json` (`recordOf({ id, rev: 2, over: { files: { "Talk.mov": 10, "final/Talk main.mp4": 10, "final/Talk main.srt": 7, "final/Talk.brimm-ops.json": 16 }, finished: { revision, at: "2026-11-06T09:59:00-06:00", on: MAC, handoff: "final/Talk.brimm-ops.json", parts: { main: { v: 0, file: "final/Talk main.mp4", bytes: 10, seconds: 12, source: { bytes: 10, mtimeMs: M }, project: "Talk main" } } } } })`), and `final/` holding `MP4`, `SRT` and `HANDOFF` under those names; when `exported`, `<home>/Downloads/Talk main.mp4` holding `MP4`, its time set to `2026-11-06T09:50:00-06:00`, and `M` its `statSync` `mtimeMs`; the work copies of the three files and of `record/0002.json` in `<dir>/work/final-<id>-<revision>/` (`r/` for the record's) unless `workCopies` is false; and `pending/final/<id>-<revision>.json` = `{ version: 1, kind: "final", id, name, folder, root: w.root, machine: MAC, revision, rev: 2, entries: <the three>, took: exported ? [{ path, snap: { size: 10, mtimeMs: M }, name: "Talk main.mp4", place: "Downloads", key: "main" }] : [], work, lastCopy: at, record: { from, to: <folder>/record/0002.json, bytes }, …(copied ? { copied: true } : {}), …(mark ? { reading: mark } : {}), …(problem ? { problem } : {}) }`. With `episode: false` only the pending and its work copies are written, for an episode already there. Gives `{ pending, key, folder, files: { mp4, srt, handoff }, work, exportPath, M, later(change) }`, where `later(change)` reads the highest-numbered record file `n`, and writes `record/<n + 1>.json` as that record with the fields `change(record)` gives laid over it and `rev: n + 1`.

**`tool/tests/checks-behind.test.js`** (create; `checks-world.js`).

describe "pendings by kind, and the loop (L5)":
- F1 "keeps a pending of each kind in a folder of its own, named by its key, and reads one kind at a time": `writePending` of an import pending (no `kind`), a `record`, two `final` (revisions 1 and 2) and an `original` (`work: null`, `entries: []`) of `ID` → the files `pending/<ID>.json`, `pending/record/<ID>.json`, `pending/final/<ID>-1.json`, `pending/final/<ID>-2.json`, `pending/original/<ID>.json`; `readPendings(home)` is the import's alone; `readPendingsOf(home, "final")` the two, oldest `lastCopy` first; `readPendingAt(home, "final/<ID>-2.json")` the second; `readEveryPending(home)` all five, oldest first; a file `final/<ID>-3.json` holding `{ half`, and a whole `final` pending of `ID` whose `revision` is 4 written at `final/<ID>-5.json` (its key is not its file's), are left out of every reader; no file ends `.writing`.
- F2 "dueAt takes the later of a pending's due and its nextLook, is due at once when owed, counts a kind's 15 minutes from a later wake, and pastLookUntil and isOpen say what the loop waits for": `dueAt({ lastCopy: L })` (an import's) is `L + LATE_CHECK_MS`, and so is `dueAt({ lastCopy: L }, L + 60000)`; `dueAt({ kind: "final", lastCopy: L, nextLook: L + 5 })` is `L + LATE_CHECK_MS` (an earlier `nextLook` brings nothing forward), and with `nextLook: L + LATE_CHECK_MS + 5`, that; `dueAt({ kind: "original", owed: true, lastCopy: L })` is `L`, and with `nextLook: L + 5`, `L + 5`; `dueAt({ kind: "final", lastCopy: L }, L + 60000)` is `L + 60000 + LATE_CHECK_MS`, with `L - 60000` or null `L + LATE_CHECK_MS`, and `dueAt({ kind: "original", owed: true, lastCopy: L }, L + 60000)` is `L`; `pastLookUntil({ lookUntil: L }, L + 1)` true, at `L` false, of `{}` false; `isOpen` false with a `problem`, and past `lookUntil`, and true with `told: L` and `wasProblem: "x"` alone; `WAIT_LOOK_MS` is 300000 and `WAIT_LOOK_FOR_MS` 3600000.
- F3 "clearRuns keeps what a pending of any kind names and skips a work of null, so no import is stopped": `.runs/` holding `finish-99999998-1`, `99999997-2` and `${process.pid}-3`; a `final` pending naming `finish-99999998-1`; an `original` pending with `work: null` → `clearRuns(home, mine)` throws nothing, leaves `finish-99999998-1` and `${process.pid}-3`, removes `99999997-2`; then `run` of a drop folder holding `B.mov` calls `importOne("B.mov")`.
- F4 "makes a record's and a final's checks of one episode in one run": `holdRecord` and `holdFinal` of `ID`, both due → one `run` → both pending files gone, both `work` folders gone, no `late-check.lock` left, and the log has the record's whole line and the final's passed line.
- F5 "makes an import's, a record's and a final's checks due at once, each under its own episode's lock": `holdImport(w, "X.mov", L)`, `holdRecord`, and `holdFinal` with `id` `e-20261106-ab0002` and `name` `2026-11-06 Other (Mac Studio)`, all due → one `run` → all three pending files gone; no `late-check.lock` in any of the three work folders; `statuses()` is [].
- F6 "writes no import status for a pending of another kind": `holdFinal`, the clock 10 minutes after its `lastCopy`, `deps.checks.final` a stand-in that removes the pending; the `wait` reads `statuses()` at each wait and moves the clock by what it is asked → every read is []; the stand-in is called once, at or after `dueAt`; the run returns.
- F7 "looks again in the same run at a pending given a nextLook, lets the run end past its lookUntil, and looks at it once at the next run's start": an `original` pending (`work: null`, `entries: []`, `lastCopy: L - LATE_CHECK_MS`) and `deps.checks.original` a stand-in that counts its calls and writes the pending again with `nextLook: now + 10 minutes` and, the first time, `lookUntil: now + 60 minutes` → one run: 7 calls (at 0, 10, …, 60 minutes), every `wait` above 0, and it returns with the pending still there; a second run at the first's start + 61 minutes: called once, and it returns.
- F8 "leaves alone a pending of a kind with no check": `pending/original/<ID>.json` and no `deps.checks` → the run returns with no wait; the file is as it was; no log line names it.
- F9 "a pending written during the loop's last look is made in the same run": the drop folder holds `B.mov`; `importOne` gives `{ ok: false, name, arriving: true }` the first time, and the second time (the last look) writes a `final` pending of another episode (`lastCopy` the clock's now) and gives `{ ok: true, name }` with no `filed`; `deps.checks.final` a stand-in that removes the pending → the run waits until it is due, the stand-in is called once, and only then the run returns; `importOne` was called twice. (Without ruling 6's read the run would end after the last look.)
- F10 "a check that moves its own time on is looked at again in the same run, whatever nextLook it kept": an `original` pending due now that carries an old `nextLook` (a minute before now), and `deps.checks.original` a stand-in that the first time writes it again with `lastCopy: now`, as L6's move does, keeping that `nextLook`, and the second time removes it → called twice, the second at least `LATE_CHECK_MS` after the first, and the run then returns.
- F11 "clearRuns asks which runs are not running before it reads the pendings": `.runs/finish-99999998-1`; the `alive` stand-in, asked of 99999998, writes a `final` pending naming that folder and answers false → the folder is kept.
- F12 "makes two final pendings of one episode, one after the other, in one run": `holdFinal(w, L)` and `holdFinal(w, L + 1000, { revision: 2, episode: false })` → both pending files and both `work` folders gone; the export deleted; no `late-check.lock` left.
- F13 "counts a kind's 15 minutes from the Mac's last wake: a check that came due while the Mac slept is made 15 minutes after the wake, not at it": `holdFinal(w, L)`, `deps.checks.final` a stand-in that notes the clock and removes the pending, and `deps.wokeAt` a stand-in giving `woke`. (a) `woke` null and the run started at `L + 2` minutes; its first `wait` moves the clock 14 hours on and sets `woke` to the clock it moved to, and every later `wait` moves the clock by what it is asked → the stand-in is called once, at `woke + LATE_CHECK_MS` (every wait after the wake is `LOOK_AGAIN_MS` or less), and the run returns. (b) A run started at `L + 14` hours with `woke` a minute before it → the stand-in is called 14 minutes into the run, not at its start. (c) A run started at `L + 2` minutes with `woke` a minute before `L`, and no jump → it is called at `L + LATE_CHECK_MS`, as with no wake. (d) A run started at `L + 14` hours, 2 minutes after the Mac started, with `woke` that start (as `lastWake` gives it on a Mac that has not slept since) → it is called 13 minutes into the run, not at its start.
- F14 "lastWake gives the later of when the Mac last woke and when it last started, and is null when neither can be read": a `run` stand-in that answers by its `args[1]`. `kern.waketime` `{ status: 0, stdout: "{ sec = 1793966294, usec = 876198 } Fri Nov  6 06:58:14 2026\n" }` and `kern.boottime` `{ status: 0, stdout: "{ sec = 1793900000, usec = 5 } Thu Nov  5 12:33:20 2026\n" }` → `1793966294000`; `kern.waketime` `{ status: 0, stdout: "{ sec = 0, usec = 0 } Thu Jan  1 00:00:00 1970\n" }` and the same boot → `1793900000000`; `kern.waketime` `{ status: 1, stdout: "" }` and the same boot → `1793900000000`; the same wake and `kern.boottime` throwing → `1793966294000`; each of `{ status: 1, stdout: "" }`, `{ status: 0, stdout: "?" }` and a throw for both → null. The stand-in is asked with `"/usr/sbin/sysctl"` and `["-n", "kern.waketime"]`, and with `["-n", "kern.boottime"]`.
- F16 "makes a pending written again under a key it has made in the run": a `final` pending due at the run's start, `deps.checks.final` a stand-in that notes the clock and removes the pending, and the drop folder holding `B.mov`, whose `importOne` writes a `final` pending at the same key with `lastCopy` the clock's now and gives `{ ok: true, name }` → the stand-in is called twice, the second time at or after that `lastCopy + LATE_CHECK_MS`; the run then returns. (With `checked` by key alone it would be called once.)
- F17 "a pending past its lookUntil that a wake keeps from being due at a run's start is looked at once in that run, when it is due, and the run waits for it": an `original` pending (`work: null`, `entries: []`, `lastCopy: L`, `lookUntil: L + 60` minutes, `nextLook: L + 70` minutes), `deps.checks.original` a stand-in that notes the clock and writes it again with `nextLook` the clock + 10 minutes, as L6's look past its hour does, `deps.wokeAt` giving a minute before the run's start, and the run started at `L + 14` hours → the stand-in is called once, 14 minutes into the run (not at its start), every `wait` is above 0, and the run then returns with the pending there. With `wokeAt` giving null, it is called once, at the run's start (row 1).
- F18 "a pending written over the key of one the run chose, while the run made another's check, is made at its own due, not at once": two `final` pendings due at the run's start, oldest first: X (`id` `e-20261106-ab0002`, `name` `2026-11-06 Other (Mac Studio)`, `lastCopy: L`) and D (`ID`, revision 2, `lastCopy: L + 1000`); `deps.checks.final` a stand-in that notes each call's key and clock, removes the pending it is given, and, when given X, first writes a `final` pending at D's key (`final/<ID>-2.json`) with `lastCopy` the clock's now (as a finish given revision 2 again after W3 writes its own over the dead one's) → the stand-in is called for X once, at the run's start, and for D's key once, at or after that new `lastCopy + LATE_CHECK_MS` (never at the run's start); the run waits for it and then returns. (Without step 1's test of the pending read again, D's key would be checked at once, seconds after its copies.)

describe "a local import's first record, checked behind (L5)", `holdRecord`, the run at `L + LATE_CHECK_MS + 1`:
- R1 "puts back a first record Dropbox lost, then goes": `copied`, `there: "none"` → `record/0001.json` is back, byte for byte the work copy; the pending and its `work` are gone; the log has `Video of 6 Nov, 10:00 (Mac Studio): Dropbox lost its first record, so it is put back.`
- R2 "puts nothing back when a later record is there": `copied`, `there: "none"`, `later` → `0001.json` is still not there, `0002.json` is as it was; the pending is gone; the later-record line.
- R3 "drops a pending whose record never reached Dropbox": not `copied`, `there: "none"`, `drop: "there"` → the pending and `work` are gone; `record/` holds nothing; the stopped line; nothing else is written in the episode's folder; the drop file is as it was. (b) The same with `drop: "unknown"` (`copied` and this Mac's note alone decide).
- R4 "takes a record that reached Dropbox before the import noted it as its own": not `copied`, `there: "ours"` → the pending is gone; `0001.json` is as it was; the whole line.
- R5 "never puts back over another import's first record": `copied`, `there: "another"` → `0001.json` is as it was; the pending is gone; the another line.
- R5b "drops the pending of a stopped import whose folder another import has filed since": not `copied`, `there: "another"`, `drop: "there"` → `0001.json` is as it was; the pending is gone; the stopped line (not the another line).
- R6 "puts back a first record Dropbox left unreadable": `copied`, `there: "unreadable"` → `0001.json` is the work copy's bytes.
- R7 "a first record that cannot be put back is a problem: one notice, made again at the next run's start": `copied`, `there: "none"`, `workCopy: false` → first run: `notices` is `["Dropbox lost a file of Video of 6 Nov, 10:00 (Mac Studio), and it could not be put back. Ask Jason."]`, the pending has `problem`, the run returns with no wait; the log has `Video of 6 Nov, 10:00 (Mac Studio): Dropbox lost its first record: its first record is not in Dropbox, and its copy on this Mac is gone, so it could not be put back.`; second run: that line again, and `notices` still has one.
- R8 "waits when its folder cannot be looked at, looking again every five minutes for an hour": `copied`; the episode's `record/` made unsearchable (`chmod 0o000`, put back in `finally`) → the pending stays, its fields as they were but `nextLook` and `lookUntil` (the run's start + `WAIT_LOOK_FOR_MS`); its `work` is still there; the run makes the check again every `WAIT_LOOK_MS` until `lookUntil` is past, and then returns; the cannot-tell line once. A second run started after that: made once, at its start; the line once.
- R9 "makes a lost first record's folder again only while this Mac holds the episode's video, and waits while Episodes cannot be listed": (a) `copied`; `Episodes/<NAME>` removed (as when Dropbox undoes the import's making of it) → `Episodes/<NAME>/record/0001.json` is there, byte for byte the work copy; the pending and `work` are gone; the put-back line; no notice. (b) The same with `video: false` → the pending and `work` are gone; `Episodes/<NAME>` is still not there; `notices` is `["The folder of Video of 6 Nov, 10:00 (Mac Studio) is gone from Episodes, so the check of its copies in Dropbox is not made. Ask Jason."]`, and the log has that line once. (c) `copied`; `Episodes` itself renamed away (put back in `finally`), as when Dropbox's folder is away a moment → the pending stays, with `nextLook` and `lookUntil`; its `work` is still there; the cannot-tell line once; no notice; nothing is made at `Episodes/<NAME>`. (d) As (c), with the test's `wait` renaming `Episodes` back at the run's first wait after the check: the next look, `WAIT_LOOK_MS` on, finds `0001.json` gone and puts it back; the pending and `work` are gone; the run then returns. (e) Not `copied`, not `written`, `there: "none"`, `video: false` and `Episodes/<NAME>` removed (what a failed import whose pending could not be taken away leaves: ruling 9) → the pending and `work` are gone; the stopped line; no notice; `Episodes/<NAME>` is still not there.
- R10 "takes a placeholder first record as in Dropbox, and does not read it": `copied`, `there: "unreadable"`, `deps.isWhole` false for `0001.json` → nothing is written; the pending is gone; the whole line.
- R11 "puts back a first record whose import stopped before it noted it, by this Mac's note that it wrote it": not `copied`, `written`, `there: "none"`, `drop: "gone"` → `0001.json` is back, byte for byte the work copy; the pending and `work` are gone; the put-back line. The same with `drop: "another"` (a file of another size in its place).
- R12 "puts nothing back while its video is still in the drop folder, for the next look to import it again": `copied`, `there: "none"`, `drop: "there"` → `0001.json` is still not there; the pending and `work` are gone; the drop file is as it was; the again line. The same with the drop file's inode changed: `rmSync` of the drop file, then `writeFileSync` of `MP4` and `utimesSync` to `L - 60000` at its path, and `statSync(dropPath).ino` asserted to differ from `dropFile.ino` before the run: the again line.
- R13 "drops a pending whose import was stopped before its clone, though its video has left the drop folder": not `copied`, not `written`, `there: "none"`, `drop: "gone"` (she took it out by hand) → the pending and `work` are gone; `record/` holds nothing; the stopped line. (The drop file is no witness: ruling 0.)
- R14 "replaces a garbled first record its stopped import wrote, which the re-import could never write over, and waits on one it cannot tell": (a) not `copied`, `written`, `there: "unreadable"`, `drop: "there"` → `0001.json` is the work copy's bytes; the pending and `work` are gone; the put-back line; the drop file is as it was. (b) Not `copied`, not `written`, `there: "short"`, `drop: "there"` → the same (it begins as the work copy). (c) Not `copied`, not `written`, `there: "unreadable"` (`{ half`, which does not), `drop: "there"` → `0001.json` is as it was; the pending stays with `nextLook` and `lookUntil`, and its `work`; the whose line once.

describe "the check of a local finish's copies in final/ (L5)", `holdFinal`, the run at `L + LATE_CHECK_MS + 1`:
- C1 "the due check puts back a missing copy, then deletes the export": `final/Talk main.srt` removed; `markChecking(home, NAME, { nonce: "", revision: 1, due: L + LATE_CHECK_MS, took: ["Downloads"] })` before the run → it is back, `SRT`; the export is gone from Downloads; the pending, its `work` and the marker are gone; the put-back line, the deleted line and the passed line.
- C2 "leaves a file the record in force lists at another size": `later((r) => ({ files: { ...r.files, "final/Talk.brimm-ops.json": 21 }, finished: { ...r.finished, revision: 2 } }))` and the hand-off written as 21 other bytes → the hand-off is those 21 bytes; the other two checked; the left line (1 file); the export deleted (its part is kept).
- C3 "leaves a file the record in force no longer lists": `later((r) => ({ files: <r.files without "final/Talk main.srt"> }))`, and the file removed → it is still not there.
- C4 "deletes the Downloads export only when every copy is whole and it is unchanged": (a) `workCopies: false` and `final/Talk main.srt` removed: a problem; the export is still in Downloads; the kept-exports line. (b) the export's time set a second later: kept; `Talk: Talk main.mp4 changed in Downloads after it was taken, so it is kept there.` (c) everything as made: deleted.
- C5 "deletes the export after a later revision that keeps its part, and keeps it after one that took the part again": `later((r) => ({ finished: { ...r.finished, revision: 2 } }))` → deleted. `later((r) => ({ finished: { ...r.finished, revision: 2, parts: { main: { ...r.finished.parts.main, source: { bytes: 10, mtimeMs: M + 1000 } } } } }))` → kept; the no-longer-holds line.
- C6 "never deletes an original that was not taken from Downloads": the pending's `took[0].place` written as "the exports folder" → kept; the not-from-Downloads line.
- C6b "keeps an export it cannot look at, and still completes": the test's Downloads made unsearchable (`chmod 0o000`, put back in `finally`) → the copies are checked; the export is kept; the could-not line; the pending is gone.
- C7 "flags a problem as today": `workCopies: false`, `final/Talk main.mp4` removed → first run: `notices` is `['The check of Talk\'s copies in Dropbox found a problem: say "how far is it?"']`, `problemFlagged(home, NAME)` is true, the pending has `problem` (`Talk main.mp4 in final/ is not whole in Dropbox (it is not there), and its copy on this Mac is gone, so it could not be put back.`, with no path of the test's folder in it) and its `work`; the run returns with no wait. Second run: no second notice; the problem logged again.
- C8 "takes the note away once a check of the episode passes and no other has a problem": `holdFinal` (revision 1) with `problem: "x"` and `workCopies: false`, `final/Talk main.srt` removed, and `flagProblem(home, NAME, L)`; then `holdFinal(w, L + 1000, { revision: 2, episode: false })`, with work copies of its own. First run: revision 1 (the older) finds a problem (its copy of the `.srt` is gone) and is kept; revision 2 puts the `.srt` back from its own copy and passes; no notice is posted (revision 1 had a `problem` already), and the note stays. The next run's start: revision 1 passes (the `.srt` is there at its size, and its copy is gone: J5) and the note is gone.
- C9 "drops a pending for a revision the record never held": not `copied`, `recordWritten: false`, `final/Talk main.srt` removed → the pending and `work` are gone; `final/Talk main.srt` is still not there; the export is still in Downloads; the stopped line.
- C10 "a finish killed after its record but before it noted it still has its check made": not `copied`, `recordWritten` true, `final/Talk main.srt` removed → it is put back; the export is deleted.
- C11 "waits when the record cannot be read, looking again every five minutes for an hour": a conflicted copy `record/0002 (Sallie's laptop's conflicted copy 2026-11-06).json` → the pending stays as it was but for `nextLook` and `lookUntil`; the check is made again every `WAIT_LOOK_MS` until `lookUntil` is past, and the run then returns; the later line once. (b) The same, with the test's `wait` removing the conflicted copy at the run's second wait after the check (as `resolve` sorts it out): the next look makes the check, which passes; the pending and the export are gone; the run then returns.
- C12 "puts the finish's own record back when it is gone and no later record is there, and then judges by it": `copied`; `record/0002.json` and `final/Talk main.srt` removed → both are back, the record byte for byte its work copy; the record-put-back line and the put-back line.
- C13 "never puts the finish's record back over a record of that number, and calls the number taken a problem": `copied`; `record/0002.json` written again as another whole record of rev 2, `finished.revision: 5` → `record/0002.json` is as written; no record-put-back line; `notices` is `['The check of Talk\'s copies in Dropbox found a problem: say "how far is it?"']`; `problemFlagged(home, NAME)` is true; the pending stays with its `work` and `problem` `its record 0002.json was lost in Dropbox, and a later change took its number.`; the export is still in Downloads; the kept-exports line; no passed line. (b) The same with `workCopies: false` (its record's work copy gone too): the same problem, as the record at 0002 is not of revision 1. (c) With `record/0002.json` written again as the finish's own record's text (the same bytes): no problem; the check passes.
- C14 "waits when it cannot tell whether its record was written": not `copied`; `record/0002.json` holding `{ half` → the pending stays, with `nextLook` and `lookUntil`; the cannot-tell line once; the run returns once `lookUntil` is past.
- C15 "puts nothing back for a video the gate holds, and deletes no export, and no line names it by its name as a video": `later(() => ({ session: { marked: "2026-11-06T10:05:00-06:00", on: MAC, teachings: null, cleared: null } }))`, `final/Talk main.srt` removed, and a live "write" lock (`liveLock`) that the first `LOCK_AGAIN_MS` wait lets go → it is still not there; the export is still in Downloads; the pending and `work` are gone; the log has `an episode (ab0001): its check waits a moment: a write is putting its files in.` and `a client or group session (ab0001): it is held as a session now, so nothing of its hand-off is put back into Dropbox, and its exports stay in Downloads.`, and no line holds `Talk`.
- C15b "so for a video held beside a session": another episode, `Episodes/2026-11-06 Talk again (Mac Studio)/record/0001.json`, `recordOf({ id: "e-20261106-cd0002", rev: 1, over: { versionOf: ID, session: { marked: "2026-11-06T10:05:00-06:00", on: MAC, teachings: null, cleared: null } } })`, and `final/Talk main.srt` removed → the `.srt` is still not there; the export is still in Downloads; the pending stays, as it was but for `lookUntil` (the run's clock), with its `work`, and the run returns with no wait for it; the log has `a video (ab0001): another version of it is a session not cleared, so nothing of its hand-off is put back into Dropbox yet, and its exports stay in Downloads.` once, and no line holds `Talk`. A second run: the same, the line once more.
- C26 "makes the check of a video once the session beside it is cleared": after C15b's two runs, the other episode's record written again as `record/0002.json` with its `session.cleared` `{ confirmed: "dialog" }` → the next run puts the `.srt` back (`SRT`), deletes the export from Downloads, and puts the pending and its `work` away; the passed line.
- C16 "never makes a gone episode folder again, says so in one short notice, and waits while Episodes cannot be listed": `Episodes/<NAME>` removed → it is still not there; the export is still in Downloads; the pending and `work` are gone; `notices` is `["The folder of an episode (ab0001) is gone from Episodes, so the check of its copies in Dropbox is not made. Ask Jason."]`, and the log has that line once and no line that holds `Talk`. (b) `Episodes` itself renamed away (put back in `finally`) → the pending stays, with its `work`, `nextLook` and `lookUntil`; the export is still in Downloads; the folder-unreadable line once; no notice; nothing is made at `Episodes/<NAME>`; the run returns once `lookUntil` is past. (c) As (a), with `pending/final/` made read-only (`chmod 0o555`, put back in `finally`), so the pending cannot be removed → the not-put-away line; no notice; the pending is still there; the next run, with the folder writable again: put away, and the one notice then.
- C17 "a check stopped after it took the export out and before it put its pending away is made again": the export removed by the test, the pending and the marker as they were → the next run finds every copy whole, says no line of the export, and puts the pending, its `work` and the marker away.
- C18 "puts back a copy of its size whose bytes are not the ones written": `final/Talk main.srt` written as `"1\nHo.\n\n"` (7 bytes) → it is `SRT` again; the put-back line says `its bytes are not the ones written`.
- C19 "takes a placeholder copy at its size as whole, and does not read it": `final/Talk main.srt` written as `"1\nHo.\n\n"` and `deps.isWhole` false for it → it is left as it is; the check passes; the export is deleted.
- C20 "takes a copy at its size whose work copy is gone as whole": `workCopies: false` → the check passes; the export is deleted.
- C21 "waits when a folder of the episode cannot be searched": the episode's `final/` made unsearchable (`chmod 0o000`, put back in `finally`; `readEpisode` then gives UNREADABLE) → the pending stays as it was but for `nextLook` and `lookUntil`; the later line once; nothing is put back; the run returns once `lookUntil` is past.
- C22 "a later hand-off that takes the lost copy out of a check with a problem reopens it, and both are made in the run": C7's state, and `holdRecord` of another episode (`e-20261106-ab0002`, `2026-11-06 Other (Mac Studio)`, `lastCopy` 30 minutes after revision 1's) to keep the run open → at its start revision 1 finds the problem (one notice; `problemFlagged` true); at the run's first wait the stand-in does what a second hand-off of the main video does: `takeOutOfCheck(ctxOf(w), { name: NAME, record: <record 2>, paths: [<the .mp4>], rev: 3 }, …)`, `end({ put: true })` (which gives `{ reopened: true }`; revision 1 then has `told`, `wasProblem` its problem, no `problem` and no entry of the `.mp4`), and `holdFinal(w, <the clock>, { revision: 2, episode: false })` (its own copy of the `.mp4`, which its check puts back) → revision 1 is made again in this run (its `told` makes it another to `checked`), passes, and goes, with no second notice; when the run returns: `problemFlagged(home, NAME)` is false; one notice; both `final` pendings are gone. (b) The same with revision 1's pending also carrying `lookUntil: L` (an hour of looks gone before its problem was found): it is still made again in this run (the reopening took its `lookUntil` away; kept, row 7 would leave it to the next run's start).
- C23 "flags a problem with the launch agent's deps, which carry no clock and no alive": `holdFinal` with `lastCopy` 20 minutes before the real clock, `workCopies: false` and `final/Talk main.mp4` removed; `run` with `clock` and `alive` left out (undefined, as `depsFor` gives them) and `wokeAt: () => null` → `problemFlagged(home, NAME)` is true; one notice; no (threw) line.
- C24 "drops a pending whose record's number holds another writer's record of its revision": not `copied`, `recordWritten: false`, `final/Talk main.srt` removed, and `record/0002.json` written as another whole record of `ID` (rev 2, `finished.revision: 1`, `name: { text: "Talk too", placeholder: false }`), as a writer given revision 1 again after W3 writes it → the pending and `work` are gone; the `.srt` is still not there; the export is still in Downloads; the stopped line. (By id and revision alone it would be taken as held.)
- C25 "keeps the export, as no longer held, when the record in force has no finish": `later(() => ({ finished: null }))`, as a correction leaves it → every copy is checked and whole; the export is still in Downloads; `Talk: the hand-off in force no longer holds Talk main.mp4, so it is left in Downloads.`; no could-not line; the pending is gone.
- C27 "takes the note away before it removes the pending, so a check that cannot remove it leaves no note behind": `holdFinal` with `problem: "x"` and every copy whole, `flagProblem(home, NAME, L)`, and `pending/final/` made read-only (`chmod 0o555`, put back in `finally`), so that removing the pending throws → the check passes; `problemFlagged(home, NAME)` is false; the marker is gone; the pending is still there; the not-put-away line once (`Talk: its check is made, but it could not be put away (EACCES). The next run looks at it again.`).

describe "a writer and the check, in each order (L5)", `holdFinal`, `liveLock`, `deadLock`:
- W1 "puts off a due check while a writer holds its lock, and makes it once it is let go, leaving the writer's file": `liveLock(w, NAME, "write")`; the second `LOCK_AGAIN_MS` wait writes `final/Talk.brimm-ops.json` as 21 other bytes, `later((r) => ({ files: { ...r.files, "final/Talk.brimm-ops.json": 21 } }))`, the pending written without the hand-off's entry, and lets the lock go → the hand-off is the writer's; the other two checked; the waits are `[LOCK_AGAIN_MS, LOCK_AGAIN_MS]`; `an episode (ab0001): its check waits a moment: a write is putting its files in.` once.
- W2 "settles a dead writer's mark by its record's number: a writer whose record was written keeps its files": `mark: { pid: 999999, at: L, to: [<hand-off>], nonce: "n", rev: 3 }`, the hand-off written as 21 other bytes, `later((r) => ({ files: { ...r.files, "final/Talk.brimm-ops.json": 21 } }))` (record 3), `deadLock(w, NAME)` → the hand-off is left; the rest checked; `an episode (ab0001): a write stopped while it put its files in. Its check leaves out 1 file the record says is that write's.`; no lock left.
- W2b "…and so when its file is of the same size": as W2, with the hand-off written as 16 other bytes and `later((r) => ({ name: { text: "Talk", placeholder: false } }))` (record 3, listing it at 16) → the hand-off is left as the writer's (it is not put back).
- W3 "keeps every marked entry when the writer's record was not written, and puts the finish's file back over the stopped write's": as W2 with no `later` (the hand-off at 21 bytes, record 2 listing it at 16) → the hand-off is `HANDOFF` again.
- W3b "…and so when the stopped write is of the same size": as W2b with no `later` → the hand-off is `HANDOFF` again (by its bytes).
- W4 "leaves a dead writer's mark, and waits, when the record cannot be read": W2's state with a conflicted copy in `record/` → the mark stays, and the pending has `nextLook` and `lookUntil`; the unsettled line once; the run returns once `lookUntil` is past.
- W5 "takes over a lock a dead check left, and makes the check": `deadLock(w, NAME, "late check")` → the check is made; no lock left.
- W6 "puts off, at the per-minute timer, a check a writer's lock holds, and throws nothing": modelled on filing.test.js test 14: the drop folder holds `B.mov`; `holdFinal` due during its import, with `final/Talk main.srt` removed; a live "write" lock (`liveLock`); `importOne` moves the clock past the check's due, calls the timer's `tick()` (which must not throw), and asserts that the pending is still there and the `.srt` still not there; the run's `wait` lets the lock go at its first call → once the run returns, the `.srt` is back and the pending is gone.
- W7 "a check of a new kind that throws is said by its code, left for the next run, and stops nothing else": `holdFinal`, `holdRecord` of another episode (`e-20261106-ab0002`, `2026-11-06 Other (Mac Studio)`, its first record lost), both due, and `deps.checks.final` a stand-in that throws `Object.assign(new Error("x"), { code: "EACCES" })` → the run returns; the log has `an episode (ab0001): its check could not be made (EACCES). The next run makes it again.` once; the `final` pending stays as it was; the other episode's first record is put back; no lock left.
- W8 "pins ruling 20's cost: after a stopped writer, a record-only write that took its number leaves the writer's files unchecked": W3's state, then `later((r) => ({ name: { text: "Talk too", placeholder: false } }))` (record 3, a name change only, listing the hand-off at 16) → the hand-off is left as the stopped writer's 21 bytes; the settled line.
- W9 "puts off a check while a live finish run holds the episode's lock, and makes it once the run ends": `episode.lock` of `NAME` taken by this process as "finish" (`takeEpisodeLock(lockOf(home, NAME), "finish", mine)`), let go at the second `LOCK_AGAIN_MS` wait; the first wait finds no `late-check.lock` and the export still in Downloads → the waits are `[LOCK_AGAIN_MS, LOCK_AGAIN_MS]`; `an episode (ab0001): its check waits until the finish at work on it ends.` once; then the check is made: the export is deleted and the pending is gone. A lock of a "finish" whose process is not running puts nothing off.
- W9b "puts off a check for a minute after her yes to a finish whose run has not taken its lock yet": `join(episodeWork(home, NAME), "finish.asked.json")` written as `{ version: 1, at: localStamp(new Date(<the run's start> - 30000)), rev: 2, parts: [] }`, no `episode.lock`, no result → the check is put off (the finish-at-work line once) until the clock is a minute past `at`, then made: the export is deleted. With a `finish.result.json` whose `started` is after `at` (that run has ended): not put off. With `at` two minutes before the run's start: not put off.
- W10 "asks again under its lock before it deletes an export, when her yes came while it read the copies": `final/Talk main.srt` removed; `deps.isWhole` a stand-in that answers true and, the first time it is asked, writes W9b's `finish.asked.json` with `at` the clock's now → the first look puts the `.srt` back and deletes nothing: the export is still in Downloads, the pending is still there with `nextLook` `LOCK_AGAIN_MS` on, no lock is left, and `Talk: its check waits until the finish at work on it ends.` is said once; later looks are put off before the lock while the minute lasts, `an episode (ab0001): its check waits until the finish at work on it ends.` said once; once it has passed, the check is made again (the `.srt` whole now), the export is deleted and the pending is gone.

describe "takeOutOfCheck and settleReading, for a local episode (L5)", `holdFinal`, `ctxOf`, the waiter of late-wait.test.js (copied in):
- T1 "always takes the lock, with no pending owed, and end lets it go": no pending → `{ end }`; the lock is held, as "write", by this process; after `end({ put: false })` it is gone.
- T2 "marks the entries among its paths in every pending of the episode, and end({ put: true }) takes them out of each": two `final` pendings of `ID` and a `record` one; `paths` `[<hand-off>]`, `rev: 3` → both `final` ones marked `{ pid, at, to: [<hand-off>], nonce, rev: 3 }`, the `record` one untouched; after `end({ put: true })` neither has the hand-off's entry nor a mark, and every other field is as it was.
- T3 "end({ put: false }) leaves the entries, unmarked; a second end changes nothing and lets no one else's lock go".
- T4 "is not stopped by a pending's problem": a `final` pending with `problem` → `{ end }`, marked.
- T5 "looks every LOCK_LOOK_MS while a check holds the lock, and is busy after LOCK_WAIT_MS, writing nothing": a live "late check" lock → `{ busy: "held" }`; the waits are `LOCK_WAIT_MS / LOCK_LOOK_MS` of `LOCK_LOOK_MS`; the pending as it was. `busyWords("held", "Talk", { done: "nothing was changed", again: "Say it again in a minute." })` is `Studio was still checking Dropbox's copies of Talk after two minutes, so nothing was changed. Say it again in a minute.`
- T6 "takes at once a lock a dead process left": no wait.
- T7 "settles an earlier dead writer's mark first, and is busy unsettled when it cannot be": W2's state → `{ end }` with the old mark settled; W4's state → `{ busy: "unsettled" }`, the old mark kept, no lock.
- T8 "end leaves alone a pending written again under its key with another mark": after the call the test writes the pending again with a mark of another `nonce` → `end({ put: true })` leaves it as written.
- T9 "for a record without local it is keepCheckInStep, with the caller's problem words": `takeOutOfCheck` of an import pending with `problem` and `problemWhy: () => "P."` → `{ why: "P." }`; with no `problemWhy`, `{ why: "Dropbox found a problem with a copy of this episode: <problem>." }`; with no pending, `{ none: true }` and nothing written (the R1c describe in late-wait.test.js is the rest of the net).
- T10 "judges the copies among its reads under the lock: one not whole is put back, and it is busy restored, marking nothing": `holdFinal`; `final/Talk main.srt` written as `"1\nHo.\n\n"` (its size, other bytes) → `takeOutOfCheck(ctxOf(w), { name: NAME, record: <record 2>, paths: [<hand-off>], reads: [{ path: <srt>, id: fileId(<srt>) }, { path: <hand-off>, id: fileId(<hand-off>) }], rev: 3 }, …)` (each id taken by the test just before the call, as a writer takes it before it reads) is `{ busy: "restored" }`; the `.srt` is `SRT` again; the pending is as it was (its `.srt` entry there, no mark); no lock left. The same with the `.srt` cut to its first 3 bytes. With every read as written: `{ end }`, the hand-off alone marked. `busyWords("restored", "Talk", { done: "nothing was changed", again: "Say it again in a minute." })` is `A copy of Talk in final/ was not whole in Dropbox, so it is put back, and nothing was changed. Say it again in a minute.`
- T11 "is busy lost, putting nothing back, when a copy among its reads is not whole and its copy on this Mac is gone": `holdFinal` with `workCopies: false`; the `.srt` cut to its first 3 bytes; T10's call, its ids taken after the cut → `{ busy: "lost" }`; the `.srt` as the test left it; no mark; no lock left. A read the record lists at another size (J1) is left, and the call gives `{ end }`. `busyWords("lost", "Talk", { done: "nothing was changed", again: "Say it again in a minute." })` is `A copy of Talk in final/ is not whole in Dropbox, and it could not be put back, so nothing was changed. Ask Jason.`
- T12 "end({ put: true }) writes a pending with a problem that loses an entry with wasProblem and told in place of its problem, and says it reopened it": a `final` pending with `problem: "x"` and `lookUntil: L - 1` (an earlier wait's hour, past); `paths: [<hand-off>]` → `end({ put: true })` gives `{ reopened: true }`; the pending has no `problem` and no `lookUntil`, `wasProblem: "x"`, `told: L` (`ctxOf`'s `now()`), and no entry of the hand-off, and `isOpen(<it>, L)` is true; a second `end` gives `{ reopened: false }`. After another call's `end({ put: false })`, a pending with `problem` keeps it, and `end` gives `{ reopened: false }`; and T2's `end({ put: true })` gives `{ reopened: false }`.
- T13 "judges its reads only by a pending whose record reached Dropbox, so a dead finish's copy is never put back": `holdFinal(w, L)` (revision 1, `copied`); then, as a finish of revision 2 that died after its puts and before its record's clone leaves it, `final/Talk.brimm-ops.json` written as `'{"revision": 2}\n'` (16 bytes, `HANDOFF`'s size) and a `final` pending of `ID`, revision 2, `rev: 3`, not `copied`, `lastCopy: L + 1000`, whose only entry is the hand-off at 16 bytes from a work copy holding that text, and whose `record` is `{ from: <a work copy>, to: <folder>/record/0003.json, bytes: 9 }` (no `record/0003.json`) → `takeOutOfCheck(ctxOf(w), { name: NAME, record: <record 2>, paths: [<hand-off>], reads: [{ path: <hand-off>, id: fileId(<hand-off>) }], rev: 3 }, …)` (the id taken just before the call) is `{ busy: "restored" }`; the hand-off is `HANDOFF` again; neither pending is marked; no lock left. Called again, with the id taken again: `{ end }`; the hand-off is still `HANDOFF`; both pendings are marked. (Judging the dead one too would put its text back after revision 1's, and the writer would be refused every time.)
- T14 "ownRecordBack puts back a held finish's lost record before a command builds on the record before it, and nothing else": `holdFinal` (`copied`), `record/0002.json` removed → `ownRecordBack(ctxOf(w), { name: NAME, folder, record: <record 1> })` is true; `record/0002.json` is back, byte for byte its work copy; no `late-check.lock` left; `<home>/Library/Logs/brimm-video.log` has `an episode (ab0001): its record 0002.json was lost in Dropbox, so it is put back from its copy on this Mac.` It is false, writes nothing in the episode's folder, and leaves no lock: with `copied: false, recordWritten: false` (the finish died before its record); with `record/0002.json` there and the record given as record 2; and with a live "late check" lock (`liveLock`), which it leaves as it was. With `workCopies: false`, it is false and puts nothing back (the check flags it).
- T15 "is busy restored when a read's file is no longer the one the writer read, though no pending holds it now": `holdFinal`; `final/Talk main.srt` cut after its first 3 bytes, and `id = fileId(<srt>)` taken (as the writer takes it before its read); then, as the check does before the writer has the lock, the `.srt` put back from its work copy with `replaceIn` (a new inode) and the pending removed → `takeOutOfCheck(ctxOf(w), { name: NAME, record: <record 2>, paths: [<srt>], reads: [{ path: <srt>, id }], rev: 3 }, …)` is `{ busy: "restored" }`; nothing is written; no lock left. With `id` taken again now: `{ end }`. With the read given `id: null`: `{ end }`.
- T16 "restoreReads judges only a read that is gone or at another size, asks no lock otherwise, and puts it back": `holdFinal`. Every file as written, with a live "late check" lock (`liveLock`) → `restoreReads(ctxOf(w), { name: NAME, record: <record 2>, folder, rels: ["final/Talk main.srt", "final/Talk.brimm-ops.json"], rev: 3 }, …)` is `""`, with no wait, and the test's lock is as it was; the test then lets it go. `final/Talk main.srt` removed → `"restored"`; the `.srt` is `SRT` again; the pending as it was, unmarked; no lock left. The `.srt` cut to 3 bytes → `"restored"`. The `.srt` removed and `workCopies: false` → `"lost"`. The `.srt` removed, the pending removed first (its check made) → `""` (the writer goes on to today's words). A rel the record does not list, not there → `""`. For a record without `local` → `""` at once.
- S1 "settles a mark with a rev by the record's number": `holdFinal` with `mark: { pid: 999999, at: L, to: [<srt>], nonce: "n", rev: 3 }`: (a) after `later(() => ({}))` (record 3, listing the `.srt` at 7) → `settleReading(pending, { home })` gives a pending without the `.srt`'s entry, written back at its key with no mark; (b) with no record numbered 3 or above (record 2 lists it at 7) → the entry is kept; (c) with the mark's `rev` 4 and `later((r) => ({ files: { ...r.files, "final/Talk main.srt": 9 } }))` (record 3) → the entry is left out.
- S2 "settles a mark with no rev by sizes alone, as today": the same mark with no `rev`, and record 3 listing the `.srt` at 7 → the entry kept.
- S4 "settles a mark whose rev this Mac noted writing as the writer's, though Dropbox lost its record": S1's mark (`rev: 3`), no record numbered 3 or above (record 2 lists the `.srt` at 7), and `acceptRev(w.home, ID, 3)` (this Mac's `written.json` noting rev 3 of `ID`) → `settleReading(pending, { home })` gives a pending without the `.srt`'s entry, with no mark; with `acceptRev(w.home, ID, 2)` (as after `Dropbox undid…`) → the entry is kept, as S1(b).
- S3 "settles a mark with a rev past a conflicted copy of a file in final/, and not past one of a record": W2's state with `final/Talk main (Sallie's laptop's conflicted copy 2026-11-06).srt` beside the `.srt` → `settleReading` settles it (the hand-off's entry left out, no mark); W2's state with `record/0003 (Sallie's laptop's conflicted copy 2026-11-06).json` → `{ later: true }`, the mark kept.

**`tool/tests/dropbox.test.js`** (D1 and D2 are nets of L3's `onCopy`, which the finish's pending relies on: they pass at once):
- D1 "textWriter's onCopy is called with the whole work copy, before it is put in": the stand-in `onCopy` reads `from` (the whole text) and finds nothing at `to`; afterwards `to` holds the text, and the writer gives `{ from, to, bytes }`.
- D2 "a throw from onCopy puts nothing in, and throws the stand-in's error": the writer throws that very error; nothing at `to`; with `keep` the work copy stays, without it it is gone.
- D3 "sameBytes and startsAlike are dropbox.js's": two files of the same bytes → true; one byte different → false; one missing → false. (unedited.test.js' tests of `ownLateCheck` stay green: the net of the move.)

**`tool/tests/late-wait.test.js`**:
- The describe "keepCheckInStep (R1c)" is unchanged and passes before and after the move (a net).
- K1 "a pending of another kind holds no reading and no copy": a record without `local` (this file's `setup`) and, of its id, a `final`, a `record` and an `original` pending due later, written with `writeFileSync` at `pending/final/<id>-1.json`, `pending/record/<id>.json` and `pending/original/<id>.json` (the `original` one `{ version: 1, kind: "original", id, …, owed: true, entries: [], took: [], work: null, lastCopy }`; not with `writePending`, which until Step 3 writes every pending at `pending/<id>.json`), and no import pending → `lateCheckAt` null, `ownCheckOnly` false, `readingWaitsUntil` null, `localSource` null, `lateWaitFor` null, `projectWaits` "" and `importHold(…).state` "" (a net: passes at once).

**`tool/tests/settings.test.js`**, where `heldInOld` is tested:
- H1 "a pending of a kind is not an import's: a switch back from the picked folder is not refused for it": a Mac with `dropPath`, its picked folder empty, and a pending in each of `pending/record/`, `pending/final/` and `pending/original/` → `write … --drop Drop --switch-back` ends 0 (a net).

**`tool/tests/finish-check.test.js`**:
- K2 "clearChecking with a revision takes away only a marker of that revision": a marker of revision 2 → `clearChecking(home, folder, 1)` leaves it; `clearChecking(home, folder, 2)` takes it away; `clearChecking(home, folder)` takes any away, as today.
- K3 "checkingNow believes a local episode's marker while its final pending is there, whoever holds the lock": a local record holding revision 1, a marker of revision 1 written with nonce "x", no lock, and `writePending` of a `final` pending of revision 1 → `{ revision: 1, due, took: ["Downloads"] }`; with the record at revision 2 (a spelling since), the same; with the pending gone, or with `problem` or `wasProblem` in it → null. A record without `local` and the same files → null, as today (no run holds the lock).

**`tool/tests/unedited.test.js`**, in L3's describe of an import on a local Mac ("an episode filed on a Mac with a drop folder of its own (Phase 3, L3)", 2814), its world `local()`, whose `wait` moves its clock (1290-1293). A local import's run now waits, after the Ready banner, for its record's check, and makes it (15 minutes on its clock) before it returns: so these tests look at the pending during the run, through that `wait`, and L3's test at 2848 ("posts the Ready banner and takes the drop file away before any wait, and writes no import pending") changes its two assertions of no wait after the banner (`seen[0][1] === t.waits.length`, and no wait of a minute or more) to: no wait of a minute or more comes before the banner, and the run's last line is the (whole) line of its record's check; its other assertions stay.
- I1 "a local import writes its record pending before its record reaches Dropbox": the `clone` stand-in, when `to` ends `record/0001.json`, notes whether `pending/record/<id>.json` is there with no `copied` → it is, and it has none. At the run's first wait of a minute or more (the `wait` stand-in reads the pending then): the pending has `copied: true`, `kind: "record"`, the record's entry alone, `label` the import's, `drop` the drop file's path and `dropFile` its `fileId` as it arrived (`ino`, `size`, `mtimeMs`), `work` the run folder (still there); the Ready notice was posted before that wait; the log has `<label>: its first record is in Dropbox. Its check is due at <hh:mm>.`; no `pending/*.json`. Once the run returns: the pending and the run folder are gone, and the log has `<label>: its first record is whole in Dropbox.`.
- I2 "a record write that fails after the hook wrote the pending takes the pending away, and nothing is ever put back for it" (the review of L3): the `clone` stand-in, when `to` ends `record/0001.json`, notes that `pending/record/<id>.json` is there (the hook fired) and throws → once the run returns: no `pending/record/*.json`; the run folder is gone; `video/` and `Episodes/<folder>` are not there and the drop file is in the drop folder, as L3's test of it (2896) says; `notices` is the import's failure alone; this Mac's `written.json` notes no rev of the id. (b) The same, the stand-in also making `pending/record/` read-only (`chmod 0o555`) before it throws, and the run's `wait` putting it back (`0o755`) at its first wait of a minute or more → the log has the (not taken away) line; and once the run returns (it waited for that pending and made its check): no pending; the run folder is gone; the stopped line; no notice but the import's failure; no `Episodes/<folder>` and no `record/0001.json` were made; `video/` is not there.
- I3 "a pending that cannot be written stops the import before its record": `pending/` made read-only first (`chmod 0o555`, put back in `finally`) → no `record/0001.json`; no pending; the drop file is still in the drop folder; nothing this run wrote is left in `video/`; the import failed as L3's test of a failing record write says, its status and log giving the reason `it could not be filed into Episodes: the check of its first record could not be kept on this Mac`; no Ready notice.
- I4 "a run stopped between the pending and the record: the pending is dropped at its check, and the import is found again by its marker": the state a stopped run leaves (the pending without `copied`, as the hook writes it, with `drop` and `dropFile` of the drop file, its run folder, `video/` with its marker and files, no `record/0001.json`, the drop file still in the drop folder) and a run with the clock past due → the old pending and run folder are gone (the stopped line); the video is imported into the same folder (by its marker), whose `0001.json` has another id; the log has the new import's (due) line and, once the run has waited for it, its (whole) line.
- I5 "its check puts back a lost first record" (end to end): an import whose run's `wait`, at its first wait of a minute or more, keeps `0001.json`'s text and removes it → once the run returns, it is back, byte for byte that text; the pending and run folder are gone; the put-back line.
- I6 "an import stopped after its clone and before it noted its record still has a lost first record put back": an import whose run's `wait`, at its first wait of a minute or more, finds `lastWritten(home, <its id>)` is 1, writes its pending again without `copied` (what a run stopped between its clone and `copied`, or whose `copied` write failed, leaves), keeps `0001.json`'s text and removes it → once the run returns, it is back, byte for byte that text; the pending and run folder are gone; the recording is still in `video/`.
- I7 "a local import of a video with no sound writes its record pending too, through its own filing": a drop the ffprobe stand-in reports with a picture and no sound, as the PR H5a tests do (unedited.test.js 2487-2490) → the `clone` stand-in, when `to` ends `record/0001.json`, finds `pending/record/<id>.json` there with no `copied`; at the run's first wait of a minute or more the pending has `copied: true`, `label` the import's, `drop` the drop file's path and `dropFile` its `fileId` as it arrived, as I1 asserts them, its run folder is there, and the `wait` keeps `0001.json`'s text and removes it → once the run returns, it is back, byte for byte that text; the pending and run folder are gone.

#### Steps

1. The world changes (`episodes.js`' kick, `checks-world.js`, `local-world.js`) and every test above, every name L5 adds reached through a namespace import (World changes). `cd tool && node --test`: every test file loads; the new tests fail, each by a missing export or a wrong value; the nets (K1, H1, F8, L16, L19, D1, D2, the R1c describe) pass at once; L3's test at unedited.test.js 2848, changed, fails until Step 6 (its run does not wait for a record check yet); every other old test still passes.

F1, F2, F3, F11 and F14 pass.

F6, F7, F9, F10, F13, F16, F17, F18, S1, S2, S3 and S4 pass, and F8 still does; filing.test.js' tests 10-15 and 36-37 still pass.
