New, Jason's: the log, each line under the name the algorithms and tests give it ("(whole)", "the whole line"). `<label>` is an import's label; `<shown>` is `shownName` of the record in force (of the record it read, for a writer's line); `<who>` is `<label>` for a `record` pending and `an episode (<short id>)` for any other (algorithm A), and names a `final` pending until the gate has let it through (ruling 15).

The finish's run, in its output (`brimm-video.log`), and `askAgent`, through `logTo`:
- (kick) `<shown>: the launch agent could not be asked to make the check of its copies (<code>). It is made when the Mac's import next runs.`

A local import and its `record` check:
- (due) `<label>: its first record is in Dropbox. Its check is due at <hh:mm>.`
- (not noted) `<label>: its first record is in Dropbox, but its check could not note it (<code>).`
- (not taken away) `<label>: its first record could not be written, and the check kept for it could not be taken away (<code>). Its check drops it.` (ruling 9)
- (whole) `<label>: its first record is whole in Dropbox.`
- (put back) `<label>: Dropbox lost its first record, so it is put back.`
- (later record) `<label>: its first record is not in Dropbox, but a later one is, so nothing is put back.`
- (another) `<label>: another import's first record is in its place now, so nothing is put back.`
- (stopped) `<label>: its import stopped before its first record reached Dropbox, so there is nothing to check.`
- (again) `<label>: its first record is not in Dropbox, and its video is still in the drop folder, so the next look imports it again, and nothing is put back.`
- (could not) `<label>: Dropbox lost its first record: <problem>` (the problem's own sentence ends the line)
- (folder gone) `FOLDER_GONE(<label>)`, also posted (ruling 16).
- (cannot tell) `<label>: its check waits: its folder in Episodes cannot be read now. It is looked at again later.`
- (whose) `<label>: its check waits: its first record in Dropbox does not read, and whether it is this import's cannot be told. It is looked at again later.`

The `final` check:
- (passed) `<shown>: the check of its copies in final/ passed: every copy the record lists is whole in Dropbox.`
- (put back) `<shown>: <file> in final/ was not whole in Dropbox (<what>), so it is put back from its copy on this Mac.` (also a writer's, algorithm D)
- (record put back) `<who>: its record <NNNN>.json was lost in Dropbox, so it is put back from its copy on this Mac.` (also `ownRecordBack`'s, for a command, in `brimm-video.log`: ruling 35)
- (left) `<shown>: <n> of its copies in final/ is from a later write, so the check leaves it as it is.` (`are`, `them`, `as they are` for more than one)
- (deleted) `<shown>: <file> is taken out of Downloads.`
- (changed) `<shown>: <file> changed in Downloads after it was taken, so it is kept there.`
- (no longer holds) `<shown>: the hand-off in force no longer holds <file>, so it is left in Downloads.`
- (not from Downloads) `<shown>: <file> was not taken from Downloads, so it is left where it is.`
- (could not) `<shown>: <file> could not be looked at or taken out of Downloads (<code>), so it is kept there. Delete it there by hand.`
- (kept exports) `<shown>: the exports it took are kept in Downloads, because a copy in final/ was not whole.`
- (problem) `<shown>: the check of its copies in final/ found a problem: <problem>`
- (stopped) `<who>: its finish stopped before its record was written, so the check of its copies is not made.`
- (cannot tell) `<who>: its check waits: whether its record reached Dropbox cannot be told now. It is looked at again later.`
- (folder unreadable) `<who>: its check waits: its folder in Episodes cannot be read now. It is looked at again later.`
- (later) `<who>: its check waits: the episode's record cannot be read now. It is looked at again later.`
- (unsettled) `<who>: its check waits: a write stopped while it put its files in, and the episode's record cannot be read now. It is looked at again later.`
- (folder gone) `FOLDER_GONE(<who>)`, also posted (ruling 16).
- (gate) `<name, as gatedAsOf names it, with its short id>: it is held as a session now, so nothing of its hand-off is put back into Dropbox, and its exports stay in Downloads.`
- (held beside) `<name, as gatedAsOf names it, with its short id>: another version of it is a session not cleared, so nothing of its hand-off is put back into Dropbox yet, and its exports stay in Downloads.` (once a run, through `once`)
- (finish at work) `<who>: its check waits until the finish at work on it ends.` (before the lock, through `sayOnce`; under the lock at step 9, `<shown>` in place of `<who>`, through `once`)

Any kind:
- (put off) `<who>: its check waits a moment: a write is putting its files in.`
- (settled) `<who>: a write stopped while it put its files in. Its check leaves out <n> file the record says is that write's.` (`files`, `are` for more than one)
- (threw) `<who>: its check could not be made (<code>). The next run makes it again.`
- (not put away) `<who>: its check is made, but it could not be put away (<code>). The next run looks at it again.`
- (not taken out) `<shown>: a write could not take its files out of an earlier check (<code>). That check settles them by the record.` (a writer's `end`, algorithm D, step 5, through `logTo`)
- `cloneIn`'s and `replaceIn`'s own lines, as today. The import's own lines are as today.

#### Known limits (for the coordinator and Jason; none deletes an export early or puts an older file over a newer one)

1. A finish killed in the moment between its record's clone and its `copied` write, whose record Dropbox then loses within the 15 minutes, is dropped as never held, and that record is not put back (nor by a command: ruling 35 asks the same). Both must happen; `written.json` cannot witness it, since after W3 the next writer's record at that number notes it too (ruling 11). A local import has such a moment only when this Mac cannot write `written.json` (ruling 9: `noteWritten` passes over that): an import whose note could not be written, stopped between its clone and `copied`, whose record Dropbox then loses within the 15 minutes, has its pending dropped as never reached, as ruling 0 drops one stopped before its clone; its drop file, deleted by then or not, is not asked.
2. A writer killed between its marks and its record, after which a record-only write (a name, a title) takes its number before the check, has its half-done files left out of the check (W8): the record lists them at their old sizes, and `episodes` says they are not whole.
3. A writer killed after its record, whose file of the same size Dropbox then reverts, keeps the reverted copy: the check holds no copy of the writer's (the spellings' copies are not checked, as today). So does a copy no pending holds any more (its check made, or a spelling's), which Dropbox changes before a writer reads it: the writer is judged against no entry (ruling 34), as today. (A copy the check puts back after the writer read it is caught by the read's id.)
4. A pending written while the agent is in its last moments, past ruling 6's read, is made at the agent's next run (a drop, a login, a hand-off's kick), not 15 minutes on. Late, never lost; the export stays in Downloads meanwhile.
5. Every copy in `final/` is read twice under the lock: a writer waits a second or two behind a gigabyte main video.
6. A copy Dropbox makes online-only within the 15 minutes is taken as whole and not read; a record file the agent cannot read keeps that check waiting, looked at every five minutes for an hour and then once a run, said once a run, with nothing deleted.
7. The export in Downloads is now deleted by the launch agent's `node`, on every Mac (Jason, 7 Nov: "Yes, let it delete"). On the Mac Studio, Downloads is a link into Dropbox, which the agent already reads. On a Mac whose Downloads is its own (the MacBooks, at their move), macOS may refuse the agent's `node` the folder (Files and Folders) until it is allowed: until then the export stays, and the log's (could not) line says to delete it by hand. "By hand, for the coordinator to add" gives P3-7 the step that looks in Downloads after the check, and each MacBook's move the step that allows it.
8. A `record` check that keeps failing keeps the import's run folder (with its sound file) until a person puts the record right.
9. `heldInOld` lets a Mac switch back with `record` or `final` pendings owed: the loop makes them on whatever drop folder it watches next.
10. Each `final` check reads Episodes' records once for the gate (`linksOf`), as each look of the import already does for `heldBy`; the cost grows with Episodes.
11. A conflicted copy Dropbox makes in a local episode keeps its `final` check waiting until it is sorted out (`resolve`, which asks the agent to run): looked at every five minutes for the first hour, then once a run (ruling 36); `status` then says the check is late (ruling 22), and the log says why.
12. When neither `kern.waketime` nor `kern.boottime` can be read (`lastWake` null), a check that came due while the Mac slept or was off is made within a minute of the wake or the login, before Dropbox may have settled (ruling 33). The start is the kernel's, not the login: a Mac that started and waited at its login window for more than 15 minutes makes an overdue check as soon as the agent starts at login. A Mac that slept between two looks of a check's 15 minutes, and woke before they were up, is checked 15 minutes after that wake, not after the copy. Power Nap stays on (Jason, 7 Nov: "fine, leave power nap on"): should macOS not set `kern.waketime` at a dark wake (by-hand step 3), a check due in the night may be made at a dark wake, before Dropbox has settled; it still deletes an export only when every copy is whole.
13. A writer killed after its record and before `end`, whose record Dropbox then loses, after which a command has said `Dropbox undid…` (`acceptRev` lowers `written.json`): its mark is settled by the record's number alone, so the earlier finish's copies are put back over the writer's, and the next hand-off writer may give the revision she already took to BRIMM Ops again, which the page refuses (ruling 20). Four things must happen; without the `acceptRev`, `written.json` keeps the writer's files (S4). And `resolveIn` asks no `undone`: a `resolve … --keep B` killed before its record, on an episode whose last record of this Mac Dropbox had lost (a spelling's, which no pending puts back), has B left out of the check by `written.json`, as W8 leaves a stopped writer's files.
14. `finishAtWork` sees a yes (`finish.asked.json`) and a run's lock, not a quick part that only asked "Hand it off?": an export that part named, whose part is unchanged since the hand-off being checked, may be deleted a moment later, and her yes then finds it gone. It is so only for an export the check would delete anyway once its 15 minutes are up.
15. A pending past its `lookUntil` (a wait's hour gone, or one held beside a session) whose one look at a run's start is put off, by a writer's lock or a finish at work, is not looked at again in that run: it waits for the next run, with nothing deleted.

2. Jason: Apple menu › Sleep; after a minute, wake the Mac. Claude: `sysctl -n kern.waketime` shows that wake's time.

- Ruling 16 as changed (Jason: "not always, keep a short notice"): a check that drops its pending because the episode's folder is gone posts and logs "The folder of <who> is gone from Episodes, so the check of its copies in Dropbox is not made. Ask Jason." A `record` check that re-makes the folder while this Mac holds the episode's `video/` says nothing: that is the first record put back.

Add its test.

### L6. Original files after the hand-off

Line numbers are at 4dd0136 (L1, L1b, L1c, L1d, L2, L2c, L3, L4, L7 and L8 built; L5 designed, at this plan's 791-1467, and built just before this task). A builder finds code by its function's name; what L6 takes from L5 is named under "Consumes", by the names of L5's design, and is used as L5 built it. L6 is built after L5 is merged, on its tip. The coordinator accepts or rejects two rulings before the builder starts: ruling 1 moves every part of a plan (`partsOf`), not only the talking parts the join took (`planned.filmed.parts`, this section's earlier draft); and ruling 9 says the Mac's copy, once deleted, frees no room on its disk until Dropbox makes its copy online-only (Known limits 1), which Jason may want Studio to ask Dropbox for.

**Goal:** At the hand-off of a video kept on this Mac, every recording she dropped for it is copied into its episode's `Original files/` in Dropbox, behind her, by the launch agent. The Mac's copy is deleted only once Dropbox's check of that copy has passed and Dropbox reports it uploaded; a failed check keeps it and tells her. A session's recording, or one held beside a session, never moves; a video that becomes a session after its move is left as it is in Dropbox, and Jason is told. Every file made from a recording stays where it is, and every reader of a recording finds it in `Original files/` once it has left the Mac (D6).

**Evidence** (4dd0136):
- Jason's words: plan 14-15 ("Yes, every recording"; the move "Right away"; the Mac's copy goes "When Dropbox says uploaded"; parts that show a client, "Yes, move them too"; "Each part's folder"); D6 (53), D9 (62), Global Constraints "Sessions stay out" (136), Review Focus 1 and 2 (143-144).
- P3-0 (By-Hand check 87, 6 Nov) passed on the Mac Studio with the plan's Swift lines (1590-1595: `ubiquitousItemIsUploadedKey`): `false` when written, `true` about 15 s after the upload, `false` at every read while syncing was paused, `true` at the first read after it resumed, `null` for a file outside Dropbox.
- The launch agent runs swift already: `findPrograms` (unedited.js 107-117) finds `swift` only with Xcode's tools, `facesIn` runs `faces.swift` with it (FACES 67, 380-388), and `depsFor` gives the loop `tools: found` and `notify: banner` (1628); the loop's `episode` is `{ root, machine, checkout, home, local, statuses, statusFolders }` (1513).
- The hand-off writers: `finishNow` (finish.js 670-1177; `nextRecord` 1019, its record 1051-1063, `recordWritten = true` 1060), which is also `finish … reels`; `finishFinished` (1342-1513: its `try` 1472, `writeEpisode` 1490, `catch` 1491-1496, `finally` 1497-1499, the reveal 1500), which `importHandoff` (edit.js 3914-3934) calls for a finished video's or an iPhone clip's hand-off as it is; `finishSession` (1547-), a session's, which owes nothing. The first hand-off of any video that is not a session is written by `finishNow` or `finishFinished`; `saveSpellings` (edit.js) writes only later revisions.
- A plan's footage: each part is an episode of its own, `partOf: { id, as, shot, client }` (footage.js 457; a voiceover's 527), listed by `partsOf(root, id)` (130-132, not archived); `isPart` (episode.js 166) has `kind: null`, so `gated` (gate.js 88) holds every part; a part is held as a session by `matchedSession` and `sessionsBeside` (`partHeld`, footage.js 182-193; finish.js 323-369, 371). A video made from a plan (`isFromPlan`, episode.js 283) has the join's video, or the voice it took in, as its own `video.file` (join.js `videoOf`, 302-314).
- A video is held as a session by `gated` (a session not cleared, or no kind) and by `sessionsBeside` (edit.js `episodeAt` 521); `heldName` (gate.js 95) names one by its label, `withShortId` (103) adds its short id. `kind … --again` can make a handed-off video a session (`answerKind`; its set-aside never names `Original files/`: `earlierFiles`, edit.js 5245-5275).
- Layout 6 already reads and writes `local.original: { file, bytes, mtimeMs }` (episode.js `isOriginal` 254-255, `isLocalPlace` 258); `filePlace` puts `Original files/…` in Dropbox (where.js 15, 28-32); `fileAt` and `folderAt` read nothing (37-42).
- The readers of a recording, all through `fileAt` (L2c): its size and time, for a key or a comparison: app.js `copyIsMade` 123, `changedSince` 414-420 (to the second), `adoptProject` 454, `makeProject` 501, `putVideoBack` 579; app-reel.js `adoptReel` 171, `makeReel` 238, `putReelVideoBack` 292; plan-project.js `copyOfFootage` 87-95; workcopy.js `makeWorkCopy` (its key from `stat(video)`, 730-734; `from` is read in place of `video`, 732) and `makeFootageCopies` (929-955, which reads a video through first, 939-944, and calls `makeWorkCopy` at 951). Its path, to read it: edit.js `planFootage` items 3610-3612, the workcopy run's `copyOf` 3703-3732 (`makeWorkCopy` at 3712, 3713, 3732), `kind`'s raw test 4927 and its copy request 5090, `whichCommand` 5626, `showForStep` 6406-6408; free.js 325-326; reading.js `renderRough` 755; unedited.js `askBlocked` 1397 and `askAgain` 1415. `/bin/cp -c` keeps a file's time (dropbox.js 1-11, 30-36), but a copy Dropbox made online-only and brought back down may take its time from Dropbox.
- What a record's files must be: `fileProblems` (episode.js 722-738) calls a local file missing when it is not in `video/`, and `problemsOf` (edit.js 1191-1195) refuses `finish` (2224-2227) and `claimAndBring` (1267-1280: `open`, `app`) over it; `notHere` (1198) asks every listed file to be whole on this Mac, and `claimAndBring` (1282-1286) and `bring` (1649) bring each that is not.
- The locks: `lockOf` (detached.js 16), `takeEpisodeLock(lock, command, alive) -> { taken, text, nonce } | { running }` (95), `releaseEpisodeLock(lock, text)` (155), `runningRun` (46), `runningCommand` (52), which reads `episode.lock` alone; `workCopyLockOf` (workcopy.js 551), held by the workcopy run (edit.js 3695). Her commands are stopped by a run through `stoppedWords` (edit.js 1986-1992; callers 2230, 3419, 4291, 4741), and `status` says `<name>: <command> is running.` (1766).
- The record: `readEpisode` (episode.js 605), `recordNumbers` (572), `writeEpisode` (677-692), `undone` (659-664), `acceptRev` (654), `lastWritten` (623); an agent-side writer's `writer` is `{ commit: deps.commit || commitOf(episode.checkout), on: episode.machine }` (filing.js 333). `fileId` (filing.js 618), `sameFile` (625-629). `clearRuns` takes a folder named `<word>-<pid>-<time>` once its run is dead (1061-1079). The run's start: `clearRuns` (1004), `clearCrashes` (1006), `finishDue(true)` (1007).
- The guide's lines under a video are `guideItems`' `also` (edit.js 6337-6364; L5 adds its checking line there); `episodes` says a video by `episodeLines` (984-1016; `Finished:` at 1012, `if (there) return lines;` at 1013).

**Rulings for L6:**
1. **What moves** (Jason: "Yes, every recording"; "Yes, move them too"): the recording she dropped for a video whose record has `local.on` this Mac: the video's own `video.file`, or, for a video made from a plan (`isFromPlan`), the `video.file` of each part `partsOf(root, id)` lists whose own record has `local.on` this Mac (a talking part, b-roll, a screen recording, a voiceover, and a part that shows a client alike). A departure from this section's earlier draft, which took `planned.filmed.parts`, the talking parts the join took: every part is a recording she dropped for the video. Never: a recording `asSession` holds (ruling 5); one already moved (`local.original`, or `Original files/<file>` listed); the plan's own `video.file` (the joined video, or the voice it took in); a HeyGen voice; the working copies, transcripts, words, cut plans, notes, teachings files, `renders/` and every HyperFrames project and its media. Studio made those, and a later Reel or work copy is made from them.
2. **Where** (Jason: "Each part's folder"): `Episodes/<the recording's own episode folder>/Original files/<file>`, listed in that episode's record.
3. **When** (Jason: "Right away"): `finishNow` and `finishFinished`, once the record holding the hand-off is written, call `oweOriginals`, which writes an owed `original` pending for each recording of ruling 1 and asks the agent to run. An owed pending is due at once (L5's `dueAt`). Owing one that has a pending already, or is moved, changes nothing. At the start of each run of the launch agent `oweForgotten` owes it for any handed-off video kept on this Mac whose recording is still in `video/`, not moved and owed by no pending: a hand-off writer stopped before it owed, or whose owe could not be written.
4. **The Mac's copy goes** (Jason: "When Dropbox says uploaded") only when, in one look of the check, under the episode's `late-check.lock` (L5's `finishDue` takes it): the folder and the record in force can be read; the gate does not hold the recording (ruling 5); the record in force lists `Original files/<file>` at the move's bytes and holds `local.original`; the Mac's file is still the one the move copied (`sameFile` with `macCopy`); the copy in Dropbox is whole by L5's table J (nothing put back in this look); `uploaded` says `true`; no live run holds the episode's `workcopy.lock`, or its video's when it is a part; and the check has the episode's `episode.lock`. A copy put back starts its 15 minutes again; `false` or `null` keeps the Mac's copy and looks again every `UPLOAD_LOOK_MS` (10 minutes) for an hour (`WAIT_LOOK_FOR_MS`), then once at each run's start. A copy that cannot be made or put back is a problem: the Mac's copy and the pending stay, the notice `ORIGINALS_PROBLEM` is posted once, and the guide says it under the video's step.
5. **Sessions** (Global Constraints, "Sessions stay out"): `asSession` is asked when the move is owed, when it is made, and in the look that deletes the Mac's copy, before anything else is done. Held before the move: nothing moves, a clone a stopped move left unlisted is taken away, and the pending goes. Held after the move (Jason: "Right away"): nothing in Dropbox is changed or deleted, the Mac's copy stays if it is there, the pending goes, and `ORIGINALS_SESSION(<label>)` is logged, and said under the video's step in the guide and on its line in `episodes` for as long as its record lists `Original files/<file>` and `asSession` holds it. Jason removes the copy by hand if he wants.
6. **Locks.** The move and the check run inside L5's dispatch, holding the episode's `late-check.lock`. Each tries the episode's `episode.lock` once, as `takeEpisodeLock(lockOf(home, name), ORIGINALS_COMMAND, alive)`, and is put off `LOCK_AGAIN_MS` (written as its `nextLook`) when a live run holds it, as the check is when a live run holds `workcopy.lock`. Neither ever waits, so no cycle is made with a finish, which holds `episode.lock` and waits for `late-check.lock` (L5's lock order 2-3). A command of hers that meets the lock says `Its recording is being copied into Dropbox. <again>`, and `status` says `<name>: its recording is being copied into Dropbox.`
7. **The move is a record writer.** Under its locks it settles every mark on a pending of its episode (`settleReading`, as L5's algorithm D, step 2), puts back a held finish's lost record first (`putOwnRecordBack`, L5's ruling 35), never takes the number a held finish's record should have, and asks `undone`, as every writer does; it goes on past `undone` only when the change Dropbox undid is its own earlier record (the pending's `rev`).
8. **Afterwards** every reader of a recording reads it through `recordingAt` (its path: `video/<file>` while that is there, else `Original files/<file>` when the record lists it) or `recordingStat` (its size and time: the Mac's file while it is there, else `local.original`'s `bytes` and `mtimeMs` while the copy in Dropbox has that size), so a working copy, a Reel's copy and a footage copy keep their keys, and `changedSince` sees no change. `fileProblems` calls the recording missing only when neither is there; `notHere` never asks for `Original files/` to be brought: a reader that needs the recording whole brings it down then (the workcopy run reads it through, as `makeFootageCopies` does).
9. **An episode from before the switch** (D9; Jason: "Yes, some do"): its own recording is not moved, and a record without `local` owes nothing. A part dropped after the switch for a plan from before it is local, and moves when the plan is handed off: the old finish's run owes it too. Deleting the Mac's copy frees no room by itself: the copy in `Original files/` is an APFS clone of it, sharing its blocks, until Dropbox makes it online-only (Known limits 1).
10. **No test runs swift.** `checkOriginal` reads the flag through `deps.uploaded` when it is given (a test's stand-in, `(path) => true | false | null`) and through `uploadedOf` with `deps.tools.swift` otherwise; every test that makes a check gives `uploaded`, and `uploadedOf`'s tests give `exec`. No test reaches `launchctl`: `setup()`'s `kickstart` stand-in (L5's ruling 27).

**Files:**
- Create `tool/originals.js`, `tool/uploaded.swift`, `tool/tests/originals.test.js`.
- `tool/dropbox.js` (`UPLOADED`, `UPLOADED_TIMEOUT_MS`, `uploadedOf`); `tool/where.js` (`ORIGINALS_FOLDER`, `recordingAt`, `recordingStat`, its head comment); `tool/episode.js` (`fileProblems`); `tool/workcopy.js` (`makeWorkCopy`'s `rawStat`, `makeFootageCopies`' `raw`); `tool/filing.js` (`CHECKS.original`, `oweForgotten` at the run's start); `tool/finish.js` (`finishNow`, `finishFinished`).
- The readers: `tool/app.js` (123, 415, 454, 501, 579), `tool/app-reel.js` (171, 238, 292), `tool/free.js` (325), `tool/plan-project.js` (89), `tool/reading.js` (755), `tool/unedited.js` (1397, 1415), `tool/edit.js` (`notHere`, `planFootage`, `copyOf`, 4927, 5090, `whichCommand`, `showForStep`).
- Her words: `tool/edit.js` (`stoppedWords`, `status`, `episodeLines`, `guideItems`).
- `README.md`: "What is where" gains `tool/originals.js` and `tool/uploaded.swift` (the paragraph on Original files and the flag is L9's).
- Tests: `tool/tests/dropbox.test.js`, `where.test.js`, `episode.test.js`, `workcopy.test.js`, `local-episode.test.js`; L5's worlds `tool/tests/local-world.js` and `tool/tests/checks-world.js` (the `original` kind off unless a test turns it on).
- Not changed: `tool/guide.js` (its `also` lines come from edit.js), `finish-reels.js` (`finish … reels` is `finishNow`), `saveSpellings`, `join.js` (no join after a hand-off), the hand-off modules and fixtures, `tool/shared/`, `lib.js`, `run.js`, `HELP` and `today-says.json`, the skill twins.

#### Interfaces

**Consumes**, by exact name:
- L5's design: `PENDING_KINDS` (with "original"), `pendingKeyOf` (`original/<id>.json`), `writePending(home, pending)` by key, `readPendingAt(home, key)`, `readPendingsOf(home, kind)`, `readEveryPending(home)`, `removePending(home, pending)`; `dueAt(pending, woke)` (an `owed: true` pending is due at its `lastCopy`; a `nextLook` only puts a look off); `isOpen`, `pastLookUntil`, `WAIT_LOOK_MS`, `WAIT_LOOK_FOR_MS`, `LOCK_AGAIN_MS`; `CHECKS` and `deps.checks` (`{ ...CHECKS, ...deps.checks }[kind]`, a missing or null check holding no run open: ruling 23); `finishDue`'s call `check(fresh, { ...deps, clock, alive, once })` under `late-check.lock` of `fresh.name`, and its read after the check (algorithm A, steps 3-4); the decision table and `lookOwed` (rows 1, 1b, 4, 7); `<who>` (`an episode (<short id>)` for a pending with no `label`); "put away" and "wait" as algorithm C has them; `FOLDER_GONE(who)`; `settleReading(pending, { home, log })` (`{ later }` or the settled pending, written back); `recordHolds(pending, { isWhole })`; `putOwnRecordBack(pending, { isWhole, opts })`; table J and its put-back, the one helper `checkFinal` and `takeOutOfCheck` share (L5's Step 5; export it from filing.js unchanged if L5 left it unexported, and add no second copy of the table); `sameBytes` (moved into dropbox.js); `logTo(home)`; `askAgent(ctx, shown)`; algorithm E's (f) and (i) in `finishNow` (its one kick, only once the record is written); algorithm F's "and ask" in `finishFinished`; L5's local branches of `stoppedWords` and `status`; `setup()`'s `kickstart` stand-in and `t.kicks`; L5's worlds `local-world.js` (`finishRun`, `agentRun`, `exportOf`) and `checks-world.js`.
- Built: where.js `keptOnAMac`, `fileAt`, `folderAt`, `filePlace`; settings.js `episodeFilesFolder`, `episodeWork`, `episodesFolder`, `workFolder`, `runsFolder`; episode.js `readEpisode`, `recordNumbers`, `writeEpisode`, `undone`, `acceptRev`, `lastWritten`, `shownName`, `isPart`, `isFromPlan`, `kindOf` and layout 6's `local.original`; gate.js `gated`, `heldName`, `withShortId`; finish.js `linksOf`, `matchedSession`; footage.js `partsOf`; dropbox.js `cloneIn`, `replaceIn`, `isWhole`, `readThrough`, `textWriter`; filing.js `fileId`, `sameFile`, `commitOf`; detached.js `lockOf`, `takeEpisodeLock`, `releaseEpisodeLock`, `runningRun`, `runningCommand`; workcopy.js `workCopyLockOf`, `makeWorkCopy`, `makeFootageCopies`, `canNotBeUsed`.

**The `original` pending** (`pending/original/<id>.json`, `id` the recording's own episode's record id):
- Owed: `{ version: 1, kind: "original", id, name, folder, root, machine, of: { id, name }, file, owed: true, entries: [], work: null, lastCopy }`. `name` is the recording's episode folder's name (its work folder, where its locks are), `folder` its folder in Episodes, `of` the id and folder name of the video whose hand-off owed it (the same as `id` and `name` but for a part), `file` its `video.file`, `lastCopy` the time it was owed.
- Moved: the same with `owed: false`, `rev` (the record number the move wrote), `entries: [{ from, to, bytes }]` (`from` the Mac's copy, `video/<file>`, absolute; `to` its copy, `Episodes/<folder>/Original files/<file>`; `bytes` its size), `macCopy: { ino, size, mtimeMs }` (`fileId(from)` at the move), and `lastCopy` the move's time.
- Either may carry `problem` (text with no path), `nextLook` and `lookUntil` (ms). It has no `took`: L5's K1 writes one with `took: []`, which is only L5's net.

*`tool/where.js`:*
- `ORIGINALS_FOLDER = "Original files"` (`DROPBOX_FOLDERS` names it through this constant).
- `recordingAt(ctx, { folder, record }) -> string`: `here = fileAt(ctx, { folder, record }, record.video.file)`. `here` when `filePlace(record, record.video.file) !== "local"`, or `statSync(here, { throwIfNoEntry: false })` gives a file (a throw counting as none); else `join(folder, ORIGINALS_FOLDER, record.video.file)` when `record.files` lists `${ORIGINALS_FOLDER}/${record.video.file}` at an integer; else `here`.
- `recordingStat(ctx, { folder, record }) -> { size, mtimeMs } | undefined`: the stat of `here` when it is there (a throw counting as none); else, when `keptOnAMac(record)` and `record.local.original` names `record.video.file`, `{ size: original.bytes, mtimeMs: original.mtimeMs }` when the file `join(folder, ORIGINALS_FOLDER, record.video.file)` is there at `original.bytes` (a placeholder shows its full size); else undefined.
- Its head comment: it reads nothing but whether a recording is on this Mac (these two).

*`tool/dropbox.js`:*
- `UPLOADED` (the path of `uploaded.swift`, by `fileURLToPath(new URL("./uploaded.swift", import.meta.url))`), `UPLOADED_TIMEOUT_MS = 20 * 1000`.
- `uploadedOf(path, { swift = "", exec = execFileSync, timeoutMs = UPLOADED_TIMEOUT_MS } = {}) -> true | false | null`, synchronous: null at once with no `swift`; else `exec(swift, [UPLOADED, path], { encoding: "utf8", timeout: timeoutMs, stdio: ["ignore", "pipe", "ignore"] })`, and the `uploaded` of `JSON.parse` of its trimmed output when that is `true` or `false`; null for anything else and for any throw (a timeout, an end that is not 0, what is not JSON). It never returns a promise, so the check stays synchronous and nothing in the loop's timer can reject.

*`tool/uploaded.swift`* (beside `faces.swift`, in its manner): `swift uploaded.swift <file>` prints one JSON document and nothing else: `{"uploaded": true}`, `{"uploaded": false}`, or `{"uploaded": null}` when Foundation gives no value (a file outside Dropbox), from `URL(fileURLWithPath:).resourceValues(forKeys: [.ubiquitousItemIsUploadedKey]).ubiquitousItemIsUploaded`, read with `try?` as P3-0 read it. Given no file, or a path where no file is, it prints nothing, says why in one line on standard error, and ends 1.

*`tool/workcopy.js`:* `makeWorkCopy({ …, rawStat = null }, deps)`: `const raw = rawStat || stat(video);` (730), so the key and the size are the recording's as the record keeps it; nothing else changes. `makeFootageCopies`' items are `{ video, shot, name, raw? }`, and its call (951) gives `rawStat: item.raw || null`.

*`tool/episode.js`:* `fileProblems`: for a path on the Mac (`onTheMac`) that is `record.video.file`, not there (`size === null`), whose record's `local.original.file` is that path and whose `files` list `${ORIGINALS_FOLDER}/<path>` at an integer: no problem (`continue`). The copy in `Original files/` is looked at in `folder` by the same loop, as any file in Dropbox is.

*`tool/originals.js`* (it imports filing.js, finish.js and footage.js and is imported by them; every name is used only inside a function, but `checkOriginal`, which filing.js's `CHECKS` names at its top level: it is a function declaration, bound before any module of the cycle runs, never a `const`):
- `ORIGINALS_COMMAND = "originals"`; `UPLOAD_LOOK_MS = 10 * 60 * 1000`.
- `ORIGINALS_RUNNING = "Its recording is being copied into Dropbox."`; `ORIGINALS_PROBLEM(name)`; `ORIGINALS_SESSION(label)` (Words).
- `listsOriginal(record) -> boolean`: `record.files` lists `${ORIGINALS_FOLDER}/${record.video.file}` at an integer.
- `moved(record) -> boolean`: `keptOnAMac(record)` and (`local.original` is an object, or `listsOriginal(record)`).
- `asSession(links, { folder, record }) -> boolean`: for a part (`isPart`), `matchedSession(links, record, { folder })` or `links.sessionsBeside(record, folder).length > 0`; for any other, `gated(record)`, `kindOf(record) === "session"`, `links.sessionsBeside(record, folder).length > 0`, or `matchedSession(links, record, { folder })`.
- `originalsOf(ctx, { folder, record }) -> [{ name, folder, record }]` (`ctx`: `root`, `machine`): for `isFromPlan(record)`, the parts `partsOf(ctx.root, record.id)` gives; else `[{ name: basename(folder), folder, record }]`; of those, each whose record has `local.on === ctx.machine`, a string `video.file` and is not `moved`; then, when any is left, `links = linksOf(ctx.root)` once, and each that `asSession` does not hold.
- `oweOriginals(ctx, { folder, record }, { ask = true } = {}) -> { owed: [id] }` (`ctx`: `home`, `root`, `machine`, `now`, `agentRunning`, `kickstart`): for each of `originalsOf`, when `readPendingAt(ctx.home, \`original/${id}.json\`)` is null, `writePending` the owed pending with `of: { id: record.id, name: basename(folder) }` and `lastCopy: ctx.now()`; a write that throws is logged through `logTo(ctx.home)` as (owe failed) and the next is written. With `ask` and any written: `askAgent(ctx, shownName(record))`. Synchronous.
- `oweForgotten({ episode: { root, machine, home }, clock, log }) -> { owed: [id] }`: for each name of `readdirSync(workFolder(home))` (a throw gives `{ owed: [] }`) not starting with ".", whose `episodeFilesFolder(home, name)` is a folder: `readEpisode(join(episodesFolder(root), name))`; passed over unless it has a record, not `newer`, with `local.on === machine`, not `moved`, its `video/<file>` there, and no `original/<id>.json` pending. A part (`isPart`): its video is `links.find(record.partOf.id)` (`links = linksOf(root)`, made once); owed when that video's record has a hand-off (`finished.revision` an integer) and `asSession` does not hold the part, with `of` that video's. Any other: owed when it is not `isFromPlan`, has a hand-off and `asSession` does not hold it, with `of` its own. Each written as `oweOriginals` writes it (`lastCopy: clock()`), logged as (owed again); a write that throws is logged and passed over. It asks no agent: it is the agent.
- `export function checkOriginal(pending, deps)`: algorithm M for an owed pending, algorithm O for any other. Synchronous; gives nothing; writes or removes its own pending. `deps` is L5's: `episode` (`root`, `machine`, `home`, `checkout`), `log`, `notify`, `clock`, `alive`, `once`, `tools`, and `clone`, `sameDisk`, `isWhole`, `commit` and `uploaded` when a test gives them.

*`tool/filing.js`:* `CHECKS = { record: checkRecord, final: checkFinal, original: checkOriginal }`. `importEpisodes`: between `clearCrashes`' loop (1006) and `finishDue(true)` (1007), `oweForgotten({ episode, clock, log })` in a `try` whose catch logs `Recordings owed to Original files could not be looked for (<code>).`

*`tool/finish.js`:*
- `finishNow`, on both branches, at once after the record is written (1060 today; algorithm E's (f), after the `copied` write, on a local record): `oweOriginals(ctx, { folder: found.folder, record: nextRecord }, { ask: !keptOnAMac(record) })` in a `try` whose catch logs (owe failed) through `logTo(ctx.home)` and adds nothing to `failed`. On a local record E's (i) is the one kick for both; a record without `local` is an old plan's at most (its local parts), and asks for itself.
- `finishFinished`: `let wrote = null` before its `try` (1472), `wrote = writeEpisode(…)` at 1490; after the `finally` (1499), before the reveal (1500): `owed = oweOriginals(ctx, { folder, record: wrote }, { ask: false })` in a `try` (a throw logged, `owed` then `{ owed: [] }`), and algorithm F's one ask is made when its `end` gave `reopened` or `owed.owed` is not empty: `askAgent(ctx, shownName(wrote))`, once.

*`tool/edit.js`:*
- `notHere` (1198): passes over a `rel` that starts with `${ORIGINALS_FOLDER}/`.
- `stoppedWords` (1986): first, `if (runningCommand(ctx.home, found.name, ctx.alive) === ORIGINALS_COMMAND) return \`${ORIGINALS_RUNNING} ${again}\`;`, ahead of L5's branches.
- `status` (1766): `else if (command === ORIGINALS_COMMAND) lines.push(\`${shown}: its recording is being copied into Dropbox.\`);` before `else if (command)`, in L5's form of that line too.
- `episodeLines`: after the `Finished:` line (1012) and before `if (there) return lines;`, `lines.push(\`    ${ORIGINALS_SESSION(heldName(record))}\`)` when `listsOriginal(record)` and `asSession(links, { folder: e.folder, record })` (`record` is `e.inForce`).
- `guideItems`: before its loop, `problemOf = new Set` of `(p.of && p.of.id) || p.id` for each pending of `readPendingsOf(ctx.home, "original")` with a `problem`, this `ctx.machine` and this root; each item's `also` gains, after L5's checking line, `ORIGINALS_PROBLEM(facts.name)` when `problemOf.has(r.id)`, then `ORIGINALS_SESSION(heldName(r))` when `listsOriginal(r)` and `asSession(links, { folder: e.folder, record: r })`.
- `planFootage` (3612): `video: recordingAt(ctx, part), raw: recordingStat(ctx, part)`.
- `copyOf` (3703-3732): `video = recordingAt(ctx, { folder: found.folder, record: held.record })` and `rawStat = recordingStat(…)` given to each of its three `makeWorkCopy` calls. Before them, when `video` is not `fileOf(ctx, found, held.record.video.file)` (it is the copy in `Original files/`) and `!ctx.isWhole(video)`: `await (ctx.deps.readAll || readThrough)(video)` in a `try`; still not whole → `{ ok: false, made: [], failed: [canNotBeUsed("Dropbox is still bringing its recording over to this Mac")], next: "" }`.
- 4927 and 5090 (`kind`): `ctx.isWhole(recordingAt(ctx, { folder: found.folder, record }))` (5090's record is `wrote.record`); `whichCommand` (5626) and `showForStep` (6408, when `where` is `[record.video.file]`): `recordingAt(ctx, { folder, record })`.

*The other readers:* app.js `copyIsMade` (123) and `putVideoBack` (579): `const raw = recordingStat(ctx, { folder: found.folder, record });`; `changedSince` (415): `ctx ? recordingStat(ctx, { folder: found.folder, record }) : statOf(join(found.folder, record.video.file))`; `adoptProject` (454) and `makeProject` (501), and app-reel.js `adoptReel` (171) and `makeReel` (238): `recordingStat(ctx, { folder: found.folder, record }) ?? statSync(fileOf(ctx, found, record.video.file))` (still throwing when neither is there); app-reel.js `putReelVideoBack` (292): `recordingStat(…)`; plan-project.js `copyOfFootage` (89): `const raw = recordingStat(ctx, part); if (!raw) return null;`; free.js (325): `ctx.stat(recordingAt(ctx, { folder: found.folder, record }))`; reading.js `renderRough` (755): `recordingAt(ctx, { folder: found.folder, record })`; unedited.js `askBlocked` (1397): `rec = recordingAt({ home }, { folder, record })`, `whole(rec) ? {} : bring([rec === at(name) ? name : \`${ORIGINALS_FOLDER}/${name}\`])`; `askAgain` (1415): `probeArgs(recordingAt({ home: deps.home || studioHome() }, { folder, record }), probed)`.

#### The algorithms (exact)

In both: `home = deps.episode.home`, `machine = deps.episode.machine`, `clock = deps.clock`, `alive = deps.alive`, `once = deps.once || deps.log`, `whole = deps.isWhole || isWhole`, `opts` the rule's options of `deps` (`clone`, `sameDisk` when given, `log`), `rel = \`${ORIGINALS_FOLDER}/${p.file}\``, `from = join(episodeFilesFolder(home, p.name), p.file)`, `to = join(p.folder, ORIGINALS_FOLDER, p.file)`, `<code>` an error's `code` or `an error`. A line names the pending `<who>` until the record in force is read, and `<shown>` (`shownName` of it) from then on. "Put away" is `removePending(home, p)` (`work` is null); a throw → L5's (not put away). "Wait" is L5's: `writePending(home, { ...p, nextLook: clock() + WAIT_LOOK_MS, lookUntil: Number.isFinite(p.lookUntil) ? p.lookUntil : clock() + WAIT_LOOK_FOR_MS })`, then its line through `once`. "Put off" is `writePending(home, { ...p, nextLook: clock() + LOCK_AGAIN_MS })`, then (put off) through `once`. `base(p)` is `p` without `problem`, `nextLook` and `lookUntil`. "Ours" (the move's own clone at `to`) is: `to` there at `statSync(from).size`, and `!whole(to)` or `sameBytes(to, from)`. A "problem" is: (problem) logged; when `p` has no `problem`, `deps.notify(ORIGINALS_PROBLEM(<shown>))`; `writePending(home, { ...p, problem })` without `nextLook`; done. Any other throw is not caught here: L5's `finishDue` catches it (ruling 23).

**M. The move** (`p.owed === true`):
1. The folder, as L5's algorithm C, step 1: `readdirSync(episodesFolder(p.root))` throws → (folder unreadable); wait. Its name not listed, or `statSync(p.folder)` not a folder → put away; once put away, `FOLDER_GONE(<who>)` posted (`deps.notify`) and logged. `statSync` throws → wait.
2. The record: `read = readEpisode(p.folder)`; no record (NONE, UNREADABLE, CONFLICTED), `read.newer`, or `(recordNumbers(p.folder)[0] || 0) > read.record.rev` → (later); wait.
3. The gate: `links = linksOf(p.root)`; `asSession(links, { folder: p.folder, record: read.record })` → when `to` is there, `read.record` does not list `rel`, and it is ours: `rmSync(to, { force: true })` (a throw → (not taken away)); put away; (session before).
4. Moved already (a look stopped between its record and its pending): `listsOriginal(read.record)` and `local.original` an object → `writePending(home, { ...base(p), owed: false, rev: read.record.rev, entries: [{ from, to, bytes: read.record.files[rel] }], macCopy: { size: local.original.bytes, mtimeMs: local.original.mtimeMs }, lastCopy: clock() })`; done.
5. The Mac's copy: `fid = fileId(from)`; null → put away; (gone).
6. The lock: `mkdirSync(episodeWork(home, p.name), { recursive: true })`; `took = takeEpisodeLock(lockOf(home, p.name), ORIGINALS_COMMAND, alive)`; `{ running }` → put off. Steps 7-11 run in a `try` whose `finally` lets it go (`releaseEpisodeLock(lockOf(home, p.name), took.text)`).
7. Earlier writes (ruling 7): for each pending `q` of `readEveryPending(home)` with `q.id === p.id`, `q.machine === machine`, `resolve(q.root) === resolve(p.root)` and a `reading` mark: `settleReading(q, { home, log: deps.log })`; `{ later }` → (unsettled); wait. Then, for each such `final` pending with `q.rev > read.record.rev` that is held (`q.copied === true`, or `recordHolds(q, { isWhole: whole })` true): `putOwnRecordBack(q, { isWhole: whole, opts })` (its problems are its own check's). Then `read = readEpisode(p.folder)` again, as step 2 (→ wait); a held such `final` pending still with `q.rev > read.record.rev` → (later); wait.
8. `undone(read.record, home)` → when `Number.isInteger(p.rev)` and `lastWritten(home, p.id) === p.rev` (the record Dropbox lost is this move's own): `acceptRev(home, p.id, read.record.rev)` and go on; otherwise (undone); wait.
9. The copy: `to` there → ours → go on; not ours → problem `Original files in Dropbox holds another file of its name.` Not there → `mkdirSync(dirname(to), { recursive: true })`, `cloneIn(from, to, opts)`; a throw → problem `it could not be copied into Original files (<code>).`
10. The record: `next = { ...read.record, files: { ...read.record.files, [rel]: fid.size }, local: { ...read.record.local, original: { file: p.file, bytes: fid.size, mtimeMs: fid.mtimeMs } } }`; `work = join(runsFolder(home), \`originals-${process.pid}-${clock()}\`)`; `wrote = writeEpisode(p.folder, next, { expectRev: read.record.rev, writeText: textWriter({ work, ...opts }), writer: { commit: deps.commit || commitOf(deps.episode.checkout), on: machine }, home })`, and `rmSync(work, { recursive: true, force: true })` in its `finally`. A throw → `rmSync(to, { force: true })` when it is ours (a throw → (not taken away)); (record refused); wait.
11. `writePending(home, { ...base(p), owed: false, rev: wrote.rev, entries: [{ from, to, bytes: fid.size }], macCopy: fid, lastCopy: clock() })`; (moved). A throw from this write is L5's (threw); the next look meets step 4.

**O. The check** (`p.owed !== true`; `e = p.entries[0]`):
1. The folder, as M1. 2. The record, as M2.
3. The gate: `asSession(links, { folder: p.folder, record: read.record })` → put away; `ORIGINALS_SESSION(heldName(read.record))` logged. Nothing in Dropbox is changed, and the Mac's copy stays.
4. Still listed: `read.record.files[rel] !== e.bytes`, or no `local.original` → `writePending(home, { ...base(p), owed: true, lastCopy: clock() })` (its `rev` kept); (listed again); done.
5. The Mac's copy: `statSync(e.from, { throwIfNoEntry: false })` gives nothing → put away; (gone). `!sameFile(e.from, p.macCopy)` → put away; (changed): nothing is put back from it, and nothing deleted.
6. The copy, by L5's table J with `listed = read.record.files` and `whole`: J2, J3 or J7 → put back from `e.from` by L5's put-back (`mkdirSync`, then `replaceIn` or `cloneIn`, with `opts`), then `writePending(home, { ...base(p), lastCopy: clock() })`; (put back); done (its 15 minutes again). A put that throws → problem `its copy in Original files could not be put back in Dropbox (<code>).` J4 (a placeholder: not read) and J6 → whole. (J1 and J5 cannot be met here: steps 4 and 5.)
7. The flag: `up = (deps.uploaded || ((path) => uploadedOf(path, { swift: (deps.tools && deps.tools.swift) || "" })))(e.to)`; `up !== true` → `writePending(home, { ...base(p), nextLook: clock() + UPLOAD_LOOK_MS, lookUntil: Number.isFinite(p.lookUntil) ? p.lookUntil : clock() + WAIT_LOOK_FOR_MS })`; (not uploaded) through `once`; done.
8. The locks: `runningRun(home, n, alive, workCopyLockOf(home, n))` for `n` of `p.name` and, when other, `p.of.name` → put off. Else take `episode.lock` as M6 (→ put off); the rest in its `try`/`finally`.
9. Under it: `!sameFile(e.from, p.macCopy)` → put away; (changed). Else `rmSync(e.from, { force: true })`: a throw → (could not delete); put away. Else (taken off); put away.

#### Failing tests first

`originals.test.js`' world: `setup({ machine: "Mac Studio" })` (episodes.js: its `clone` stand-in keeps a file's times; `sameDisk: () => true`); `local(t, { id, name, stem, record, own })` writes a record kept on the Mac Studio (`local: { on: "Mac Studio" }`), answered, with a hand-off (`finished: { revision: 1, … }`), and its files (`<stem>.mov` with `"the recording"`, its transcript, words and cut plan) in `video/`, listed; `depsOf(t, over)` gives `{ episode: { root, machine, home, checkout: "/checkout" }, commit: "c709b57", log, notify, clock: t.now, alive, once, clone, sameDisk, isWhole, uploaded }`; `look(t, deps)` reads the pending again (`readPendingAt`) and calls `checkOriginal` with it. `ID`, `NAME`, `FOLDER` are the raw video's.

`dropbox.test.js`:
- U1 "uploadedOf reads the helper's one JSON document, synchronously": `exec` a stand-in giving `{"uploaded": true}\n`, then `{"uploaded": false}`, then `{"uploaded": null}` → `true`, `false`, `null`; the call was `(swift, [UPLOADED, path], { encoding: "utf8", timeout: UPLOADED_TIMEOUT_MS, stdio: ["ignore", "pipe", "ignore"] })`; no result is a promise.
- U2 "anything else is null, and no swift runs nothing": `exec` throwing `Object.assign(new Error("t"), { code: "ETIMEDOUT" })`, throwing an end of 1, giving `yes`, giving `{}` → `null` each; `swift: ""` → `null`, and `exec` is not called.
- U3 "the helper is beside the tool": `UPLOADED` ends with `/tool/uploaded.swift`, is there, and its text holds `ubiquitousItemIsUploadedKey` (read as text, never run).

`where.test.js`:
- W1 "recordingAt": a local record with its recording in `video/` → that path; gone and `Original files/<file>` listed → `Episodes/<folder>/Original files/<file>`; gone and not listed → the `video/` path; a record without `local` → its folder in Dropbox.
- W2 "recordingStat": there → its size and `mtimeMs`; gone, with `local.original` and the copy in `Original files/` at its bytes but with its time set to whole seconds (`utimesSync`) → `local.original`'s `bytes` and `mtimeMs`; the copy at another size → undefined; neither there → undefined.

`episode.test.js`:
- E1 "fileProblems calls a recording gone from video/ missing only when Original files has none": a local record with `local.original` and `Original files/<file>` listed, the recording gone from `localFolder` → no problem for it; the copy gone from `folder` too → one, `missing`, for `Original files/<file>`; without `local.original` → the recording `missing`, as today.

`workcopy.test.js`:
- K1 "a copy's key is the rawStat it is given": `makeWorkCopy` with `rawStat: { size: 5, mtimeMs: 1000 }`, a `stat` stand-in giving the video another size and time, and the copy and its facts there at `workCopyKey({ bytes: 5, mtimeMs: 1000 })` → ok, `made: []`, `key` that key; `makeFootageCopies` with an item's `raw` passes it on (the same, for a footage copy).

`originals.test.js`, the owe:
- M1 "a raw video kept on this Mac is owed at its hand-off, and the agent asked once": `oweOriginals(t.ctx, found)` → `{ owed: [ID] }`; `pending/original/<ID>.json` is `{ version: 1, kind: "original", id: ID, name: NAME, folder: FOLDER, root, machine: "Mac Studio", of: { id: ID, name: NAME }, file: "<stem>.mov", owed: true, entries: [], work: null, lastCopy: t.now() }`; `t.kicks` is `["kickstart"]`. With `{ ask: false }`, `t.kicks` is empty.
- M2 "owing again changes nothing": a second call → `{ owed: [] }`, the pending's text as it was, no kick; a record with `local.original`, and one listing `Original files/<file>` → `{ owed: [] }`.
- M3 "a video made from a plan owes each of its parts kept on this Mac, a client's included, and not its own video": a plan kept on the Mac Studio made its video, with a talking part (`client: false`), a b-roll part (`client: true`), a voiceover part, a part kept on Jason's MacBook and a part in Dropbox (no `local`) → owed are the first three parts' ids; each pending's `name` and `folder` its part's, `of` the plan's; no pending of the plan's id.
- M4 "an old plan's part dropped after the switch is owed, and an old record owes nothing": a layout-5 plan (no `local`) made its video, with one local part and one in Dropbox → that part alone; a raw record without `local` → `{ owed: [] }`, no `pending/original/`, `t.kicks` empty.
- M5 "a session's recording is never owed": a session kept on this Mac with a hand-off → `{ owed: [] }`; a raw video one of whose other versions is a session not cleared → `{ owed: [] }`; a plan's part whose `answer.match` is a session not cleared → not among the owed.

The move:
- M6 "the move clones the recording into Original files and lists it, and the Mac keeps its copy": M1's pending, `look` → `Original files/<file>` there, its bytes and `mtimeMs` the recording's (`t.clones` holds the pair); the record `N + 1` is `N`'s with `files["Original files/<file>"]` the recording's size and `local: { on: "Mac Studio", original: { file, bytes, mtimeMs } }`, every other field as `N`'s; the pending has `owed: false`, `rev: N + 1`, `entries: [{ from, to, bytes }]`, `macCopy` the `fileId` of `video/<file>`, `lastCopy: t.now()`, and no `problem`, `nextLook` or `lookUntil`; `video/<file>` is still there; the log has (moved) once; no `originals-*` folder in `.runs/`; no `episode.lock`.
- M7 "a run at work puts the move off": `takeEpisodeLock(lockOf(home, NAME), "read", () => true)` and `alive` true for this process → nothing cloned, the record as it was, the pending owed with `nextLook: t.now() + LOCK_AGAIN_MS`; two looks, (put off) once.
- M8 "a record write refused takes the clone away, and waits": the `clone` stand-in throwing for `record/<N + 1>.json` → no `Original files/<file>`; the record as it was; the pending owed, with `nextLook` and `lookUntil`; (record refused) once.
- M9 "a move stopped after its clone is made again with no second clone": `Original files/<file>` there with the recording's bytes, not listed → the record lists it, `t.clones` has no clone of the recording, the pending is moved.
- M10 "another file at its name in Original files is a problem, told once": a file of the same size and other bytes there → no record written; `notices` is `[ORIGINALS_PROBLEM("Pricing talk")]` after two looks; the pending owed with `problem: "Original files in Dropbox holds another file of its name."`; `video/<file>` there.
- M11 "a held finish's lost record is put back first, and its number is never taken": a `final` pending (`copied`, `rev: N + 1`, its `record` entry's work copy there) and `record/<N + 1>.json` gone → `record/<N + 1>.json` is the work copy's bytes and the move's record is `N + 2`. The same with that work copy gone too → no record written, nothing cloned, the pending waits; (later) once.
- M12 "a change Dropbox undid waits for her command; the move's own does not": `acceptRev(home, ID, N + 1)` with the record at `N` → nothing cloned, (undone), wait. The same with the pending's `rev: N + 1` → the move writes `N + 1`.
- M13 "a stopped writer's mark is settled before the move writes": a `final` pending of `ID` whose entry `final/<x>.srt` is listed at its bytes and marked `{ pid: <a dead one>, at, to: [<its to>], nonce, rev: N + 1 }` → after the look the mark is gone and the entry is still in it; the move's record is `N + 1`.
- M14 "a recording gone from the Mac is put away": `video/<file>` removed → no pending, no `Original files/`, the record as it was; (gone).
- M15 "a gone folder is put away with a short notice": `Episodes/<FOLDER>` removed → no pending; `notices` is `[FOLDER_GONE("an episode (<short id>)")]`; `video/<file>` there. Episodes renamed away (put back in `finally`) → the pending waits.

Sessions:
- N1 "answered a session between the owe and the move: nothing moves": record `N + 1` a session with its mark not cleared → no pending, no `Original files/`, `video/<file>` there; the log's (session before) names the label with its short id and holds no file name.
- N2 "a session after a move stopped before its record: its clone is taken away": `Original files/<file>` there (ours), not listed, and the record a session → no `Original files/<file>`; no pending.
- N3 "a session after the move: nothing in Dropbox changes, and the Mac keeps its copy": after M6, record `N + 2` a session; at `lastCopy + LATE_CHECK_MS`, `look` → no pending; `Original files/<file>` and the record's entry as they were; `video/<file>` there; the log has `ORIGINALS_SESSION(<label>)`; `uploaded` never asked.
- N4 "so for a video held beside a session": after M6, another version answered as a session not cleared → as N3.

The check:
- O1 "the check deletes the Mac's copy only when whole, listed, uploaded and unchanged": after M6, at `lastCopy + LATE_CHECK_MS`, `uploaded: () => true` → `video/<file>` gone, its transcript, words and cut plan there; no pending; `Original files/` and the record as they were; `uploaded` called once, with `to`; (taken off).
- O2 "not uploaded yet keeps the Mac's copy": `uploaded` false, then null → `video/<file>` there; the pending has `nextLook: t.now() + UPLOAD_LOOK_MS` and `lookUntil` the first look's `t.now() + WAIT_LOOK_FOR_MS`, kept by the second look; (not uploaded) once in a run (`once`); a third look giving `true` deletes it.
- O3 "a copy Dropbox lost, cut short, or put back older at its size is put back from the Mac's copy, and its 15 minutes start again": each → `Original files/<file>` holds the recording's bytes; the pending's `lastCopy` is `t.now()`; `uploaded` not asked; `video/<file>` there.
- O4 "a copy that cannot be put back is a problem, told once": O3's lost copy and the `clone` stand-in throwing for `to` → `problem` `its copy in Original files could not be put back in Dropbox (an error).`; one notice after two looks; `video/<file>` there. A third look with the stand-in working → put back, and the pending has no `problem`.
- O5 "the Mac's copy changed since the move is kept, and nothing is put back from it": `video/<file>` written again at its size with other bytes → no pending; `Original files/<file>` holds the first bytes; `video/<file>` there; (changed).
- O6 "the Mac's copy gone is no problem": no pending; (gone); no notice.
- O7 "a run at work puts the delete off": a live `episode.lock`; then a live `workcopy.lock` of the episode; then, for a part, of its plan → each time nothing deleted, `nextLook: t.now() + LOCK_AGAIN_MS`; with the lock gone, deleted.
- O8 "a record that lost the move lists it again, with no second clone": after M6, record `N + 2` written from `N` (no entry, no `local.original`) → the pending owed again; the next look writes `N + 3` listing it and clones nothing; it is checked 15 minutes later.
- O9 "a placeholder copy is taken as whole and not read": `isWhole` false for `to`, whose bytes differ at the same size → `uploaded` asked; `true` → deleted.
- O10 "a part's recording is taken off its own video/, and the plan's video stays".
- O11 "Episodes away a moment waits, and is looked at again": the pending has `nextLook: t.now() + WAIT_LOOK_MS` and `lookUntil`; nothing deleted.

Through L5's worlds (the `original` check turned on as `checks: { original: checkOriginal }`, with `uploaded` given):
- X1 "a local finish owes its recording, and its one kick serves both": after L5's L1, `pending/original/<ID>.json` is owed with `of.id` ID; `t.kicks` is `["kickstart"]`.
- X2 "the agent's run moves it at once, and takes it off once Dropbox has it uploaded": `agentRun` with `uploaded: () => true` → the move at the run's start; the `final` check and this check 15 minutes on; at the end `video/<file>` gone, `Original files/<file>` there and listed, no pending; the export out of Downloads (L5's check).
- X3 "a paused upload is looked at for an hour, and the loop then ends": `uploaded: () => false` → called 7 times, at the move's `lastCopy + LATE_CHECK_MS` and every 10 minutes to an hour after it; every `wait` above 0; the run returns with the pending there. A second run three hours on: called once, at its start.
- X4 "a hand-off writer stopped before it owed is owed at the next run's start": after L1, `pending/original/<ID>.json` removed → `agentRun` logs (owed again) and moves it.
- X5 "the first hand-off of a finished video from its spellings owes its recording": reels-world's `finishedWorld` made local (as L5's L7), `spell … offers all` → the pending owed; `t.kicks` is `["kickstart"]`.

`local-episode.test.js`, a describe "L6: after the recording's move", on `KEPT.raw` handed off (`handedOff`) and moved off (`oweOriginals`, then `checkOriginal` at the move and 15 minutes on with `uploaded: () => true`):
- A1 "`episodes` calls nothing of it missing, and `finish` is not refused over its recording": no `missing` line; `finish 3fa9c1` says no `recording is not whole on this Mac` line.
- A2 "`app` opens its project as it was": its working copy made before the move → no workcopy started, no line that the recording changed; the same once the copy in `Original files/` has its time set to whole seconds.
- A3 "`open` and `app` bring nothing of Original files": `isWhole` false for `Original files/<file>` → no `bring` in `t.started`.
- A4 "a later Reel is made from the copy in Original files, read through first, at the key of the recording as it was": `app 3fa9c1 reel 1`, then the run `workcopy --now <NAME> --reel 1` with `isWhole` false for the copy until `readAll` (a stand-in) is called with its path → its Reel copy at `reelCopyKey({ bytes: original.bytes, mtimeMs: original.mtimeMs }, <stretch>)`; with `readAll` leaving it not whole, the run's result says `It can't be used: Dropbox is still bringing its recording over to this Mac.`
- A5 "`which` opens the copy in Original files"; A6 "`free` takes its working copy away"; A7 "`kind … raw --again` reads it there": not refused as not here, and `renderRough`'s ffprobe stand-in is given that path.
- A8 "`askBlocked` stands on it": whole → `{}`; a placeholder → `bring: ["Original files/<file>"]`.
- A9 "a plan's footage copy is found by the key of its part's recording as it was" (`copyOfFootage` after the part is moved off).
- A10 "`guide … show` of a finished video's posting step shows the copy in Original files".
- P1 "`status` says the move": a live `episode.lock` of command `originals` (this process, `alive` true) → `Pricing talk: its recording is being copied into Dropbox.`, and no line `originals is running.`
- P2 "her command during the move is told so": the same lock, `finish 3fa9c1` → a line `Its recording is being copied into Dropbox. Say "hand it off" again then.`; nothing written.
- P3 "the guide and `episodes` say the Original files lines": M10's problem → the guide's lines under the video include `ORIGINALS_PROBLEM("Pricing talk")`; N3's state (on `KEPT.raw` made a session after its move) → the guide's lines under it and its `episodes` line include `ORIGINALS_SESSION(<its label>)`; a video moved off well has neither.

#### Steps

1. L5's worlds lay `{ original: null }` under any `checks` a test gives (every call of `importEpisodes` in `local-world.js` and `checks-world.js`), so a local finish's `original` pending holds no run of L5's tests open; `originals.test.js`' world; every test above. `cd tool && node --test`: the new tests fail, each by a missing export or a wrong value; every test of L5 still passes.
2. `uploaded.swift`; dropbox.js `UPLOADED`, `UPLOADED_TIMEOUT_MS`, `uploadedOf`. U1-U3 pass.
3. where.js `ORIGINALS_FOLDER`, `recordingAt`, `recordingStat`; episode.js `fileProblems`; workcopy.js `rawStat` and `raw`. W1, W2, E1 and K1 pass.
4. originals.js: the words, `ORIGINALS_COMMAND`, `UPLOAD_LOOK_MS`, `listsOriginal`, `moved`, `asSession`, `originalsOf`, `oweOriginals`, `oweForgotten`. M1-M5 pass.
5. originals.js `checkOriginal` (algorithms M and O); filing.js `CHECKS.original` and `oweForgotten` at the run's start. M6-M15, N1-N4 and O1-O11 pass.
6. finish.js: the owe in `finishNow` and in `finishFinished`, with its one ask. X1-X5 pass; L5's L1, L2, L11, L12, L19, L25 and L26 still pass as they are (a test of L5's that fails only by an `original` pending it now meets, or one kick more after a first local hand-off from `finishFinished`, is changed to expect it, and named in the commit).
7. The readers: app.js, app-reel.js, free.js, plan-project.js, reading.js, unedited.js, and edit.js `notHere`, `planFootage`, `copyOf`, `kind`'s two tests, `whichCommand`, `showForStep`. A1-A10 pass; L2c's tests in `local-episode.test.js` still pass.
8. edit.js `stoppedWords`, `status`, `episodeLines`, `guideItems`. P1-P3 pass.

#### Words

Sallie's:
- The notice, posted once, and the guide's line under the video: `ORIGINALS_PROBLEM(name)` = `The recording of <name> stays on this Mac: Dropbox didn't keep its copy whole. Ask Jason.`
- The guide's line under the video, and a line under it in `episodes`: `ORIGINALS_SESSION(label)` = `The recording of <label> was copied into Dropbox, in Original files, before it became a session. Dropbox keeps it there and in its history. Jason removes it by hand if he wants.` (also logged, at the check that meets it).
- A command stopped by the move's lock: `Its recording is being copied into Dropbox. <again>`; `status`: `<name>: its recording is being copied into Dropbox.`
- The workcopy run, when Dropbox does not bring the copy down: `It can't be used: Dropbox is still bringing its recording over to this Mac.`
- Nothing is said when a move goes well, or while it waits for the upload: the log says it.

Jason's, in the log (no path, no file's name; `<who>` and `<shown>` as the algorithms give them):
- (moved) `<shown>: its recording is copied into Original files in Dropbox. It leaves this Mac once Dropbox has it uploaded.`
- (not uploaded) `<shown>: Dropbox has not uploaded its copy in Original files yet, so the recording stays on this Mac. It is looked at again later.`
- (taken off) `<shown>: Dropbox has its copy in Original files uploaded, so the recording is taken off this Mac.`
- (put back) `<shown>: its copy in Original files was not whole in Dropbox (<what>), so it is put back from the recording on this Mac.` (`<what>` as L5's table J says it)
- (listed again) `<shown>: the record no longer lists its copy in Original files, so it is listed again.`
- (put off) `<shown>: its recording waits for the run at work on it to end.`
- (gone) `<shown>: its recording is no longer on this Mac, so nothing is copied or taken off.`
- (changed) `<shown>: its recording on this Mac changed after it was copied into Original files, so it is kept on this Mac.`
- (session before) `<heldName, with its short id>: it is held as a session now, so its recording stays on this Mac.`
- (undone) `<shown>: its recording waits: Dropbox undid this Mac's last change to it, which the next command on it says first. It is looked at again later.`
- (record refused) `<shown>: its copy in Original files could not be listed in its record (<code>), so it is taken away again. It is looked at again later.`
- (not taken away) `<shown>: a copy of its recording in Original files could not be taken away (<code>). Ask Jason.`
- (could not delete) `<shown>: its recording could not be taken off this Mac (<code>), so it stays there.`
- (problem) `<shown>: its recording stays on this Mac: <problem>`, `<problem>` one of `Original files in Dropbox holds another file of its name.`, `it could not be copied into Original files (<code>).`, `its copy in Original files could not be put back in Dropbox (<code>).`
- (owe failed) `<shown>: its recording could not be noted to go into Original files (<code>). The launch agent notes it at its next run.`
- (owed again) `<shown>: its recording is noted to go into Original files.`
- L5's (folder unreadable), (later), (unsettled), (not put away), (threw) and `FOLDER_GONE(<who>)`, as L5 says them.

#### Contract touched

The guide's words, in step with brimm-operations: under a video kept on a Mac, `ORIGINALS_PROBLEM` while a move or its check has a problem, and `ORIGINALS_SESSION` for a video that became a session after its move (`guide.js`' output, through the `also` lines edit.js gives it). None of the BRIMM Ops page guide's listed lines (167, 248, 253, 282, 286, 295) changes because of L6 alone; L8 already left "Make online-only" out for a video kept on a Mac. The hand-off file, its fixtures, its name and place in `final/`, `madeBy`, `tool/shared/`, `lib.js` and `run.js` do not change.

#### Known limits

1. Deleting the Mac's copy frees no room on its disk by itself: the copy in `Original files/` is an APFS clone of it (`/bin/cp -c`), sharing its blocks, until Dropbox makes it online-only. P3-7 step 4 has Jason do that by hand; whether Studio should ask Dropbox for it is his call.
2. On a Mac with no swift (`findPrograms`, without Xcode's tools) the flag is never read: the recording stays, and the log says (not uploaded) once a run.
3. A record-only write that does not look at the episode's lock (a claim, a name, a title) and crosses the move's record write in the same moment is refused once, in today's words; said again, it goes.
4. A work copy run that starts between the check's look at `workcopy.lock` and its delete meets the recording gone and fails once; said again, it reads `Original files/`.
5. A move waiting on `undone` waits for her next command on the episode, which says `Dropbox undid…` first.
6. A hand-off writer stopped between its record and its owe is owed at the agent's next run (a drop, a login, a kick): late, never lost.

**Size:** L (about 450 lines of code: originals.js about 300, uploaded.swift about 25, the rest across where.js, dropbox.js, episode.js, workcopy.js, filing.js, finish.js, edit.js and the readers; and about 1300 lines of tests in 55 tests).

**The coordinator's rulings on this section (7 Nov 2026), binding over it:**
- Every part of a plan moves to Original files (b-roll, screen recordings and an unused talking part included, by `partsOf`), each in its own part's episode folder: Jason, "Yes, every recording" and "Each part's folder".
- Whether Studio also asks Dropbox to make the copy in Original files online-only (so the Mac's disk is freed: the copy is an APFS clone of the Mac's until Dropbox evicts it) is Jason's question, asked separately. L6 builds the move and the delete as written, and asks Dropbox for nothing.
- unedited.js `askAgain` reads the recording through `recordingAt`, as the section's other readers do.

### L6b. The copy in Original files made online-only

**Goal:** Once L6 has deleted the Mac's copy of a recording (its copy in Original files whole and Dropbox saying it is uploaded), Studio asks Dropbox to make that copy online-only, so the Mac's disk space really comes back: on the same disk the copy in Original files is an APFS clone of the Mac's, sharing its blocks until Dropbox evicts it. Jason, 7 Nov, to "Should Studio ask Dropbox to make each moved recording online-only, once its upload is confirmed?": "yes".

**Evidence:** L6's delete in `checkOriginal` (originals.js, the step after the uploaded flag reads true and the gate is asked again under the lock, before `rmSync` of the Mac's copy); `uploadedOf` and `uploaded.swift` (dropbox.js), whose shape this follows.

**Files:** create `tool/online-only.swift`; `tool/dropbox.js` (`ONLINE_ONLY`, `ONLINE_ONLY_TIMEOUT_MS`, `makeOnlineOnly`); `tool/originals.js` (one call after the delete); tests `dropbox.test.js`, `originals.test.js`. Not README: L9 and the final review carry its line.

**Interfaces:**
- `online-only.swift <file>`: calls `FileManager.default.evictUbiquitousItem(at:)`; prints one JSON document: `{"onlineOnly": true}`, or `{"onlineOnly": false, "error": "<NSError domain and code>"}`; ends 0; given no file, or a path where no file is, it prints nothing, says why on standard error, and ends 1. It touches nothing but whether this Mac keeps the file's data: Dropbox keeps the file.
- `ONLINE_ONLY` (its path, as `UPLOADED` is found), `ONLINE_ONLY_TIMEOUT_MS = 20 * 1000`.
- `makeOnlineOnly(path, { swift = "", exec = execFileSync, timeoutMs = ONLINE_ONLY_TIMEOUT_MS } = {}) -> true | false | null`, synchronous, as `uploadedOf`: null at once with no `swift`; `true` or `false` from the document; null for anything else and any throw.
- In `checkOriginal`, right after the Mac's copy is deleted (and only then): `(deps.onlineOnly || ((path) => makeOnlineOnly(path, { swift: (deps.tools && deps.tools.swift) || "" })))(<the copy in Original files>)`, once. `true` logs `<who>: its recording in Original files is made online-only.`; anything else logs `<who>: its recording in Original files could not be made online-only (<error or "no answer">); it stays on this Mac's disk until Dropbox frees it.` Never a problem, a notice, a pending or a retry: the check is complete either way.

**Failing tests first:**
- "makeOnlineOnly reads the helper's one JSON document, synchronously": `exec` stand-ins giving `{"onlineOnly": true}`, `{"onlineOnly": false, "error": "NSCocoaErrorDomain 256"}`, garbage, and a throw → `true`, `false`, `null`, `null`; the call is `(swift, [ONLINE_ONLY, path], { encoding: "utf8", timeout: ONLINE_ONLY_TIMEOUT_MS, stdio: ["ignore", "pipe", "ignore"] })`; no `swift` → null with no call.
- "the check makes the copy in Original files online-only once the Mac's copy is deleted": with `deps.uploaded` true and `deps.onlineOnly` a recording stand-in → called once, with the path in Original files, after the Mac's copy is gone; the log has the made-online-only line.
- "it is never asked while the Mac's copy stays": `uploaded` false or null, the gate refusing, the copy not whole → `onlineOnly` never called.
- "a copy that cannot be made online-only is logged, and the check is still done": `onlineOnly` → false, then a throw → the log line with the error, the pending gone, no notice, no problem.
- No test runs swift: every check's test gives `onlineOnly`, as it gives `uploaded`.

**Steps:** the tests, seen failing; `online-only.swift` (parse it with `swiftc -parse`; never run it); dropbox.js; the call in originals.js; the affected test files; commit.

**Words:** Jason's log lines above; nothing new for Sallie.

**By hand:** before the PR, a probe like P3-0 on a test file in Jason's Dropbox outside BRIMM Video: written, its flag `true`, then `swift online-only.swift <file>` prints `{"onlineOnly": true}`, and `ls -lO <file>` shows `dataless` and `du -k <file>` prints `0`; the file still opens (Dropbox brings it down). Check 97's step for the moved recording uses the same `ls -lO` and `du -k`.

**Contract touched:** none. **Size:** S.

### L7. Open it once: drop it, and it runs until it needs her

**Goal:** After her answer nothing asks her to wait or to say a thing twice. Her answer starts the reading and the copy with no 20-second claim; "open it in HyperFrames" said while the copy is being made opens HyperFrames by itself when the copy is done.

**Evidence:** `kind` and `teachings` claim and wait `CLAIM_WAIT_MS` (20 s, edit.js 1116) in `claimAndWait` (5290-5320, called at 4740 in `answerKind` and 5588 in `askTeachings`), to keep two Macs from answering one video; with a local drop another Mac never sees it (D3). `app` says `The video for HyperFrames is being made. … Say "open <name> in HyperFrames" again when it is done; …` (app.js `beingMade`, 82), so she says it twice. Phase 1's M7: the join's notice says "It opens in HyperFrames about 17:26" (`opensAt`, join.js 571-577), when its video is ready; she still has to ask. `runDetached` takes the episode's lock for the whole run (detached.js 172).

**Files:** `tool/edit.js` (`answerKind`/`claimAndWait` for a local record; `app`'s copy-at-work branch; a detached `app --when-ready`), `tool/app.js` (`beingMade` words; the run that opens when ready), `tool/workcopy.js` (the run's end is what it waits for: `workCopyLockOf`, its result), `tool/join.js` (the notice), `tool/guide.js` (`copyStep`, 346-352, which says `Say "open it in HyperFrames" when it is done.`), the skill twins (the "Open it in HyperFrames" row: when Studio says it opens by itself, tell her and stop); tests `edit-kind.test.js`, `edit-app.test.js`, `app.test.js`, `join.test.js`, `guide.test.js`, `studio.test.js`.

**Interfaces.** Produces `app --when-ready "<episode folder>"` (a detached run, not in her `HELP`; like `workcopy --now`): it waits, without the episode's lock, while the copy's lock is held by a live run (at most the copy's own limit); then it takes the lock and does what `app` does (the project made and opened); if the copy failed, it opens nothing and posts the notice below. An episode from before the switch keeps its claim and today's words.

**Failing tests first:**
- `edit-kind.test.js`: a local raw answer writes its record once, with no `wait(CLAIM_WAIT_MS)`, and starts the reading and the copy; an old episode's answer still claims and waits.
- `edit-app.test.js`: with the copy at work, `app` says `Its video for HyperFrames is being made. HyperFrames opens it by itself about 17:26.` (`… by itself when it is ready.` with no measured speed), starts one `app --when-ready`, and claims nothing; a second `app` meanwhile says the same and starts nothing.
- The `--when-ready` run: when the copy's result is ok, the project is made and the `openApp` stand-in called once; when it failed, nothing opens and the notice is posted; while it waits, `free`, L6's move and her other commands are not refused for it.
- `join.test.js`: the notice is `Ready: "<title>" is made from its footage. Say "open <title> in HyperFrames": it opens by itself once its video is ready.`
- `guide.test.js`: for a local episode whose copy is being made, the step ends `Say "open it in HyperFrames": it opens by itself when it is ready.`; an old episode's ends as today.

**Steps:** tests; fail; change; the skill row; whole suite; commit.

**Words Sallie sees:**

- The notice when it cannot: `The video for HyperFrames of <name> couldn't be made. Say "open <name> in HyperFrames" to try again; if it says this again, ask Jason.`
- The join's notice above (Phase 1's M7, now true).
